一定有人——她不知道是谁——给堂弟杰斐逊·派克先生通了消息，因为当天傍晚他就到了。他在托基住了三四天，往来阿什伯顿见警察、打听消息。其中一趟，他登门看望了她，声言自己是堂兄唯一在世的亲人，要接管死者的随身遗物。他把死者的许多东西打点带走，说余下的他不要了，请她自行处置。

===Original===

It had not occurred to her to question Mr. Jefferson Pyke’s right to take her lodger’s property. She had seen him once before, in Mr. Stanley’s lifetime. Some two months before the tragedy Mr. Stanley had told her that his cousin was home on a visit from the Argentine﻿—she believed it was the Argentine and not Australia﻿—and that he was coming down to see him. He asked her could she put him up. Mr. Jefferson had arrived a day or two later and she had given him her spare bedroom. He stayed for four days and the cousins had explored the moor together. Mrs. Berlyn, she had heard, had driven them about in her car. The landlady had found Mr. Jefferson very pleasant; indeed, when the two men were together they had nearly made her die laughing with their jokes and nonsense. Mr. Jefferson had told her that he owned a ranch in the Argentine and that he was thinking of starting flower gardens from which to supply the cities. He was then on his way back from the Scillys, where he had gone to investigate the industry. A week after Mr. Jefferson left, Mr. Stanley took his holidays, and he had told her he was going with his cousin to the south of France to a place called Grasse, where there were more gardens. He was only back some three weeks when he met his death.

===Chinese===

她压根儿不曾想到，去质疑杰斐逊·派克先生取走她房客财物的权利。斯坦利先生在世时，她见过他一回。惨案前约莫两个月，斯坦利先生告诉她，他堂弟从阿根廷回国省亲——她相信是阿根廷，不是澳大利亚——要下来看他。他问她能不能留他住下。一两天后杰斐逊先生到了，她把自己那间备用卧室给了他。他住了四天，堂兄弟俩结伴游逛了荒原。听说伯林太太开车带他们各处转过。房东太太觉得杰斐逊先生非常讨人喜欢；说真的，那两人凑在一处打趣胡闹，差点没把她笑死。杰斐逊先生告诉过她，他在阿根廷拥有一座大牧场，还打算开办花圃，向各城市供货。他那时刚从锡利群岛（the Scillys）回来，是专程去考察那门营生的。杰斐逊先生走后一个星期，斯坦利先生休了假，他告诉她，要跟堂弟去法国南部一个叫格拉斯（Grasse）的地方，那边的花圃更多。他回来不过三个星期左右，就遭了毒手。

===Original===

All this was given to French with a wealth of detail which, had it been material to his investigation, he would have welcomed, but by which, as it was, he was frankly bored. However, he could do nothing to stop the stream and he simulated interest as best he could.

===Chinese===

这一切，连带着层出不穷的细节，一齐灌给了弗伦奇。这些细节倘若与他的调查相干，他本会欢迎；无奈实际上，他老实说是听腻了。可他没法叫这股水流止住，只得竭力装出一副听得津津有味的样子。

===Original===

“By the way, Mrs. Billing,” he said, pausing on his way out, “if I take these rooms could you look after the mending of my clothes? Who did it for Mr. Pyke?”

===Chinese===

「对了，比林太太（Mrs. Billing），」他出门时停住脚说，「要是我租下这些房间，我的衣裳缝补，你能照应吗？这些活儿，先前是派克先生的，谁替他做的？」

===Original===

Mrs. Billing had, and she would be delighted to do the same for Mr. French.

===Chinese===

比林太太正是做这活儿的人，而且巴不得也为弗伦奇先生效劳。

===Original===

“Well, I have some that want it at the present time. Suppose I bring them over now. Could you look at them?” Five minutes later he returned with his suitcase and spread out the clothes as he had done for Lizzie Johnston an hour or two before. Like the maid, Mrs. Billing glanced over them and remarked that there didn’t seem to be much wrong.

===Chinese===

「正好，我眼下就有几件要补。这样吧，我这就去拿来，你能给看看吗？」五分钟后他拎着手提箱回来了，把衣裳摊开，同一两个钟头前摊给莉齐·约翰斯顿看的一模一样。跟那女仆一样，比林太太把衣裳扫了一遍，说看着没什么大毛病。

===Original===

French picked up the grey sock.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇拿起那只灰袜子。

===Original===

“But you see they have not been very neatly darned. This grey one has been done with a different coloured wool. I thought perhaps you could put that right.”

===Chinese===

「可你瞧，这几双先前补得不算齐整。这只灰色的是用了别种颜色的毛线补的。我想，兴许你能把它拾掇好。」

===Original===

Mrs. Billing took the grey sock and stared at it for some time, while a puzzled expression grew on her face. French, suddenly keenly excited, watched her almost breathlessly. But after turning it over she put it down, though the slightly mystified look remained.

===Chinese===

比林太太接过灰袜子，盯着看了半晌，脸上渐渐浮起困惑的神色。弗伦奇蓦地心头一紧，几乎屏住呼吸地盯着她。可她把袜子翻检了一遍，又放下了，那副略带迷惑的表情却还挂在脸上。

===Original===

“Here are some underclothes,” French went on. “Do these want any mending?”

===Chinese===

「这儿有几件内衣。」弗伦奇接着说，「这些要补吗？」

===Original===

Slowly the landlady turned over the bundle. As she did so incredulity and amazement showed on her birdlike features. Then swiftly she turned to the neck of the vest and the shirt cuffs and scrutinised the buttons and links.

===Chinese===

房东太太慢慢翻着那包衣裳。翻着翻着，她那小鸟似的面孔上露出难以置信的惊诧。接着她迅速翻到汗衫的领口和衬衫的袖口，细细察看纽扣和袖扣。

===Original===

“My Gawd!” she whispered, hoarsely, and French saw that her face had paled and her hands were trembling.

===Chinese===

「我的老天爷！」她哑着嗓子低呼。弗伦奇看见她脸色发白，双手发颤。

===Original===

“You recognise them?”

===Chinese===

「你认得出来？」

===Original===

She nodded, her flood of speech for once paralysed.

===Chinese===

她点了点头，那滔滔的话头这一回竟瘫住了。

===Original===

“Where did you get them?” she asked, still in a whisper.

===Chinese===

「这些是从哪儿来的？」她仍压着嗓子问。

===Original===

French was quite as excited as she, but he controlled himself and spoke easily.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇激动得不亚于她，但他按捺住自己，语气从容地说：

===Original===

“Tell me first whose they are and how you are so sure of them.”

===Chinese===

「先告诉我这是谁的衣裳，你又怎么认得这么准。」

===Original===

“They’re Mr. Pyke’s, what he was wearing the night he was lost. I couldn’t but be sure of them. See here. There’s the wool first. I darned that and I remember I hadn’t the right colour. Then these buttons.” She picked up the vest. “I put that one on. See, it’s not the same as the rest; it was the only one I could get. And then if that wasn’t enough, these are the cufflinks. I’ve seen them hundreds of times and I’d know them anywhere. Where did you get them?”

===Chinese===

「是派克先生的，是他出事那晚身上穿的。我不可能认不准。您瞧这儿。先说这毛线，这块是我织补的，我记得当时手头没有颜色对的线。再说这些纽扣。」她拿起汗衫。「这一颗是我钉的。您瞧，跟其余的不一样；当时我只弄得着这一颗。这还不算，还有这副袖扣。我见过它们几百回，走到天边也认得。您是从哪儿弄来的？」

===Original===

French dropped his suave, kindly manner and suddenly became official and, for him, unusually harsh.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇卸下那副温文和气的做派，忽然摆出公事公办的腔调，而且就他而论，严厉得异乎寻常。

===Original===

“Now, Mrs. Billing,” he said, sharply, “I’d better tell you exactly who I am and warn you that you’ve got to keep it to yourself. I am Inspector French of New Scotland Yard; you understand, a police officer. I have discovered that Mr. Pyke was murdered and I am on the track of the murderer.”

===Chinese===

「听着，比林太太，」他声色俱厉地说，「我不妨把我的确切身份向您挑明，也警告您，这事必须烂在自己肚里。我是新苏格兰场的弗伦奇探长；您明白，是个警官。我已经查明，派克先生是被人谋害的，眼下正追查着凶手。」

===Original===

The landlady gave a little scream. She was evidently profoundly moved, not only by surprise and excitement, but by horror at her late lodger’s fate. She began to speak, but French cut her short.

===Chinese===

房东太太发出一声短促的尖叫。她显然被深深撼动了，不但是又惊又激动，还有对已故房客下场的恐惧。她刚要说话，弗伦奇把她截住了。

===Original===

“I want you to understand,” he said, threateningly, “that you must keep silence on this matter. If any hint of it gets about, it will be a very serious thing for you. I take it you don’t want to be mixed up in a murder trial. Very well, then; keep your mouth shut.”

===Chinese===

「我要你明白，」他带着威吓说，「这件事你必须守口如瓶。但凡走漏一点风声，对你就是桩非常严重的事。我谅你也不愿搅进一桩凶杀案的审理里去。那就好；闭上你的嘴。」

===Original===

Mrs. Billing was terrified and eagerly promised discretion. French questioned her further, but without result. She did not believe her late lodger was on bad terms with anyone, nor did she know if he had a birthmark on his upper arm.

===Chinese===

比林太太吓坏了，急切地应承守口如瓶。弗伦奇又盘问了几句，却无结果。她不信她那位已故房客同谁结过怨，也不晓得他上臂上有没有胎记。

===Original===

French’s delight at his discovery was unbounded. The identification of the dead man represented the greatest step towards the completion of his case that he had yet made. He chuckled to himself in pure joy.

===Chinese===

这一发现让弗伦奇喜不自胜。查明死者的身份，是他着手结案以来迈出的最大一步。他独自乐得咯咯直笑。

===Original===

But his brain reeled when he thought of his four test points. If this news were true, he had made some pretty bad mistakes! Each one of his four conclusions must be false. As he remembered the facts on which they were based, he had to admit himself completely baffled.

===Chinese===

可一想到那四项检验，他的脑袋就发懵。这消息若是真的，他可犯下了几个相当糟糕的大错！四项结论，项项必定皆错。他回想起这些结论所依据的事实，只得承认自己全然被难住了。

===Original===

Presently his mood changed and a wave of pessimism swept over him. The identification of the underclothes was not, after all, the identification of the body. Such an astute criminal as he was dealing with might have changed the dead man’s clothes. But when he reminded himself that the man who called for the crate resembled Berlyn, the thing became more convincing. However, it had not been proved, and he wanted certainty.

===Chinese===

不多时，他心境一变，一阵悲观袭来。认出内衣，毕竟不等于认出尸体。他过手的凶手如此狡黠，说不定调换过死者的衣裳。可再一想到前去领取板条箱的那个人貌似伯林，这事便更可信了几分。然而这终归未经证实，而他要的是确凿。

===Original===

Fortunately there was the birthmark. French had examined it carefully and was satisfied that it was genuine. Who, he wondered, could identify it?

===Chinese===

幸而还有那块胎记。弗伦奇仔细查验过，确信它不是伪造。谁认得出它呢？他思忖着。

===Original===

The most likely person, he thought, was Jefferson Pyke. It would be worth a journey to London to have the point settled. That night, therefore, he took the sleeping-car express to Paddington.

===Chinese===

他想，最合适的人选是杰斐逊·派克。为敲定这一点，值得专程去一趟伦敦。于是当夜，他搭上开往帕丁顿的卧铺快车。

===Original===

Daw had given him the address﻿—17b, Kepple Street, off Russell Square, and before ten next morning he was there.

===Chinese===

道已把地址给了他——罗素广场（Russell Square）附近凯普尔街（Kepple Street）17b 号。第二天上午不到十点，他已到了那儿。

===Original===

Jefferson Pyke was a clean-shaven man of about forty, of rather more than medium height and stoutly built. He was a study in browns: brown eyes, a dusky complexion, hair nearly black, brown clothes and shoes, and a dark-brown tie. He looked keenly at his visitor, then pointed to a chair.

===Chinese===

杰斐逊·派克四十岁上下，脸刮得干干净净，个头略高于中等，身量敦实。他整个人像一幅棕色调的习作：棕色的眼睛，黝暗的肤色，近黑的头发，棕色的衣裤鞋履，再配一条深棕的领带。他锐利地打量来客，随即指了指一把椅子。

===Original===

“Mr. French?” he said, speaking deliberately. “What can I do for you, sir?”

===Chinese===

「弗伦奇先生？」他字斟句酌地说，「我能为您效什么劳，先生？」

===Original===

“I’ll tell you, Mr. Pyke,” French answered. “First of all, here is my professional card. I want some help from you in an investigation I am making.”

===Chinese===

「我这就奉告，派克先生。」弗伦奇答道，「首先，这是我的名片。我正在办理一桩调查，要向您讨些帮助。」

===Original===

Pyke glanced at the card and nodded.

===Chinese===

派克瞥了一眼名片，点点头。

===Original===

“A case on which I was engaged took me recently to Ashburton, and while there I heard of the tragic death of Mr. Pyke and Mr. Berlyn of the Veda Works staff. I understand that Mr. Pyke was a relative of yours?”

===Chinese===

「我经手的一桩案子，近来正巧办到阿什伯顿。在那边，我听说了维达工厂的派克先生和伯林先生双双罹难的惨事。据我了解，派克先生是您的亲戚？」

===Original===

“That is so. My first cousin.”

===Chinese===

「是这么回事。我的亲堂弟。」

===Original===

“Well, Mr. Pyke,” French said, gravely, “I have to inform you that a discovery had been made which may or may not have a bearing on your cousin’s fate. A body has been found﻿—the body of a murdered man. That body has not been identified, but there is a suggestion that it may be your cousin’s. I want to know if you can identify it?”

===Chinese===

「那么，派克先生，」弗伦奇郑重地说，「我必须通知您，近日有一项发现，与您堂弟的命运或有关联，或无关联。有人发现了一具尸体——一具被谋杀的男人的尸体。尸体身份尚未查明，但有一种推测，认为可能是您堂弟。我想知道，您能否辨认？」

===Original===

Mr. Pyke stared incredulously.

===Chinese===

派克先生瞪着眼，满脸不敢相信。

===Original===

“Good Heavens, Inspector! That’s an astonishing suggestion. You must surely be mistaken. I went down to Ashburton directly I heard of the accident, and there seemed no doubt then about what had happened. Tell me the particulars.”

===Chinese===

「天哪，探长！这说法可太惊人了。您一定是弄错了。我一听说出了事故，立马就赶到了阿什伯顿，当时事情的经过看来毫无疑义。请把细节讲给我听。」

===Original===

“About a fortnight ago, as you may have noticed in the papers, a crate was picked up in the sea off Burry Port in South Wales, which was found to contain the body of a murdered man. The face had been disfigured and there was no means of identification. However, I traced the crate and I learned that it was sent out from the Veda Works on the morning after your cousin and Mr. Berlyn disappeared.”

===Chinese===

「约莫两个星期以前，您或许在报上留意到了，南威尔士伯里港的外海捞起一只板条箱，里面装着一具被谋杀的男尸。脸面已被毁损，无从辨认身份。不过，我追查了那只板条箱，查明它是在您堂弟和伯林先生失踪的次日早晨，从维达工厂发出去的。」

===Original===

“Good Heavens!” Mr. Pyke exclaimed again. “Go on.”

===Chinese===

「天哪！」派克先生又是一声惊呼。「往下说。」

===Original===

“I made enquiries and the only persons known to have disappeared were those two men. You see the suggestion? I am sorry to have to ask you, but can you help me to identify the remains?”

===Chinese===

「我做了一番查访，已知的失踪者只有这两位先生。您看出这层意思了吧？很抱歉不得不劳驾您——您能帮我辨认遗骸吗？」

===Original===

Mr. Pyke’s face showed both amazement and horror.

===Chinese===

派克先生脸上又惊又怖。

===Original===

“This is terrible news, Inspector. I need hardly say I hope you are mistaken. Of course you may count on me to do all I can.”

===Chinese===

「这是可怕的消息，探长。不必说，我盼望是您弄错了。当然，您尽管指望我竭尽绵力。」

===Original===

“You think you can identify the body, then?”

===Chinese===

「这么说，您认为您认得出这具尸体？」

===Original===

“Surely I ought to recognise my own cousin?”

===Chinese===

「我自己的堂弟，我总该认得出来吧？」

===Original===

“Otherwise than by the face? Remember the face has been disfigured. I might say, indeed, it is nonexistent, it has been so savagely battered.”

===Chinese===

「不看脸面也认得出吗？别忘了，脸已经毁掉了。甚至可以说，那张脸已经不复存在——被砸得那么凶残。」

===Original===

“By Heaven! I hope you will get the man who did it!” Pyke said, hotly. “But that does not answer your question.” He hesitated. “If it is not possible to recognise the features, I’m not so sure. How do you suggest it might be done?”

===Chinese===

「老天在上！我盼望您逮住那下手的人！」派克激动地说，「可这并没回答您的问题。」他迟疑了一下。「要是面容认不成了，我就不敢说了。您看该怎么个辨认法？」

===Original===

French shrugged.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇耸了耸肩。

===Original===

“Identification otherwise than by the features is usually possible. It is a matter of observation. Some small physical defect, a crooked finger, the scar of an old cut, or mole on the neck﻿—there are scores of indications to the observant man.”

===Chinese===

「不凭面容的辨认，通常总是办得到的。这全在观察。什么小小的身体特征呀，手指弯曲呀，旧伤疤呀，脖子上的一颗痣呀——在善于观察的人眼里，这类标记数也数不清。」

===Original===

Mr. Pyke sat in silence for a few minutes.

===Chinese===

派克先生默然坐了几分钟。

===Original===

“Then I’m afraid I’m not very observant,” he said at last. “I can’t remember any such peculiarity in poor Stanley’s case.”

===Chinese===

「那恐怕我这人算不得善于观察了。」他终于开口，「可怜的斯坦利身上，这样的特征我一处也想不起来。」

===Original===

“Nothing in the shape of the finger nails,” French prompted. “No birthmark, no local roughness or discoloration of the skin?”

===Chinese===

「指甲的形状没有特别之处吗？」弗伦奇提示道，「没有胎记，没有哪里的皮肤局部粗糙或变色？」

===Original===

“By Jove!” Mr. Pyke exclaimed with a sudden gesture. “There is something. My cousin had a birthmark, a small red mark on his left arm, here. I remembered it directly you mentioned the word.”

===Chinese===

「哎呀！」派克先生猛地做了个手势，喊道。「还真有一样。我堂弟有块胎记，左臂上一小块红的，就在这儿。您一提这个词，我登时想起来了。」

===Original===

“Then you have been fairly intimate with your cousin? Have you often seen this mark?”

===Chinese===

「这么说，您同堂弟相当亲近？这印记常常见到？」

===Original===

“Seen it? Scores of times. We were boys together and I have noticed it again and again. Why, now I come to think of it, I saw it on these last holidays I spent with Stanley. We went to the south of France and shared a cabin in the steamer to Marseilles.”

===Chinese===

「见到？几十回都不止。我们小时候就在一起，这印记我见了又见。嗐，现在想来，上回同斯坦利一起度假，我还见过呢。我们去法国南部，去马赛（Marseilles）的轮船上合住一间舱。」

===Original===

“Could you describe it?”

===Chinese===

「您能描述它的样子吗？」

===Original===

“No, but I could sketch it.” He seized a piece of paper and drew a rough triangle.

===Chinese===

「描述不来，可我能画出来。」他抓过一张纸，画了个粗略的三角形。

===Original===

French laid his photograph beside the sketch. There could be no doubt that they represented the same object. Pyke seized the photograph.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇把照片摆到草图旁边。两者表现的分明是同一样东西。派克一把抓起照片。

===Original===

“That’s it. I could swear to it anywhere. You’ve found Stanley’s body right enough. Good Heavens! Inspector, it’s incredible! I could have sworn he hadn’t an enemy in the world. Have you any clue to the murderer?”

===Chinese===

「就是它。到哪儿我都能认它起誓。您找到的确确实实是斯坦利的尸体。天哪！探长，这真叫人难以置信！我敢起誓，他在这个世上连一个仇家也没有。凶手那边，您可有什么线索？」

===Original===

Natural caution and official training made French hedge.

===Chinese===

天性的谨慎和警务训练，都教弗伦奇把话说得含糊。

===Original===

“Not as yet,” he answered, assuring himself that his ideas about Berlyn were hypothetical. “I was hoping that you could give me a lead.”

===Chinese===

「眼下还没有。」他答道，一面提醒自己，他对伯林的那些想法还只是假说。「我正指望您能给我指点一条明路呢。」

===Original===

“I?” Jefferson Pyke shook his head. “Far from it. Even now I can scarcely credit the affair.”

===Chinese===

「我？」杰斐逊·派克摇了摇头。「差得远呢。到这会儿，这桩事我还难以置信。」

===Original===

“Well, I should like you to run over his associates and see if you can’t think of any who might have hated him. Now to start with the senior partner: What about Colonel Domlio?”

===Chinese===

「那么，我想请您把他来往的人在心里数一遍，看能不能想起哪个可能恨他。先从大合伙人说起：多姆利奥上校这个人，您怎么看？」

===Original===

Mr. Pyke had never met him and knew nothing about him, though he had heard his cousin mention his name. French went on through the list he had made at Ashburton till in the natural sequence he came to Berlyn.

===Chinese===

派克先生从未见过上校，对他一无所知，只是听堂弟提起过名字。弗伦奇顺着他在阿什伯顿开列的名单一路问下去，按自然的顺序，最后问到了伯林。

===Original===

“Now Mr. Berlyn. Could he have had a down on your cousin?”

===Chinese===

「再说说伯林先生。他会不会记恨您堂弟？」

===Original===

“But he was lost, too,” Pyke rejoined, then stopped and looked keenly at French. “By Jove! Inspector, I get your idea! You think Berlyn may have murdered him and cleared out?” He shook his head. “No, no. You are wrong. It is impossible. Berlyn wasn’t that sort. I knew him slightly and I confess I didn’t care for him, but he was not a murderer.”

===Chinese===

「可他自己也失踪了呀。」派克应道，随即打住，锐利地望着弗伦奇。「哎呀！探长，我明白您的意思了！您是想说，伯林可能谋害了他，自己远走高飞？」他摇了摇头。「不，不。您错了。这不可能。伯林不是那种人。我同他略有交往，老实说也不喜欢他，可他不是杀人犯。」

===Original===

“Why did you not like him, Mr. Pyke?”

===Chinese===

「您为什么不喜欢他呢，派克先生？」

===Original===

Pyke shrugged.

===Chinese===

派克耸了耸肩。

===Original===

“Hard to say. Not my style, perhaps. A good man, you know, and efficient and all that, but﻿—too efficient, shall I say? He expected too much from others; didn’t make allowances for human errors and frailties. Poor Mrs. Berlyn had rather a time with him.”

===Chinese===

「难说。兴许不是我一向投脾气的那种人。他是个好人，您知道，也干练，样样都好，可就是——该怎么说呢——干练得过了头？他对别人指望太多；人家出的错儿、人的短处，他一概不作体谅。可怜的伯林太太跟了他，日子着实不好过。」

===Original===

“How so?”

===Chinese===

「怎么讲？」

===Original===

“Well, an example will explain what I mean. On this last holiday after Stanley and I got back to London we met Berlyn and his wife, who were in town. The four of us dined together and went to a theatre. We were to meet at the restaurant at seven. Well, Mrs. Berlyn had been off somewhere on her own and she was five minutes late. What was that for a woman? But Berlyn was so ratty about it that I felt quite embarrassed. You see, he wouldn’t have been late himself. If he had said seven, he would have been there﻿—on the tick. He couldn’t see that other people were not made the same way.”

===Chinese===

「咳，举个例子就说明我的意思了。上次度假，斯坦利和我回到伦敦，碰上伯林和他太太也在城里。我们四个一道吃饭，一道看戏。约好七点在饭馆碰头。结果呢，伯林太太自己逛到别处去了，迟到了五分钟。一个女人家，这算得了什么？可伯林为这个恼火成那样，弄得我怪难为情的。您想，他自己是断不会迟到的。他要是说了七点，七点就准到场——分秒不差。他就是不明白，别人不是照他一个模子造出来的。」

===Original===

“I follow you. You say that Mrs. Berlyn had rather a time with him. Did they not get on?”

===Chinese===

「我明白您的意思。您说伯林太太跟了他日子不好过。他们处得不好吗？」

===Original===

“Oh, they got on﻿—as well as fifty percent of the married people get on. Berlyn did his duty to her strictly, even lavishly, but he expected the same in return. I don’t know that you could blame him. Strictly speaking, of course he was right. It was his instinct for scrupulously fair play.”

===Chinese===

「哦，处得来——就跟半数已婚夫妻处得来的程度差不多。伯林对她尽本分，一丝不苟，甚至不吝慷慨，可他也指望得到同等的回报。要说怪他，我倒说不上。严格说来，他自然在理。那是他天性里一丝不苟的公平劲儿。」

===Original===

“Your late cousin and Mrs. Berlyn were very good friends, were they not?”

===Chinese===

「您那位亡故的堂弟同伯林太太交情很深，是不是？」

===Original===

“We were both good friends with Mrs. Berlyn. Stanley and I knew her as children. In fact, it was through Stanley that Berlyn met her. I was in the Argentine at the time, but he told me about it. Berlyn was going for a holiday﻿—one of those cruises round the western Mediterranean. Stanley happened to have met Phyllis Considine, as she was then, in London, and she had mentioned she was going on the same trip. So he gave Berlyn an introduction. Berlyn, it appears, fell in love with her and was accepted before the cruise was over.”

===Chinese===

「我们俩同伯林太太都是好朋友。斯坦利和我从小就认得她。说起来，伯林还是通过斯坦利认识她的呢。那时我在阿根廷，是他写信告诉我的。伯林要去度假——乘那种环西地中海的邮轮。斯坦利恰好在伦敦结识了菲莉斯·康西丁（Phyllis Considine）——她那时还叫这个姓——她提过自己也搭同一趟船。斯坦利便给伯林写了封引荐。据说伯林爱上了她，航程未完，求婚就得了允准。」

===Original===

“Do you think Berlyn could have been jealous of your cousin?”

===Chinese===

「您看，伯林会不会为您堂弟吃过醋？」

===Original===

“I’m sure he could not, Inspector. Don’t get that bee into your bonnet. Stanley certainly went often to the house, but Berlyn was always friendly to him. I don’t for a moment believe there was anything to be jealous about.”

===Chinese===

「我可以肯定不会，探长。这个念头您可别钻牛角尖。斯坦利确实常上他们家去，可伯林待他一直友好。要说他俩有什么可醋的，我一刻也不信。」

===Original===

“There was enough intimacy for them to be talked about.”

===Chinese===

「他们的来往，深到了招人议论的地步。」

===Original===

“In Ashburton!” Pyke retorted, scornfully. “In a little one-horse place like that they’d talk no matter what you did.”

===Chinese===

「在阿什伯顿那种地方！」派克轻蔑地反诘。「在那么个巴掌大的偏僻小镇，随你做什么，人们都有的说。」

===Original===

“It was believed that there was something between them until about four months before the tragedy, then for some unknown reason the affair stopped.”

===Chinese===

「据信他们之间是有些什么的，直到惨案前约四个月；后来不知什么缘故，这层来往断了。」

===Original===

“That so?” Pyke retorted. “Well, if it stopped four months before the tragedy it couldn’t have caused it.”

===Chinese===

「是吗？」派克顶了一句。「嗐，既然惨案前四个月就断了，那这祸总不致是它闯出来的。」

===Original===

“Do you know where Mrs. Berlyn is now?”

===Chinese===

「您知道伯林太太如今在哪儿吗？」

===Original===

“Yes, in London; at 70b Park Walk, Chelsea, to be exact.”

===Chinese===

「知道，在伦敦；确切说，是切尔西（Chelsea）公园巷（Park Walk）70b 号。」

===Original===

French continued his questions, but without learning anything further of interest, and after cautioning Pyke to keep his own counsel, he took his leave.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇继续问了些问题，却没再问出什么值得留意的，叮嘱派克守口如瓶之后，便告辞了。

===Original===

So he had reached certainty at last! The body was Stanley Pyke’s. He had admittedly made four ghastly blunders in his test points and these he must now try to retrieve. There was also a reasonable suspicion that Charles Berlyn was the murderer. Splendid! He was getting on. As he went down to the Yard he felt he had some good work behind him to report.

===Chinese===

他终于拿到确凿无疑了！尸体就是斯坦利·派克的。无可讳言，他在那几项检验上犯了四个糟糕的大错，如今必须设法补救。此外还有一条说得过去的疑点：查尔斯·伯林（Charles Berlyn）就是凶手。好极了！案子有了进展。去苏格兰场的路上，他觉得身后已有一批漂亮活计可以交差。

## X London’s Further Contribution / X 伦敦的进一步贡献

===Original===

Now that he was in London, French decided that he should complete certain enquiries.

===Chinese===

既然人已到了伦敦，弗伦奇决定把几项查证办完。

===Original===

First he should satisfy himself that everything possible had been done to trace the letter-writers of the Euston and St. Pancras hotels and the purchaser of the money order for £62 10s. 0d. Next he must visit the manufacturers of the Ardlo magneto and get their views on short-circuited windings. Lastly he must have an interview with Mrs. Berlyn.

===Chinese===

首先，他要确证一下：尤斯顿旅馆和圣潘克拉斯旅馆两处写信人的下落，以及那张六十二英镑十先令邮政汇票的购买人，是否已经追查到了山穷水尽的地步。其次，他得去拜访阿德洛磁电机的制造商，听听他们对绕组短路的高见。最后，他还得会见伯林太太。

===Original===

As it happened, he took the last of these items first, and three o’clock that afternoon found him ascending the stairs of No. 70b Park Walk, Chelsea. The house was divided into a number of what seemed small but comfortable flats. Pretty expensive, French thought, as he rang.

===Chinese===

事有凑巧，他倒先办了最后一项——当天下午三点，他已经登上切尔西公园巷70b号的楼梯。这宅子隔成了若干套公寓，看上去都小巧而舒适。怕是不便宜呢，弗伦奇按门铃时心里想。

===Original===

A neatly dressed maid opened the door and, after taking in his card, announced that Mrs. Berlyn would see him. He followed her to a tiny, but pleasantly furnished drawing room, and there in a few minutes he was joined by the lady of the house.

===Chinese===

开门的是位衣着整洁的女仆，收下他的名片进去禀报，回来说伯林太太愿意见他。他跟着她走进一间小巧却陈设悦目的客厅，几分钟后，女主人也来了。

===Original===

French looked at her with some curiosity. Of medium height and with a slight, graceful figure, she still gave an impression of energy and competent efficiency. She was not beautiful, but her appearance was arresting and French felt instinctively that she was a woman to be reckoned with. Her manner was vivacious and French could imagine her dancing all night and turning up next morning to breakfast as cool and fresh and ready for anything as if she had had her accustomed eight hours’ sleep.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇带着几分好奇打量她。中等身材，体态轻盈优雅，她却照样给人一股精明干练、游刃有余的印象。她算不上美丽，容貌却引人注目，弗伦奇凭直觉感到，这是个不可小觑的女人。她举止活泼，弗伦奇简直能想见她通宵跳舞之后，第二天早晨照常出来吃早饭，照样神清气爽、随时可战，仿佛睡足了惯常的八个钟头。

===Original===

“Inspector French, Scotland Yard,” she said, briskly, glancing at the card in her hand. “Won’t you sit down, Mr. French, and tell me what I can do for you?”

===Chinese===

「苏格兰场弗伦奇探长。」她扫一眼手里的名片，爽利地说。「弗伦奇先生，请坐吧——有什么要我效劳的？」

===Original===

“Thank you, Mrs. Berlyn. I am sorry to say I have called on distressing business. It may or may not concern your late husband. I am hoping for information from you which may decide the point.”

===Chinese===

「多谢，伯林太太。很遗憾，我是为一桩令人痛心的事登门的。它可能与您亡夫有关，也可能无关。我正指望从您这里得到些情况，好把这一点定下来。」

===Original===

The lady’s expression became grave.

===Chinese===

太太的神色凝重起来。

===Original===

“Suppose you give me the details,” she suggested.

===Chinese===

「那就请您把详细情形讲给我听吧。」她提议道。

===Original===

“I am about to do so, but I warn you that you must prepare yourself for a shock. It is in connection with the tragedy by which Mr. Berlyn and Mr. Pyke were believed to have lost their lives.”

===Chinese===

「我正要讲，只是先得提醒您，务请做好受惊的准备。这件事，同伯林先生和派克先生据信双双罹难的那桩惨案有关。」

===Original===

Mrs. Berlyn started and her gaze became fixed intently on French.

===Chinese===

伯林太太猛地一惊，目光紧紧盯住弗伦奇。

===Original===

“It has been discovered that Mr. Pyke was not lost on the moor as was supposed. Of Mr. Berlyn’s fate nothing new has been learnt. But I deeply regret to inform you that Mr. Pyke was murdered.”

===Chinese===

「现已查明，派克先生并不像原先料想的那样迷失在荒沼上。伯林先生的下落，也没有任何新的消息。但我极为沉痛地通知您：派克先生是被人谋害的。」

===Original===

“Stanley Pyke murdered! Oh, impossible!” Horror showed on the lady’s face and her lips trembled. For a moment it looked as if she would give way to her emotion, but she controlled herself and asked for details.

===Chinese===

「斯坦利·派克被谋害了！噢，不可能！」太太脸上露出惊恐，嘴唇直打颤。有一瞬间，她看上去就要控制不住自己的情绪，但她强自镇定下来，要求讲述详情。

===Original===

French told her exactly what had occurred, from the discovery of the crate to Jefferson Pyke’s identification of the birthmark.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇把事情的经过原原本本讲给她听，从板条箱被发现，一直讲到杰斐逊·派克辨认出那块胎记。

===Original===

“I’m afraid it must be true,” she said, sadly, when he had finished. “I remember that birthmark, too. We were children together, the Pykes and I, and I have often seen it. Oh, I can’t say how sorry I am! Who *could* have done such a terrible thing? Stanley was so jolly and pleasant and kind. He was good to everyone and everyone liked him. Oh, it is too awful for words!”

===Chinese===

「只怕这是真的了。」他讲完后，她悲伤地说。「那块胎记我也记得。派克家和我，我们从小一起长大，我常常见到它。唉，我说不出有多难过！这么可怕的事，究竟*会*是谁干的？斯坦利那么快活、那么和气、那么厚道。他对谁都好，谁都喜欢他。唉，简直可怕得没法形容！」

===Original===

French made a noncommittal reply.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇不置可否地应了一声。

===Original===

“But what about my late husband?” Mrs. Berlyn went on. “You said nothing had been learnt about him. But﻿—if they were together﻿—?”

===Chinese===

「可我亡夫呢？」伯林太太接着问。「您说他的事毫无所获。可是——倘若他们俩当时是在一起——？」

===Original===

She paused suddenly, as if seeing that a meaning which she had not intended might be read into her words. But French replied, soothingly:

===Chinese===

她突然顿住，似乎觉察到这话可能被人听出她本没有的意思。弗伦奇却宽慰道：

===Original===

“That’s one of the things I wanted to talk to you about, Mrs. Berlyn. Did you know if either he or Mr. Pyke had any enemies? You need not fear to tell me the merest suspicions. I will act only on knowledge that I obtain, but your suspicion might suggest where to look for that knowledge.”

===Chinese===

「这正是我想同您谈的事情之一，伯林太太。据您所知，他或是派克先生，可有什么仇人？哪怕最轻微的怀疑，您也不必顾虑，尽管告诉我。我只凭自己查实的情况行事，但您的怀疑或许能指点我该到哪里去查。」

===Original===

“Are you suggesting that my husband might have been murdered also?” she said in a low voice.

===Chinese===

「您是说，我丈夫也可能被人谋害了？」她低声问。

===Original===

“Not necessarily. I am asking if you can think of anything which could sustain that view?”

===Chinese===

「不一定。我只是问，您能不能想到什么足以支持这种看法的事？」

===Original===

Mrs. Berlyn could not think of anything. She did not know of anyone who had a grudge against either of the men. Indeed, only for the inspector’s assurance she could not have brought herself to believe that Mr. Pyke had met so dreadful an end.

===Chinese===

伯林太太想不出什么来。她不知道有谁对这两位先生怀恨在心。说实在的，若不是探长言之凿凿，她怎么也不能相信，派克先生竟落得这样可怕的下场。

===Original===

French then began pumping her in his quiet, skilful way. But though she answered all his questions with the utmost readiness, he did not learn much that he had not already known.

===Chinese===

接着，弗伦奇开始用他那一套不动声色的巧劲儿套她的话。她有问必答，极为爽快，可他并没有得到多少先前不知道的东西。

===Original===

Her father, she told him, was a doctor in Lincoln and there she had known the Pykes. Stanley’s mother﻿—his father was dead﻿—lived about a mile from the town, and he and his cousin Jefferson, who boarded with them, used to walk in daily to school. The three had met at parties and children’s dances and had once spent a holiday together at the seaside. The Pykes had left the town when the boys had finished their schooling and she had lost sight of them. Then one day she had met Stanley in London and he told her that he was at the Veda Works. She had mentioned that she was going on a cruise to the Mediterranean and he had said that his employer, Mr. Berlyn, was going on the same trip and to be sure to look out for him. That was the way she had met Mr. Berlyn. He had proposed to her on the trip and she had accepted him.

===Chinese===

据她说，她父亲是林肯的一位医生，她就是在那儿认识派克家的。斯坦利的母亲——他父亲已经去世——住在城外约一英里的地方，他和寄住在他们家的堂弟杰斐逊每天步行进城上学。他们三个在聚会和儿童舞会上相见，还一道去海滨度过一次假。两个男孩学业结束后，派克家就搬离了那座城，她也同他们断了音讯。后来有一天，她在伦敦遇见斯坦利，他说自己在维达工厂做事。她提到自己要乘船去地中海巡游，他说他的东家伯林先生也搭同一趟船，嘱咐她务必留心照应。她就是这样认识伯林先生的。旅程中他向她求婚，她答应了。

===Original===

French then delicately broached the question of her relations with Stanley Pyke. And here for the first time he was not satisfied by her replies. That there had been something more between them than friendship he strongly suspected. Indeed, Mrs. Berlyn practically admitted it. As a result of French’s diplomatic probing it came out that Mr. Berlyn had shown marked disapproval of their intimacy and that about four months prior to the tragedy they had decided that for the sake of peace they should see less of each other. They had carried out this resolve and Berlyn’s resentment had apparently vanished.

===Chinese===

随后，弗伦奇委婉地探起她同斯坦利·派克的关系来。在这里，他头一回对她的回答不甚满足。两人之间不止是友谊，这一点他深为怀疑。而伯林太太也几乎是默认了。经弗伦奇旁敲侧击地一问，问出了这么一段内情：伯林先生对他们过从甚密颇示不悦，约在惨案发生前四个月，两人为了相安无事，决定减少来往。两人都履行了这一决定，伯林的芥蒂似乎也随之消散。

===Original===

French next turned to the subject of Colonel Domlio, but here Mrs. Berlyn had as good as laughed. It appeared that the man had tried to flirt with her, but her opinion was evidently that there was no fool like an old fool. French had no doubt that any lovemaking that might have taken place was not serious, on the lady’s side, at all events.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇接着转到多姆利奥上校这个话题上，可这一节，伯林太太几乎付之一笑。据说此人对她有过殷勤之举，而她的见解显然是：天下之蠢，莫过于老糊涂。弗伦奇毫不怀疑，即便有过什么调情，至少在女方这一边，从来不是当真的。

===Original===

Thinking that he had obtained all the information that he was likely to get, French at last rose to go. But Mrs. Berlyn signed to him to sit down again and said, gravely:

===Chinese===

弗伦奇觉得能问的大概都问到了，终于起身告辞。伯林太太却示意他重新坐下，郑重说道：

===Original===

“If that is all, Mr. French, I want to ask you a question. I never think there is any use in pretending about things, and from your questions I cannot but guess what is in your mind. You think my late husband may have murdered Mr. Pyke?”

===Chinese===

「要是就这些的话，弗伦奇先生，我想问您一句话。我一向认为，事情上遮遮掩掩毫无用处，从您这些问话里，我不难猜到您的心思。您是认为，我亡夫可能谋害了派克先生？」

===Original===

“I take it from that, Mrs. Berlyn, that you want a perfectly straight answer? Well, I shall give it to you. The idea, of course, occurred to me, as it would to anyone in my position. I am bound to investigate it and I am going to do so. But I can say without reservation that so far it remains an idea.”

===Chinese===

「这么说，伯林太太，您是要一句完完全全的实话了？好，我就给您实话。这个念头我自然起过——处在我这个位置上，谁都会起。职责所在，我必须查，我也一定查。但我可以毫无保留地说：到目前为止，它仍然只是一个念头。」

===Original===

Mrs. Berlyn bowed.

===Chinese===

伯林太太欠身一礼。

===Original===

“Thank you for that. Of course I recognise that you must investigate all possibilities, and I recognise, too, that you will not give any weight to what I am going to say. But I must tell you that if you suspect Mr. Berlyn you are making a mistake. Though he was not perfect, he was utterly incapable of a crime like that﻿—utterly. If you had ever met him you would have known that. I wish I could say or do something to convince you. Besides, if he were alive, why did he disappear? If he were guilty, would he not have come forward with a story that Mr. Pyke had gone alone across the moor and been lost in the mires?”

===Chinese===

「谢谢您这句话。我当然明白，您必须排查一切可能；我也明白，我接下来要说的话，您不会给它多少分量。但我必须告诉您：您若怀疑伯林先生，那就错了。他虽算不上完人，却万万做不出那样的罪孽——万万做不出。您只要见过他，就会明白这一点。我真希望说点什么、做点什么，好让您相信。再说，倘若他还活着，他为什么要销声匿迹？他若真有罪，何不编一套说辞出面——就说派克先生独自一人穿越荒沼，陷进泥潭里失踪了？」

===Original===

French had already noted the point as the chief difficulty in his theory, and he admitted it fully. He added that Mrs. Berlyn’s statement had made an impression on him and that he would not fail to bear it in mind. Then promising to let her know the result of his enquiry, he took his leave.

===Chinese===

这一难点，弗伦奇早已认定是自己推论里最过硬的障碍，此刻也坦然认了账。他补充说，伯林太太这番话给了他很深的印象，他一定铭记在心。随后他答应把侦查结果告知她，便起身告辞。

===Original===

He had not lied when he said her statement had impressed him. That it represented her firm conviction he had not the least doubt. And it certainly was a point in Berlyn’s favour that such testimony should be forthcoming from his wife, when it was evident that their married life had been an indifferent success. Of course it might be simply that the woman did not wish to be involved in the misery and disgrace which would come with proof of Berlyn’s guilt. But French did not think it was this. Her thought had seemed to be for her husband rather than herself.

===Chinese===

他说这番话令他印象深刻，并非虚言。这确是她的坚定信念，对此他毫不怀疑。何况他们的婚姻生活明摆着谈不上美满，做妻子的还能作这样的证词，这当然算是伯林的一个有利之点。当然，也可能不过是这女人不愿沾灾惹祸——一旦伯林罪名坐实，愁苦与耻辱便会接踵而至。但弗伦奇不这么想。她心里惦记的，似乎是她丈夫，而不是她自己。

===Original===

It was still fairly early in the afternoon and French thought he would have time to make another call. He therefore walked up the Fulham Road and took an eastbound district train at South Kensington. Half an hour later he was at the headquarters of the Ardlo Magneto Company in Queen Elizabeth Street.

===Chinese===

午后时光尚早，弗伦奇觉得还来得及再走访一处。于是他沿富勒姆路走去，在南肯辛顿搭上一列东行的区域线列车。半小时后，他已到了伊丽莎白女王街阿德洛磁电机公司的总部。

===Original===

When the managing director heard French’s business he touched a bell.

===Chinese===

总经理听明弗伦奇的来意，按了按铃。

===Original===

“You had better see Mr. Illingworth, our chief electrical engineer,” he said. “I am afraid I could not help you in these technical matters.”

===Chinese===

「您还是见见我们的总电气工程师伊灵沃斯（Illingworth）先生吧。」他说。「这类技术问题，恐怕我帮不上您的忙。」

===Original===

Mr. Illingworth was a pleasant young man with a quiet, efficient manner. He took French to his office, supplied him with cigarettes, and asked what he could do for him.

===Chinese===

伊灵沃斯先生是位讨人喜欢的年轻人，举止沉静干练。他把弗伦奇领进自己的办公室，敬上香烟，问他有何贵干。

===Original===

French put his problem, recounting the enquiries he had already made.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇说明来意，把自己已经做过的查证复述了一遍。

===Original===

“Those people told you quite correctly,” was Mr. Illingworth’s answer. “Your question is this: Could a man drive a car up to a certain place and then short circuit the magneto armature so that the car couldn’t be started again? The answer is, Yes, but not without leaving marks.”

===Chinese===

「那些人对您说的完全在理。」伊灵沃斯先生这样回答。「您的问题是：一个人能不能把车开到某个地点，然后把磁电机的电枢弄成短路，让车再也发动不起来？答案是：能，但不可能不留痕迹。」

===Original===

“But that’s just my puzzle,” French returned. “That’s exactly what seems to have been done.”

===Chinese===

「可这正是叫我犯难的地方，」弗伦奇答道，「事情看起来恰恰就是这么干的。」

===Original===

“Well,” Mr. Illingworth answered with a smile, “you may take it from me that it wasn’t.”

===Chinese===

「唔，」伊灵沃斯先生含笑回答，「这一点您尽管信我的话：不是这么干的。」

===Original===

“Then in the case that I have described, the breakdown must have been a pure accident?”

===Chinese===

「那么在我所说的那种情形里，那场故障就必定是纯粹的意外了？」

===Original===

“I should say, absolutely. Mind you, I don’t say that a breakdown couldn’t be faked without leaving traces. It could be. But not so as to stop the car then and there. The concealed injury would take time to develop.”

===Chinese===

「我看是纯粹的意外。不过您听着，我不是说故障没法伪造得不着痕迹——能。可没法让车当场就地趴窝。暗藏的损伤发作起来，需要时间。」

===Original===

“That’s a bit cryptic, isn’t it? Can you make it clearer to a lay intelligence?”

===Chinese===

「这话有点儿玄，是不是？能给一个外行的脑子讲得更明白些吗？」

===Original===

“Well, it is possible to damage the insulation by jamming a needle into the armature winding between the wire and the iron core, and if you’re careful it’ll leave no mark. But it won’t disable the magneto straight away. In fact, the car will run as usual and it may be a considerable time before any defect shows. But sparking takes place at the injury, perhaps at first only when the engine is working specially hard. This causes carbonisation of the insulation, leading eventually to complete breakdown. The car begins to misfire and it gradually grows worse until it won’t run at all.”

===Chinese===

「是这样：把一根钢针扎进电枢绕组，卡在导线和铁芯之间，是可以损伤绝缘的，只要手法小心，不会留下痕迹。但它不能立即使磁电机失灵。事实上，车会照常行驶，也许要过相当长的时间才显出毛病。但受伤处会跳火花，起初也许只在发动机格外吃力的时候。这会使绝缘碳化，最终彻底击穿。车子开始断火，毛病渐渐加重，直到完全发动不起来。」

===Original===

“I follow you. I may take it, then, that it is possible to cause a breakdown without leaving a mark, but that this is a comparatively lengthy process and cannot be done at a given time.”

===Chinese===

「我明白了。那么我是否可以认定：不留痕迹地造成故障是可能的，但这是一个比较漫长的过程，没法在指定的时刻发作？」

===Original===

“That’s right.”

===Chinese===

「正是如此。」

===Original===

“Suppose the winding was short-circuited as you describe, could an electrician afterwards tell what had been done?”

===Chinese===

「假如绕组真像您说的那样短了路，事后电工能看出被动过手脚吗？」

===Original===

“No. It might have happened through some carelessness in the original winding.”

===Chinese===

「看不出。也许当初绕线时有些粗心，也可能出这样的毛病。」

===Original===

“That seems pretty clear. Now, just one other point, Mr. Illingworth. Those people, Makepeace, in Ashburton, sent the actual magneto up here to be overhauled. Can you trace it and let me know just what was wrong?”

===Chinese===

「这算是相当清楚了。还有一点，伊灵沃斯先生。阿什伯顿的梅克皮斯那几家，把那台磁电机原件送到你们这里来检修过。您能查到它，告诉我究竟是什么毛病吗？」

===Original===

“Certainly. We have records of every machine which passes through our hands.” He consulted an index, finally withdrawing a card. “This is it. Sent in from John Makepeace, Ashburton, on Monday, twenty-second August. Would that date work in?”

===Chinese===

「当然。凡经我们之手的机器，都有记录。」他翻检一本索引，最后抽出一张卡片。「就是它。八月二十二日，星期一，阿什伯顿的约翰·梅克皮斯寄来检修。这个日期对得上吗？」

===Original===

“Yes, that’s all right.”

===Chinese===

「对，没错。」

===Original===

“We’ve not had another from Makepeace for five years previously, so it must be,” Mr. Illingworth went on, rapidly turning over the cards. “Well, it’s just what we were speaking of. It failed from a short-circuit in the armature winding and it might have been caused purposely or it might not. There was nothing to indicate.”

===Chinese===

「此前整整五年，梅克皮斯再没送来过别的，所以必定是它。」伊灵沃斯先生继续说，一面飞快地翻动卡片。「喏，正是我们方才讲的那种情形。它坏在电枢绕组短路，可能是蓄意造成的，也可能不是。看不出任何迹象。」

===Original===

French rose.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇站起身来。

===Original===

“That’s good enough for me,” he declared.

===Chinese===

「这对我够用了。」他说。

===Original===

He felt his brain reel as he considered the contradictory nature of the evidence he was getting. The breakdown of the car *had* happened, and at a time and place which made it impossible to doubt that it had been deliberately caused. To cause such a breakdown was mechanically impossible. That was the dilemma which confronted him. And the further he probed this contradiction, the more strongly he found its conflicting details confirmed.

===Chinese===

细想自己得到的证据竟如此自相矛盾，他觉得脑瓜发晕。那场故障*确实*发生了，而且发生的时间和地点摆在那里，使人无法怀疑它是蓄意造成的。可要造成这样一场故障，从机械上讲又绝无可能。这就是摆在他面前的两难。而这层矛盾他追得越深，那些彼此抵触的细节反倒被证实得越牢。

===Original===

In a dream he returned to the Yard, and there with an effort switched his mind off the conundrum and on to the features of his case which had been dealt with from headquarters.

===Chinese===

他恍恍惚惚回到苏格兰场，在那儿强打精神，把心思从这道谜题上挪开，转到案子中由总部经办的几个环节上去。

===Original===

Inspector Tanner, it appeared, had handled these matters, and by a lucky chance French found him just about to leave for home.

===Chinese===

这些事看来是坦纳（Tanner）探长经手的，而碰巧得很，弗伦奇撞见他正要动身回家。

===Original===

“I’ll walk with you,” said French. “I don’t want to delay you, and what’s more to the point, I want to get home myself.”

===Chinese===

「我陪您走一段，」弗伦奇说，「我不想耽搁您——更要紧的是，我自己也想回家。」

===Original===

Tanner was a man who liked a joke, or at least what he considered a joke. He now chaffed French on being unable to carry on his case by himself, and they sparred amicably for some time before coming to business. But Tanner was also exceedingly able, and when he described what he had done at the hotels and post office, French was satisfied that no further information could be extracted from these sources.

===Chinese===

坦纳是个爱开玩笑的人——至少爱开他自己认作玩笑的玩笑。这会儿他便打趣弗伦奇，说他的案子离了帮手自己办不下去，两人你来我往，友善地斗了一阵嘴才谈到正事。不过坦纳也精明干练得出奇，等他讲完在两家旅馆和邮局所做的一切，弗伦奇便放了心：从这些源头再也榨不出更多的东西了。

===Original===

All the next day, which was Sunday, the problem of the magneto remained subconsciously in French’s mind, and when on Monday morning he took his place in the 10:30 a.m. Limited to return to Devonshire, he was still pondering it. In a dream he watched the bustle of departure on the platform, the arrival of more and ever more travellers, the appropriation of seats, the disposal of luggage. (That armature *had* been tampered with. It must have been, because otherwise it would not have worked in with a prearranged crime.) Lord! What a pile of luggage for one woman to travel with! American, he betted. (But, it could not have been done at the time. In no way could it have been made to fail just when it was wanted.) What price that for a natty suit? Why, the man was a moving chessboard! What was the connection between chessboard suits and horses? (It must have been tampered with; but it couldn’t have been. That was the confounded problem.) There was the guard with his green flag, looking critically up and down and glancing first at his watch and then over his shoulder at the platform clock. It was just twenty-nine and a half minutes past. In another half minute.﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===

第二天是星期天，磁电机这个难题一整天都沉在弗伦奇意识的底层；星期一早晨，他登上上午10点30分返回德文郡的特别快车，在座位上坐定时，仍在心里盘算。他恍恍惚惚望着月台上启程前的忙乱，旅客越聚越多，座位被一一占去，行李被逐件安顿。（那台电枢*确实*被动过手脚。必定动过，否则它配合不上一桩预先筹划的罪案。）天哪！一个女人出门，竟带这么一大堆行李！准是个美国人，他敢打赌。（可是，事发当时没法动手。无论如何，不可能让它在正要用的当口失灵。）那身时髦西装，开价几何？嗬，这汉子活脱一块会走路的棋盘格！棋盘格西装同马匹又有什么相干？（必定动过手脚；可又不可能动过手脚。可恶，难题就难在这里。）车长举着绿旗，挑剔地上下打量，先看看怀表，又回头瞧瞧月台大钟。此刻刚过二十九分半。再过半分钟。……

===Original===

Suddenly into French’s mind flashed an idea and he sat for a moment motionless, as with a sort of trembling eagerness he considered it. Why, his problem was no problem at all! There was a solution of the simplest and most obvious kind! How had he been stupid enough not to have seen it?

===Chinese===

忽然，一个念头闪进弗伦奇的脑海。他有一阵子坐着纹丝不动，怀着一种近乎发颤的兴奋掂量着它。嗐，他这道难题根本就算不上难题！有一种再简单、再明白不过的解法！他怎么会蠢到一直没有看出来？

===Original===

As the guard waved his flag French sprang to his feet, and, amid the execrations of the porters, he hurled himself and his baggage from the moving train. Then, smiling pleasantly at the exasperated officials, he hurried from the station, jumped into a taxi, and told the man to drive to the Ardlo Magneto Works in Queen Elizabeth Street.

===Chinese===

车长挥旗的一刹那，弗伦奇霍地跳起，在搬运工的一片咒骂声中，拖着自己和行李，从开动着的火车上纵身跃下。随后他朝气急败坏的站员们和气地一笑，匆匆出了车站，跳上一辆出租汽车，吩咐司机开往伊丽莎白女王街的阿德洛磁电机厂。

===Original===

“Sorry to trouble you so soon again, Mr. Illingworth,” he apologised on being shown in, “but I’ve thought of a way in which that car could have been disabled at the time and place required and I want to know if it will hold water.”

===Chinese===

「这么快又来麻烦您，实在过意不去，伊灵沃斯先生。」被领进来后他先赔了句不是，「不过我想到了一个法子，能让那辆车在指定的时间、指定的地点趴窝，我想请教您，它站不站得住脚。」

===Original===

“If your method covers all the factors in the case as you have described it, I should like to hear it, Mr. French.”

===Chinese===

「只要您这个法子把您所描述的案情各项因素都照顾到了，我倒愿意听听，弗伦奇先生。」

===Original===

“Well, it’s simple enough, if it’s nothing else. I take it that if the magneto of my car goes wrong I can buy another?”

===Chinese===

「唔，要说别的谈不上，简单是够简单的。我这么想：要是我车上的磁电机坏了，我能再买一台，对不对？」

===Original===

“Why of course! But I don’t follow you.”

===Chinese===

「那当然！可我没听懂您的意思。」

===Original===

“They are all made to a standard﻿—interchangeable?”

===Chinese===

「它们都是按统一规格造的——可以互换？」

===Original===

Mr. Illingworth whistled.

===Chinese===

伊灵沃斯先生打了个呼哨。

===Original===

“Gee! I’m beginning to get you! Yes, they’re all made standard. There are several models, you understand, but all the magnetos of any given model are interchangeable.”

===Chinese===

「嚯！我有点儿明白您了！对，全是标准件。您知道，型号有好几种，但同一型号的磁电机，台台可以互换。」

===Original===

“Good! Now tell me, what’s to prevent my man from buying a duplicate magneto, damaging the armature winding invisibly with a needle, and running it on his car till it gives up; then taking it off, carrying it as a spare, and putting it on again when he had got the car to the point of breakdown?”

===Chinese===

「好！那么请告诉我，有什么能拦住我那位先生：买一台同样的磁电机，用针把电枢绕组暗暗弄伤，装在自己车上跑到它报废为止；然后把它卸下来，当作备件随身带着，等把车弄到该抛锚的地方，再把它装上去？」

===Original===

“You’ve got it, Mr. French! Great, that is! I didn’t think it was possible, and there, as you say, it’s as simple as A B C.”

===Chinese===

「您说中了，弗伦奇先生！真是绝了！我原以为这不可能，可正如您所说，这简单得就跟数一二三似的。」

===Original===

“Well,” said French. “Then did he?”

===Chinese===

「那么，」弗伦奇说，「他到底这么干了没有？」

===Original===

Illingworth looked his question and French went on:

===Chinese===

伊灵沃斯把疑问写在了脸上，弗伦奇接着说：

===Original===

“I’m looking to you for proof of the theory. First, do these magnetos carry a number? If so, is there a record of the number fitted to each car? If so, what was the number supplied with Mr. Berlyn’s car? Next, is that the number that came in for repair? Next, was there a magneto of that type ordered separately recently, and if so, by whom?”

===Chinese===

「证实这套推论，我就全指望您了。第一，这些磁电机上带编号吗？若带，装在每辆车上的编号可有记录？若有，配在伯林先生车上的那台编号是多少？第二，送来检修的是不是那个编号？第三，近来可有人单独订购过那个型号的磁电机？若有，订购人是谁？」

===Original===

“Steady on, Mr. French,” Mr. Illingworth laughed. “What do you take me for? I’m not a detective. Now let’s go over that again, one thing at a time. Magnetos carry a number, yes, and we have a note of the numbers supplied to the different car manufacturers. They can tell you the number of the magneto they put on any given car. What car are you interested in?”

===Chinese===

「您慢着点儿，弗伦奇先生。」伊灵沃斯先生笑道。「您把我当成什么人了？我又不是侦探。来，咱们把话从头捋一遍，一次说一件。磁电机带编号，没错，供给各家汽车制造厂的编号我们都记着底。装在哪辆车上的是哪个编号，厂家能告诉您。您问的是哪辆车？」

===Original===

“A fifteen-twenty four-seater Mercury touring car, number thirty-seven thousand and sixteen, supplied through Makepeace to a Mr. Berlyn of Ashburton.”

===Chinese===

「一辆十五至二十马力的四座『墨丘利』旅行车，编号三万七千零一十六，经梅克皮斯之手，供给阿什伯顿的一位伯林先生。」

===Original===

“Right. I’ll ring up the Mercury people now.”

===Chinese===

「好。我这就给『墨丘利』那边打电话。」

===Original===

Mr. Illingworth was indefatigable in his enquiries, but he was not prepared for the state of delighted enthusiasm into which his results threw French.

===Chinese===

伊灵沃斯先生查起来不辞劳苦，可查出的结果竟叫弗伦奇欢喜成那副模样，倒是他始料未及的。

===Original===

“That’s got it,” the latter cried, eagerly. “A long shot, but a bull’s-eye! I have to thank you for it, Mr. Illingworth, and you don’t know how grateful I am.”

===Chinese===

「这就成了！」弗伦奇急切地喊道。「放的一记远枪，偏偏正中靶心！这一功得记在您账上，伊灵沃斯先生，您不知道我有多感激。」

===Original===

The first fact was not encouraging. The magneto which had been supplied originally with Mr. Berlyn’s car was the same that had been sent in by Makepeace with the short-circuited winding. So far, therefore, the breakdown might have been genuine enough. But it was the second item which had so transported French. A precisely similar magneto had been sold as a spare about a month earlier and under circumstances which left no doubt as to the motive. It had been ordered by a Mr. Henry Armstrong, in a typewritten letter headed “The Westcliff Hotel, Bristol,” and it was to be sent to the parcels office at St. David’s Station, Exeter, marked, “To be kept till called for.” The letter was being sent over by hand, and when French received it a few minutes later he saw that it had been typed by the same machine as that ordering the duplicator.

===Chinese===

头一条消息并不鼓舞人。伯林先生车上原配的那台磁电机，正是梅克皮斯送来检修的那台短路绕组的机器。单看这一条，那场故障尽可以是真的。可叫弗伦奇喜出望外的，是第二条。约莫一个月前，有人买走过一台一模一样的磁电机充作备件，而且种种情形使动机昭然若揭。订货人是位亨利·阿姆斯特朗（Henry Armstrong）先生，来函是打字的，抬头是「布里斯托尔西崖旅馆（Westcliff Hotel）」，机器要寄往埃克塞特圣大卫车站（St. David's Station）的包裹房，标注「留存待提取」。那封信正由专人送来，几分钟后弗伦奇拿到手里一看：打这封信的，同打那封订购复印机的信，是同一台打字机。

===Original===

“That’s fine, Mr. Illingworth,” he repeated in high delight. “That’s one of my major difficulties overcome. I just want you to tell me one other thing. How long would it take to change the magneto﻿—out in the country on a dark night?”

===Chinese===

「好极了，伊灵沃斯先生。」他喜不自胜地连声说。「我的一大难关就这么破了。只想再请教一件事。换一台磁电机要多久——在乡间，黑夜里？」

===Original===

“It’s a half-hour’s job for a skilled man. The actual lifting in and out of the machine is easy, but the setting is the trouble. The contact-breaker, as I’m sure you know, has to be set so as to give the spark at the right point in the engine cycle. That takes a bit of time.”

===Chinese===

「熟手来干，半个钟头的活。机器抬进抬出不费事，麻烦在调校。断电器您必定知道，得调到发动机循环中的正确时刻跳火。这就费些工夫。」

===Original===

“I follow that. But is there no way that the adjustable parts could be set beforehand to save that time?”

===Chinese===

「这我明白。可那些可调的部件，难道没法事先调好，省下这道工夫吗？」

===Original===

“That’s right. They could be marked and everything set to the marks. That would speed things up.”

===Chinese===

「可以的。做上记号，到时候样样照记号对准。那就快了。」

===Original===

“By how much, should you say?”

===Chinese===

「能快多少，您估摸？」

===Original===

“With everything marked, a man could do the whole thing in fifteen minutes.”

===Chinese===

「样样都做了记号的话，整桩活儿一刻钟就能完。」

===Original===

“Good!” said French. “I guess that’s everything at last.”

===Chinese===

「好！」弗伦奇说。「我想这回总算问齐了。」

===Original===

He returned to Paddington and caught the 1:30 express for Exeter. He was overjoyed at his progress. The issue was rapidly narrowing.

===Chinese===

他回到帕丁顿，赶上1点30分开往埃克塞特的快车。有这样的进展，他喜不自胜。症结正在迅速收窄。

===Original===

How rapidly it was narrowing struck him even more forcibly as he thought of a further point. The trick had been played with Berlyn’s car. Could it have been done without Berlyn’s knowledge? Could, in fact, anyone but Berlyn have carried it out? French did not think so. It was beginning to look as if the solution of the whole problem were in sight.

===Chinese===

想到另一层，这收窄的速度更叫他心头震动。这花招是拿伯林的车使的。这事能瞒着伯林干成吗？说实在的，除了伯林，还有谁能干得成？弗伦奇认为不能。整个案子的谜底，眼看就要在望了。

===Original===

At Exeter he went about the package. As far as book entries were concerned, he was quickly satisfied. But no one remembered the transaction, nor could anyone recall enquiries having been made by a tallish, red-faced man with light hair and glasses.

===Chinese===

到了埃克塞特，他便着手查那件包裹。单看账面记录，他很快就满意了。可是谁也不记得那桩交易，也想不起曾有个子偏高、红脸膛、浅色头发戴眼镜的人来打听过。

===Original===

Nothing daunted, French caught the last train from Exeter to Ashburton, full of an eager anxiety to get to grips with his remaining problems.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇毫不气馁，赶上埃克塞特开往阿什伯顿的末班车，满心急切，巴不得立刻扑上去，解决他剩下的那些难题。

## XI John Gurney, Night Watchman / XI 约翰·格尼，夜班守卫

===Original===

French had now reconciled the apparent contradiction in regard to one of his four test points. Obviously his next job was to clear up the other three.

===Chinese===

他指望用来验证此案的四个要点，其中一处的表面矛盾，弗伦奇如今已经理顺了。下一步显然是把其余三处也弄个水落石出。

===Original===

As he considered on which he should first concentrate, his mind fastened on the one point which at the time had seemed not completely satisfactory﻿—the slightly suspicious manner of Gurney, the night watchman. During the night, as he now knew, the body of Stanley Pyke had been taken to the works and put into the crate. It was impossible that this could have been done without Gurney’s knowledge. Gurney must be made to speak.

===Chinese===

他盘算着先集中对付哪一点，心思却落在了当时便觉得不甚熨帖的那一处——夜班守卫格尼那副略嫌可疑的神态。他如今已经知道，那天夜里，斯坦利·派克的尸体被运进了工厂，装进了板条箱。这事要瞒着格尼办成，断无可能。必须叫格尼开口。

===Original===

Accordingly, after breakfast next morning he set off to the man’s house. He passed out of the town on the Newton Abbot road, then turning into a lane to the left, struck up the side of the valley. Soon he reached the cottage, a tiny place with deep overhanging eaves and creeper-covered walls. In front was a scrap of well-kept garden and in the garden was the man himself.

===Chinese===

于是，第二天早晨吃过早饭，他便动身往这人家里去。他出了城，走上牛顿阿伯特大道，随即拐进左手一条小巷，顺着谷侧的坡道往上走。不多时便到了那座村舍——小小的一处屋子，屋檐低低地探出，墙上爬满藤蔓。屋前是一小方拾掇得整整齐齐的园子，园子里正是那人本人。

===Original===

“Good morning, Gurney,” French greeted him. “I thought you would have been in bed by now.”

===Chinese===

「早安，格尼。」弗伦奇招呼道。「我还当你这会儿已经睡下了。」

===Original===

“I be just going,” answered the old man. “I came out an’ begun a bit o’ weeding an’ the time ran round without my noticing.”

===Chinese===

「俺这就走。」老头儿答道。「俺出来锄了会儿草，没留神，时候就溜过去了。」

===Original===

“That’s lucky for me,” said French, heartily. “I want a word with you. A nice place you’ve got here.”

===Chinese===

「这可巧了。」弗伦奇热诚地说。「我有话要跟你说。你这地方真不错。」

===Original===

“Not too bad, it ain’t,” the other admitted, looking about him with obvious pride. “The soil’s a bit ’eavy, but it don’t do so bad.”

===Chinese===

「也不赖，真不赖。」对方环顾四周，显然颇为得意地认可道。「土性儿是黏了点，可长势倒还不坏。」

===Original===

“Good for your roses, surely? Those are fine ones beside the house.”

===Chinese===

「这土对你的玫瑰准有好处吧？房边那一畦就很好。」

===Original===

Gurney laid aside his hoe and led the way to the really magnificent bed of La Frances to which French had pointed. It was evident that these were the old man’s passion. French was not a gardener, but he knew enough to talk intelligently on the subject and his appreciation evidently went straight to the watchman’s heart. For some minutes they discussed horticulture, and then French wore gradually round to the object of his visit.

===Chinese===

格尼放下锄头，领着路，朝弗伦奇指的那畦着实气派的「法兰西」月季走去。看得出，这些花是老头儿的心头所好。弗伦奇算不得园艺里手，但好在懂得够多，谈起来能说到点子上，他这一番赏识显然径直说进了守卫的心窝。两人谈论了一阵园艺，弗伦奇才渐渐把话绕到此行的正题上。

===Original===

“Terrible business that about Mr. Berlyn and Mr. Pyke,” he essayed. “It must have set this town talking.”

===Chinese===

「伯林先生和派克先生那档子事，真够惨的。」他试着开口。「想必满城都议论开了。」

===Original===

“It didn’t ’alf, sir. Everyone was sorry for the poor gentlemen. They was well liked, they was.”

===Chinese===

「可不是嘛，先生。大伙儿都替两位可怜的先生难过。他们人缘好着呢，真是。」

===Original===

“And that was another terrible affair,” pursued French after the local tragedy had been adequately discussed, “that finding of the dead body in the crate. Extraordinary how the body could have been put in.”

===Chinese===

等本地的惨事谈得差不多了，弗伦奇又接下去：「还有一档子事也够吓人的——板条箱里发现死尸那桩。尸首究竟是怎么装进去的，真叫人想不通。」

===Original===

“I didn’t ’ear naught about that,” Gurney answered, with a sudden increase of interest. “You don’t mean the crate you was speaking about that day you was up at the works?”

===Chinese===

「这事俺一点儿没听说。」格尼答道，兴致陡然浓了。「您不是说您那天到厂里来问起过的那只板条箱吧？」

===Original===

“No other. Keep it to yourself and I’ll tell you about it.” French became deeply impressive. “That crate that I was enquiring about was sent from here to Swansea. There it was called for by a man who took it on a lorry to a place called Burry Port and threw it into the sea. A fisherman chanced to hook it and it was brought ashore more than a month later. And when it was opened the dead body of a man was found inside.”

===Chinese===

「正是它。别往外传，我讲给你听。」弗伦奇显出一副郑重其事的架势。「我打听得的那只板条箱，就是从这儿发往斯旺西的。在那儿有人提取了货，用卡车拉到一个叫伯里港的地方，扔进了海里。一个打鱼的碰巧钩着了它，一个多月后才弄上岸。开箱一看，里面装着一具男尸。」

===Original===

“Lord save us! I read in the noospaper about that there body being found, but it fair beats me that the crate came from ’ere, it does.”

===Chinese===

「老天爷保佑！捞着死尸那事儿，俺在报上瞧见了，可这箱子竟是从俺们这儿出去的，真把俺闹糊涂了，真是。」

===Original===

French continued to enlarge on the tale. That Gurney’s surprise was genuine he felt certain. He could have sworn that the man had no inkling of the truth. But he marked, even more acutely than before, a hesitation or self-consciousness that indicated an uneasy mind. There was something; he felt sure of it. He glanced at the man with his shrewd, observant eyes and suddenly determined on directness.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇接着把这桩事添枝加叶地铺陈下去。格尼的惊讶是出于真心，这一点他笃定。他可以起誓，这人对真相毫不知情。可是他比先前更敏锐地察出一丝迟疑，或者说扭捏，透出心里不踏实。里头有事；他对此有数。他用那双精明善察的眼睛瞥了瞥这人，蓦地打定了开门见山的主意。

===Original===

“Look here, Gurney,” he said. “Come over and sit on this seat. I have something important to say to you.” He paused as if considering his words. “You thought a good deal of your employers, those two poor men who were lost on the moor?”

===Chinese===

「听着，格尼。」他说。「过来，坐这条凳上。我有要紧话对你说。」他顿了顿，像在斟酌措辞。「你对你的两位东家很有感情——荒沼上遇难的那两位可怜人？」

===Original===

“An’ I had reason to. It wasn’t an accident ’appening in the execution of my dooty, as you might say, as made me lame and not fit to work. It was rheumatism, and they could ’ave let me go when I couldn’t work no more. But they found this job for me and they let me the ’ouse cheap. Of course it was Mr. Berlyn as ’ad the final say, but I know as Mr. Pyke spoke for me. It wasn’t everyone as would ’ave done that, now was it, sir?”

===Chinese===

「俺有理由啊。您或许会说，俺这条腿残了、干不得活了，不是当班当差出的事故闹的——是风湿闹的。俺干不动了，他们本可以把俺打发走。可他们给俺寻了这个差事，这房子也便宜租给俺。当然，最后是伯林先生点的头，可俺知道派克先生替俺说过话。换了别人，可不一定肯这么办，是不是，先生？」

===Original===

Consideration on the part of an employer was not, French knew, to be taken as a matter of course, though it was vastly more common than the unions would have the public believe. But gratitude on the part of an employee was not so frequent, though it was by no means unique. Its exhibition, however, in the present instance confirmed French in the course he was taking.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇知道，东家体恤伙计，并非理所当然的事，尽管这也远比工会想让公众相信的普遍得多。而伙计感念东家，倒不多见，却也绝非绝无仅有。不过此番这番情的流露，叫弗伦奇对自己正走的这步棋更有了底气。

===Original===

“Now, Gurney, do you know who I am?” he went on. “I’m an inspector from Scotland Yard and I’m down here to try to solve these two mysteries. Because, Gurney, do you know what I think? I think that on that night the body of one of these two gentlemen was taken to the works and put into the crate.”

===Chinese===

「呃，格尼，你知道我是谁吗？」他接着说。「我是苏格兰场来的探长，到这儿来就是为了破这两桩疑案。因为，格尼，你知道我是怎么想的吗？我想那天夜里，这两位先生当中有一位的尸首，就是被运进工厂、装进那只板条箱里的。」

===Original===

Gurney started and paled. “Lord save us!” he muttered. “But wot about the accident?”

===Chinese===

格尼猛地一惊，脸色发白。「老天爷保佑！」他喃喃道。「那……那事故呢？」

===Original===

“There was no accident,” French replied, sternly. “There was murder. Who committed it, I don’t know at present. Where the other body is, if there is another body, I don’t yet know. But I have no doubt about one of the bodies. It was put into the crate on that night.”

===Chinese===

「没有什么事故。」弗伦奇声色俱厉地答道。「是凶杀。是谁下的手，我眼下还不知道。另一具尸首在哪儿——倘若真有另一具——我也还不知道。但其中一具，我毫不怀疑：那天夜里，它被装进了那只板条箱。」

===Original===

Gurney moistened his dry lips.

===Chinese===

格尼舔了舔发干的嘴唇。

===Original===

“But﻿—” he began, and his voice died away into silence.

===Chinese===

「可是——」他刚起头，声音便低下去，没了。

===Original===

“That’s it,” French went on, impressively. “Now, Gurney, I’m not accusing you of anything. But you know something. You needn’t attempt to deny it, because it has been plain to me from the first moment I spoke to you. Come now. Something out of the common took place that night. What was it?”

===Chinese===

「问题就在这儿。」弗伦奇加重了语气，接着说。「呃，格尼，我没有指控你的意思。可你知道点什么。不必打算抵赖，因为我头一眼跟你搭话时就看得明明白白。来吧。那天夜里出了桩异乎寻常的事。是什么？」

===Original===

Gurney did not deny the charge. Instead he sat motionless, with scared, unhappy eyes. French remained silent also; then he said, quietly:

===Chinese===

格尼没有否认这层指责。他只是坐着不动，眼里又惊惶又愁苦。弗伦奇也不作声；随后他轻声说：

===Original===

“What was it? Were you away from your post that night?”

===Chinese===

「是什么？那天夜里你擅离过岗位？」

===Original===

“No, sir, not that. I was there all the time,” the other answered, earnestly. Again he paused, then with a sudden gesture he went on: “I didn’t know nothing about what you ’ave been saying, but I see now I must tell you everything, even if I gets the sack over it.”

===Chinese===

「不是，先生，不是那个。俺一直都在来着。」对方恳切地答道。他又顿了顿，随后猛一摆手，接着说：「您方才说的这些，俺一点儿不知道，可俺现在明白了，俺得把什么都告诉您，哪怕为这个丢了差事。」

===Original===

“You’ll not get the sack if I can help it,” French said, kindly, “but go on and tell me, all the same.”

===Chinese===

「只要我管得过来，就不会叫你丢差事。」弗伦奇和气地说，「不过你还是照样都讲给我听吧。」

===Original===

“Well, sir, I did that night wot I never did before nor since. I slept the ’ole night through. I sat down to eat my supper in the boiler-’ouse like I always does, an’ I didn’t remember nothing more till Peter Small ’e was standing there shaking me. ‘Wake up,’ ’e says; ‘you’re a nice sort of a night watchman, you are.’ ‘Lord,’ I says. ‘I never did nothing like that before,’ an’ I asks him not to say nothing about it. An’ ’e didn’t say nothing, nor I didn’t, neither. But now I suppose it’ll come out an’ I’ll get wot for about it.”

===Chinese===

「唉，先生，那天夜里，俺干了桩从前没干过、往后也没干过的事。俺一觉睡到了大天亮。俺照老样子在锅炉房坐下吃夜宵，再往后就啥也不记得了，直到彼得·斯莫尔（Peter Small）站在跟前摇晃俺。『醒醒，』他说；『你可真是个好样的夜班守卫，真是。』『老天，』俺说，『俺从来没那样过。』俺就求他别往外声张。他没说，俺也没提。可俺琢磨着，如今这事要兜出来了，俺少不了要挨收拾。」

===Original===

“Don’t you worry about that,” French said, heartily. “I’ll see you through. I’ll undertake to get Mr. Fogden to overlook this little irregularity on one condition. You must tell me everything that took place that night without exception. Go ahead now and let’s have the whole of it.”

===Chinese===

「这个你别愁。」弗伦奇热诚地说。「我保你过关。我可以担保让福格登先生不计较这桩小小的违规，但有一个条件：那天夜里的事，不论巨细，你必须毫无例外全告诉我。这就讲吧，一点不落，都倒出来。」

===Original===

The old man gazed at him in distress.

===Chinese===

老头儿苦着脸望着他。

===Original===

“But there weren’t naught else,” he protested. “I went to sleep, an’ that’s all. If there were anything else took place, w’y I didn’t see it.”

===Chinese===

「可再没别的事了呀。」他分辩道。「俺睡着了，就这么点事。真还出了别的什么，那俺也没瞧见哪。」

===Original===

“That’s all right. Now just answer my questions. Go back to when you left your house. What time was that?”

===Chinese===

「这就好。现在只管答我的问话。回到你出家门那会儿。几点出的门？」

===Original===

“The usual time, about twenty minutes to seven.”

===Chinese===

「老时辰，七点差二十分光景。」

===Original===

“You brought your supper with you?”

===Chinese===

「夜宵是随身带去的？」

===Original===

“Yes, sir.”

===Chinese===

「是，先生。」

===Original===

“Who prepared it?”

===Chinese===

「谁预备的？」

===Original===

“My wife.”

===Chinese===

「俺屋里的。」

===Original===

“Did you meet anyone on your way to the works?”

===Chinese===

「去工厂的路上碰见人没有？」

===Original===

“Well, I couldn’t rightly say. No one that I remember.”

===Chinese===

「唔，俺说不上来。记得的没有。」

===Original===

“No one could have got hold of your supper, anyway?”

===Chinese===

「不管怎么说，总归没人能沾过你的夜宵吧？」

===Original===

The man started. “You think it might ’ave been tampered with?” he queried. He thought for some moments, then shook his head. “No, sir, I’m afraid not. I don’t never let my basket out o’ my ’and till I gets to the boiler-’ouse.”

===Chinese===

这人一怔。「您是说，夜宵兴许叫人动过手脚？」他问道。他想了片刻，又摇摇头。「没有，先生，俺看不至于。没到锅炉房之前，俺从来不叫篮子离手。」

===Original===

“Very well. Now when you got to the boiler-house?”

===Chinese===

「很好。那么到了锅炉房以后呢？」

===Original===

“I put it where I always do, beside one o’ the boilers.”

===Chinese===

「俺搁在老地方了，一台锅炉旁边。」

===Original===

“And you left it there?”

===Chinese===

「就撂在那儿了？」

===Original===

“While I made my rounds, I did. But there wasn’t no one else in the works then.”

===Chinese===

「俺巡夜那阵子是撂在那儿的。可那会儿厂里没有别人。」

===Original===

“How do you know?”

===Chinese===

「你怎么知道没有？」

===Original===

Gurney hesitated. In the last resort he didn’t know. But he had not seen anyone and did not believe anyone had been there.

===Chinese===

格尼迟疑了。追到根子上，他并不知道。只是他没见着人，也不信有谁待在厂里。

===Original===

“But suppose someone had been hidden in the works,” French persisted. “He could have doctored your supper while you were on your rounds?”

===Chinese===

「可要是有人预先藏进了厂里呢？」弗伦奇紧追不舍。「你巡夜的时候，他不就能在你的夜宵上做手脚了？」

===Original===

“If there ’ad been ’e might,” the man admitted. “But I didn’t see no one.”

===Chinese===

「要有那么个人，兴许能。」这人认了。「可俺没瞧见谁。」

===Original===

“What time do you have your meal?”

===Chinese===

「你几时用饭？」

===Original===

Gurney, it appeared, had two meals during the night. Time hung heavy on his hands and the meals made a break. He had his dinner about six, started work at seven, and had his first meal about eleven. His second meal he had about three, and he was relieved at six.

===Chinese===

看来格尼夜里要吃两顿。长夜难熬，两顿饭好岔一岔。他六点前后吃正餐，七点上工，十一点上下吃头一顿，第二顿约在三点，六点便有人来接班。

===Original===

On the night in question he had his first meal at the usual time. Until then he had felt perfectly normal, but he had scarcely finished when he found himself growing overpoweringly sleepy, and the next thing he remembered was being wakened by the fireman at six the next morning.

===Chinese===

出事那晚，他照常在老时辰吃了头一顿。在那之前他浑身妥帖，毫无异样，可这一顿刚吃完，他就觉着困得撑不住，再往后记得的事，便是第二天早晨六点被司炉叫醒了。

===Original===

“It’s clear that your supper was doped,” French said. “Now think, did nothing in any way out of the common happen between six and eleven?”

===Chinese===

「明摆着，你的夜宵叫人下了药。」弗伦奇说。「好好想想，六点到十一点之间，就没出过一星半点不寻常的事？」

===Original===

Gurney began a denial, then stopped.

===Chinese===

格尼刚要否认，又止住了。

===Original===

“There were one thing,” he said, slowly, “but I don’t believe as ’ow it could have ’ad anything to do with it. A little before ten there were a ring at the office door. I went to open, but there weren’t no one there. I didn’t think naught of it, because children do ring sometimes just by way o’ mischief. But there weren’t no children there so far as I could see.”

===Chinese===

「倒有一桩事。」他慢吞吞地说，「可俺不信它能跟这扯上什么关系。十点差一点儿，办公室的门上有人拉铃。俺去开门，门外一个人也没有。俺没当回事，小孩子家有时候就爱拉个铃淘气。可俺能瞧见的，连小孩子的影儿都没有。」

===Original===

“How far is this door that you opened from the boiler-house?”

===Chinese===

「你开的这扇门，离锅炉房有多远？」

===Original===

“At t’other end o’ the building. Two ’undred yards, maybe.”

===Chinese===

「在厂房那头顶上。两百码上下吧。」

===Original===

“Is that the only door?”

===Chinese===

「就这一扇门？」

===Original===

“No, sir, there be a gate near the boilers for lorries, but people going to the office use the other.”

===Chinese===

「不是，先生，锅炉近旁还有一道大门走卡车，可上办公室的人走那一扇。」

===Original===

“Is the large gate locked at night?”

===Chinese===

「大门夜里上锁吗？」

===Original===

“Yes, sir.”

===Chinese===

「上，先生。」

===Original===

“Who keeps the key?”

===Chinese===

「钥匙归谁管？」

===Original===

“I do. There’s a key in the office that any o’ the gentlemen can get if they wants, but I carry one with me.”

===Chinese===

「归俺。办公室里也搁着一把，哪位先生要用就取，俺身上还随身带一把。」

===Original===

For some moments French sat thinking, then a fresh point struck him.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇坐着寻思了一阵，又想到一个新题目。

===Original===

“What did your supper consist of on that night?”

===Chinese===

「那天夜里你的夜宵都是些什么？」

===Original===

“Tea an’ bread an’ butter and a slice o’ meat. I have a can o’ tea. I leave it on the boiler and it keeps ’ot.”

===Chinese===

「茶，黄油面包，还有一片肉。俺有一罐茶，搁在锅炉上，一直温着。」

===Original===

“You mean that you don’t make your tea separately for each meal? You drink some out of the can at the first meal and finish what is left at the second?”

===Chinese===

「你是说，茶不是每顿现沏的？头一顿从罐里倒出一些喝，剩下的第二顿喝完？」

===Original===

“That’s right, sir.”

===Chinese===

「正是，先生。」

===Original===

“And the same with food?”

===Chinese===

「吃食也一样？」

===Original===

“Yes, sir.”

===Chinese===

「是，先生。」

===Original===

“Now on the night we’re talking about you had only one meal. You slept through the time of the second. What happened to the tea and food that was left over?”

===Chinese===

「这么说，咱们说的那天夜里你只吃了一顿，第二顿的时辰睡过去了。剩下的茶和吃食哪儿去了？」

===Original===

“We ’ad it for breakfast, my wife an’ I.”

===Chinese===

「俺跟俺屋里的当早饭吃了。」

===Original===

“That is what I wanted to get at. Now did either of you feel sleepy after breakfast?”

===Chinese===

「我要问的正是这个。那么你们俩吃过早饭，犯没犯困？」

===Original===

A mixture of admiration and wonder showed in the old man’s eyes.

===Chinese===

老头儿眼里露出又佩服又惊奇的神色。

===Original===

“Why, no, we didn’t, an’ that’s a fact,” he said in puzzled tones. “An’ we should ’ave if so be as wot you think is true.”

===Chinese===

「哟，没有，俺们没犯困，这是实打实的。」他纳闷地说。「要是您想的是那么回事，俺们本该犯困才是。」

===Original===

This looked like a snag, but French reminded himself that at the moment he was only getting information and his theorising could wait till later. He continued his questions, but without learning much more.

===Chinese===

这话听来像块绊脚石，但弗伦奇提醒自己，眼下他只是在搜集情况，理论推敲尽可放到后头。他接着盘问下去，只是再没问出多少东西。

===Original===

“Now, Gurney,” he said at last, “under no circumstances are you to mention what we have been speaking of﻿—not to your wife nor to Mr. Fogden nor to anyone. You understand?”

===Chinese===

「呃，格尼，」他末了说，「咱们今天谈的话，无论如何不许吐露半个字——不许对你屋里的说，不许对福格登先生说，对谁都不许。你明白？」

===Original===

“I understand, sir, right enough.”

===Chinese===

「俺明白，先生，明白得很。」

===Original===

“Very good. Now I’m anxious to go into this matter further, and I’ll call at the works tonight.”

===Chinese===

「很好。我急着把这桩事再往深里究一究，今夜就到厂里去。」

===Original===

“Right, sir. I’ll be on the lookout.”

===Chinese===

「好嘞，先生。俺候着您。」

===Original===

It was dark as French rang at the big gate of the works. Gurney soon appeared at the wicket and French followed him across the yard to the boiler-house, a distance of perhaps forty yards. It was a fair-sized shed, housing five Babcock & Wilcox water-tube boilers with mechanical stokers and the usual stoker engines and pumps. On a ledge of the warm brickwork near one of the ash openings stood the old man’s can of tea, and his basket of food was placed on the repair bench close by. French took in these details and then said:

===Chinese===

天已黑透，弗伦奇在工厂大门口拉了铃。格尼很快从边门里现身，弗伦奇跟着他穿过院子，走进锅炉房——这段路约莫四十码。那是座挺宽敞的棚屋，装着五台巴布科克-威尔科克斯（Babcock & Wilcox）水管锅炉，配着机械加煤机和常备的加煤引擎与水泵。温热的砖墙上、一处出灰口近旁的搁架上，立着老头儿那罐茶，饭篮就放在近边的修理台上。弗伦奇把这些细节一一看在眼里，然后说：

===Original===

“I want now to try an experiment. Will you lend me your key of the wicket. I will go out, lock the wicket behind me, and go round to the office door and ring. When you hear the ring you go and open. Repeat everything exactly as you did that night so as to get back here at the same time. In the meantime I shall let myself in again by the wicket and see if I should have time to dope your tea and get away again before you appear. You understand?”

===Chinese===

「这会儿我要做个试验。把你边门的钥匙借我一用。我出去，从外头锁上边门，绕到办公室门口拉铃。你听见铃响就去开门。把那天夜里的事分毫不差地重演一遍，好叫你回到这儿的时辰跟那天对得上。趁这工夫，我再从边门自己进来，看看在你露面之前，我来不来得及在你茶里下药、再脱身出去。你明白吗？」

===Original===

This program was carried out. French went out and rang at the office door, then ran round to the large gate, let himself in through the wicket, found the can of tea, opened it and counted ten, closed it and relocked the wicket. Then he began to time. Three minutes passed before Gurney appeared.

===Chinese===

这套程序照办了。弗伦奇出去，在办公室门上拉铃，随即绕回大门，从边门进来，找到茶罐，开盖数十个数，盖上，再把边门锁好。接着他开始计时。过了三分钟，格尼才露面。

===Original===

So that was all right. Anyone who had access to the key in the office could have doctored the watchman’s food. Moreover, the fact that the Gurneys had breakfasted without ill effect on the remainder was not such a difficulty as French had at first supposed. The criminal might have doped the tea on his first visit and during his second poured away what was over and replaced it with fresh. In fact, if he were to preserve his secret he must have done so. The discovery of the drugging would have started an enquiry which might have brought to light the whole plot.

===Chinese===

这样就说得通了。凡是拿得到办公室那把钥匙的人，都有可能在守卫的饭食里做手脚。再者，格尼两口子把剩下的当了早饭吃下去竟安然无恙，这一层也不像弗伦奇起初设想的那么难办。罪犯头一回来也许在茶里下了药，第二回来把剩下的倒掉，换上新茶。事实上，他要保住秘密，就必定这么干过。下药一事一旦败露，势必引发追查，整个阴谋都可能随之见光。

===Original===

Though French was enthusiastic about his discovery, he saw that it involved one disconcerting point. What about the theory of Berlyn’s guilt? The ring at the office door had come shortly before ten. But shortly before ten Berlyn was at Tavistock. Therefore some other person was involved. Was this person the murderer and had he made away with Berlyn as well as Pyke? Or was he Berlyn’s accomplice? French inclined to the latter supposition. In considering the timing of the car he had seen that it could have been used to carry the body to the works provided an accomplice was ready to drive it back to the moor without delay. On the whole, therefore, it looked as if the murder was the work of two persons, of whom Berlyn was one.

===Chinese===

这发现虽叫弗伦奇兴致勃勃，他也看出其中有一点叫人挠头。伯林有罪的那套推论怎么办？办公室门上的铃声是十点差一点儿响的。可十点差一点儿，伯林人在塔维斯托克。可见另有一人参与其事。此人是正凶，连伯林也同派克一道断送在他手里？还是他是伯林的同伙？弗伦奇倾向于后一种猜想。推算那辆车的行程时刻时他已看出，只要有个同伙随时待命、能把车即刻开回荒沼去，车尽可以用来把尸体运到工厂。因此总起来看，这桩凶案像是两人所为，其中一人便是伯林。

===Original===

But whether principal or accomplice, it was at least certain that the man who had drugged Gurney’s food knew the works intimately and had access to the key in the office. Only a comparatively small number of persons could fill these requirements and he should, therefore, be quickly found.

===Chinese===

但无论身为主犯还是从犯，在格尼饭食里下药的那个人，必是对工厂了如指掌、又拿得到办公室钥匙的人，这一点至少是笃定的。能同时满足这两条的人为数不多，因此他应该很快就能被找出来。

===Original===

Well pleased with his day’s work, French returned to the hotel and spent the remainder of the evening in writing up his diary.

===Chinese===

这一天的活计办得称心，弗伦奇回到旅馆，把当晚余下的工夫都用来写日记。

## XII The Duplicator / XII 复印机

===Original===

The saying “it never rains but what it pours” is a popular expression of the unhappy fact that misfortunes never come singly. Fortunately for suffering humanity, the phrase expresses only half the truth. Runs of good luck occur as well as runs of bad.

===Chinese===

俗话说「天不下则已，一下便是倾盆」，生动道出了那个令人丧气的事实：灾祸从不单独降临。所幸对受苦受难的人类而言，这话只说对了一半——好运也会接二连三地来，正如厄运一样。

===Original===

As French was smoking his after-breakfast pipe in the lounge next morning it was borne in on him that he was at that time experiencing one of the most phenomenal runs of good luck that had ever fallen to his lot. Four days ago he had proved that the dead man was Pyke. Two days later he had learned how the breakdown of the car had been faked. Yesterday he had found the explanation of the watchman’s inaction, and today, just at that very moment, an idea had occurred to him which bade fair to solve the problem of the disposal of the duplicator! Unfortunately, nothing could be done towards putting it to the test until the evening. He spent the day, therefore, in a long tramp on the moor, then about five o’clock walked for the second time to Gurney’s house.

===Chinese===

第二天早晨，弗伦奇在客厅里抽着饭后一斗烟，忽然醒悟：自己此刻正经历着平生罕有的一阵鸿运。四天前，他证实了死者正是派克；两天后，他弄清了汽车故障是如何伪造的；昨天，他解开了守卫不作为之谜；而今天，就在此时此刻，一个念头浮上心头，大有望解开复印机处置之谜！可惜，要检验这个念头，非等到晚上不可。于是他把白昼消磨在荒原上的一次长足漫游里，到五点来钟，第二次走向格尼家。

===Original===

“I want to have another chat with you,” he explained. “I haven’t time to wait now, but I shall come up to the works later in the evening. Listen out for my ring.”

===Chinese===

「我想再跟你聊聊。」他说明来意。「这会儿没空多耽搁，晚上晚些时候我再上厂里来。留神听我按铃。」

===Original===

He strolled back to the town, had a leisurely dinner, visited the local picture house, and killed time until after eleven. Then when the little town was asleep he went up to the works. Five minutes later he was seated with Gurney in the boiler-house.

===Chinese===

他踱回镇上，从容用了晚餐，去本地的电影院看了一场，把时光磨到十一点以后。待小镇睡熟，他才上工厂去。五分钟后，他已同格尼一起坐在锅炉房里了。

===Original===

“I have been thinking over this affair, Gurney,” he began, “and I am more than ever certain that some terrible deeds were done here on that night when you were drugged. I want to have another look around. But you must not under any circumstances let it be known that I was here.”

===Chinese===

「格尼，这案子我又翻来覆去想了一遍，」他开口道，「如今比先前更确信：在你被药倒的那天夜里，这厂里干下了骇人的勾当。我要再查看一番。可无论如何，你决不能让人知道我来过这儿。」

===Original===

“That’s all right, guv’nor. I ain’t goin’ to say nothing.”

===Chinese===

「放心吧，长官。俺啥也不往外说。」

===Original===

French nodded.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇点点头。

===Original===

“You told me that you had been a mechanic in the works before your rheumatism got bad. Have you worked at any of those duplicators like what was packed in the crate?”

===Chinese===

「你跟我说过，风湿闹重之前，你是厂里的机工。像装进板条箱的那台复印机，你经手装配过没有？」

===Original===

“I worked at all kinds of erecting works﻿—duplicators an’ files an’ indexes an’ addressing machines an’ all the rest o’ them. I knows them all.”

===Chinese===

「俺各式装配活计都干过——复印机呀、文件柜呀、索引柜呀、写地址机呀，还有其余那些名堂。俺全懂。」

===Original===

“Good! Now I want you to come round to the store and show me the different parts of a duplicator.”

===Chinese===

「好！这就请你带我到库房去，把复印机的各个部件指给我看。」

===Original===

Gurney led the way from the boiler-house.

===Chinese===

格尼领路出了锅炉房。

===Original===

“Don’t switch on the light,” French directed. “I don’t want the windows to show lit up. I have a torch.”

===Chinese===

「别开灯。」弗伦奇吩咐道。「我不想让窗户透出亮光。我带了手电。」

===Original===

They passed through the packing-shed and into the completed-machine store adjoining. Here French called a halt.

===Chinese===

两人穿过装箱棚，走进紧连着的成品库。到这儿弗伦奇叫停。

===Original===

“Just let’s look at one of these duplicators again,” he said. “Suppose you wanted to take one of them to pieces, let me see how you would set about it. Should I be correct in saying that if five or six of the larger pieces were got rid of, all the rest could be carried in a handbag?”

===Chinese===

「咱们再来看看这些复印机里的一台。」他说。「假定你要把其中一台拆开，做给我瞧瞧你怎么下手。要是我说，先把五六件大件打发掉，其余的部件就能装进一只手提包拎走——我这么说对不对？」

===Original===

“That’s right, sir.”

===Chinese===

「一点不错，先生。」

===Original===

“Now show me the bins where these larger parts are stocked.”

===Chinese===

「那就带我瞧瞧这些大件都存放在哪些料箱里。」

===Original===

They passed on to the part store and across it to a line of bins labelled “Duplicators.” In the first bin were rows of leg castings. French ran his eye along them.

===Chinese===

两人接着进零件库，穿过库房，来到一排标着「Duplicators」（复印机）字样的料箱前。头一只箱子里码着一排排支脚铸件。弗伦奇的目光顺着扫过去。

===Original===

“There must be fifty or sixty here,” he said, slowly. “Let’s see if that is a good guess.”

===Chinese===

「这里头怕有五六十件。」他慢慢地说。「来看看我猜得准不准。」

===Original===

On every bin was a stock card in a metal holder. French lifted down that in question. It was divided into three sets of columns, one set showing incomes, the second outgoes, and the third the existing stock. The date of each transaction was given, and for each entry the stock was adjusted.

===Chinese===

每只料箱上都用金属卡槽插着一张库存卡。弗伦奇取下眼下这张。卡片分作三组栏：一组记入库，一组记出库，一组记结存。每笔往来都注明日期，记上一笔，库存数随之核算一次。

===Original===

“Not such a bad guess,” French remarked, slowly, as he scrutinised the entries. “There are just fifty-four.”

===Chinese===

「猜得不算离谱。」弗伦奇细看那些条目，慢悠悠地说。「不多不少，整五十四件。」

===Original===

The card was large and was nearly full. French noticed that it went back for some weeks before the tragedy. He stood gazing at it in the light of his torch while a feeling of bitter disappointment grew in his mind. Then suddenly he thought he saw what he was looking for, and whipping out a lens, he examined one of the entries more closely. “Got it, by Jove! I’ve actually got it!” he thought, delightedly. His luck had held.

===Chinese===

这张卡片很大，快记满了。弗伦奇注意到，上面的记录往回追溯了好几个星期，早于惨案之前。他站在那儿，借着手电的光盯着卡片，心里那股苦苦的失望一阵浓似一阵。忽然，他仿佛瞥见了要找的东西，急忙抽出放大镜，凑近去细看其中一条。「到手了，老天作证！真叫我到手了！」他喜不自胜地想。他的好运仍未见底。

===Original===

One of the entries had been altered. A loop had been skilfully added to a six to make it an eight. The card showed that two castings had been taken out which either had never been taken out at all or, more probably, which had been taken out and afterwards replaced.

===Chinese===

其中一条记录被人改过。一个「6」被巧妙地添上一钩，成了「8」。照卡片所记，有两件铸件发了出去；而这两件，要么压根没发出去过，要么——多半如此——发出去之后又还了回来。

===Original===

Convinced that he had solved the last of his four test problems, French examined the cards of the other bins. In all of those referring to large parts he noticed the same peculiarity; the entries had been tampered with to show that one more duplicator had been sent out than really was the case.
