The cards for the small parts were unaltered and French could understand the reason. It was easier to get rid of the parts themselves than to falsify their records. The fraud was necessary only in the case of objects too big and heavy to carry away.

===Chinese===

确信四个验证难题的末一关也已攻破，弗伦奇接着查验其余料箱的卡片。凡涉及大件的那几张，他都发现了同样的蹊跷：条目全被做过手脚，多显示发出了一台复印机，实情却非如此。小件的卡片原封未动，缘故弗伦奇也明白：把零件本身处理掉，比篡改它们的账目容易。惟有那些大得重得搬不走的物件，才有作假的必要。

===Original===

French was highly pleased. His discovery was not only valuable in itself, but he had reached it in the way which most appealed to his vanity﻿—from his own imagination. He had imagined that the fraud might have been worked in this way. He had tested it and found that it had been. Pure brains! Such things were soothing to his self-respect.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇大为快慰。这一发现本身就已可贵，而抵达它的路径更投合他的虚荣——全凭他自己的头脑。他曾猜想骗局可能是这么运作的；一经检验，果然如此。纯凭脑力！这样的时刻，最能让他的自尊得到抚慰。

===Original===

He stood considering the matter. The evidence was valuable, but it was far from permanent. A hint that suspicion was aroused, and it would be gone. The criminal, if he were still about, would see to it that innocuous copies of the cards were substituted for these dangerous ones. French felt he dare not run such a risk. Nor could he let Gurney suspect his discovery, lest unwittingly the old man might put the criminal on his guard. He therefore went on:

===Chinese===

他站着把这事掂量了一番。这证据固然值钱，却远非一劳永逸。只要透出一点风声，说疑云已起，它便会烟消云散。那罪犯倘若还在左近，必定设法拿几张无害的卡片把这些惹祸的换掉。弗伦奇觉得这个险冒不得。他也决不能让格尼嗅出他的发现，怕的是老头儿一个不小心，反叫罪犯起了防备。于是他接着说道：

===Original===

“Now all I want is to make a sketch of each of these parts. The duplicator which went out in the crate may have been taken to pieces and I want to be able to recognise them if they’re found. I suppose I could get a sheet or two of paper in the storeman’s desk?”

===Chinese===

「如今我只想把这些部件一样样画个草图。装箱运走的那台复印机兴许已被拆散，日后部件若是寻着了，我要能认出它们来。库管员的写字台里，想必找得出一两张纸吧？」

===Original===

In one corner a small box with glass sides constituted an office for the storeman. French led the way thither. The door was closed but not locked. The desk, which he next tried, was fastened. But above it in a rack he saw what he was looking for, a pile of blank bin-cards. He turned back.

===Chinese===

库房一角有个四面玻璃的小隔间，权当库管员的办公室。弗伦奇领路走过去。门关着，却没上锁。他接着去试那张写字台，台子锁死了。可台子上方的架子里，正有他要找的东西——一叠空白库存卡。他转身折回。

===Original===

“It doesn’t matter about the paper, after all,” he explained. “I see the desk is locked. I can make my sketches in my notebook, though it’s not so convenient. But many a sketch I’ve made in it before.”

===Chinese===

「纸的事到底不碍了。」他解释道。「我瞧见写字台上了锁。草图我可以画在笔记本上，虽说不那么便当。不过从前在里头画过的草图，也不止一张两张了。」

===Original===

Chatting pleasantly, he returned to the bins and began slowly to sketch the leg casting. He was purposely extremely slow and detailed in the work, measuring every possible dimension and noting it on his sketch. Gurney, as he had hoped, began to get fidgety. French continued talking and sketching. Suddenly he looked up.

===Chinese===

他一边闲谈着，一边回到料箱跟前，开始慢慢画那件支脚铸件。他故意做得极慢极细，凡量得出的尺寸逐一量过，记上草图。不出所望，格尼渐渐烦躁起来。弗伦奇只管又说又画。忽然他抬起头来。

===Original===

“By the way,” he said, as if a new idea had suddenly entered his mind, “there is no earthly need for me to keep you here while I am working. It will take me an hour or two to finish these sketches. If you want to do your rounds and to get your supper, go ahead. I’ll find you in the boiler-house when I have done.”

===Chinese===

「对了，」他说，像是心里蓦地冒出个新念头，「我干活用不着把你拴在这儿。这些草图总得画一两个钟头。你要巡夜、要吃夜宵，只管去。我画完了到锅炉房找你。」

===Original===

Gurney seemed relieved. He explained that it really was time to make his rounds and that if French didn’t mind he would go and do so. French reassured him heartily, and he slowly disappeared.

===Chinese===

格尼像是松了口气。他说眼下确是该去巡夜的时候了，弗伦奇若不介意，他就去转他的。弗伦奇热诚地叫他放心，老头儿便慢吞吞地去了。

===Original===

No sooner had his shuffling footsteps died away than French became an extremely active man. Quickly slipping the four faked cards from their metal holders, he carried them to the office. Then taking four fresh cards from the rack, he began slowly and carefully to copy the others. He was not a skilful forger, but at the end of half an hour’s work he had produced four passable imitations. Two minutes later he breathed more freely. The copies were in the holders and the genuine cards in his pocket. Hurriedly he resumed his sketching.

===Chinese===

老头儿那双趿拉的脚步声刚刚消逝，弗伦奇立时成了个身手极其麻利的人。他飞快从卡槽里抽出那四张动过手脚的卡片，拿进办公室；再从架上取四张新卡，开始慢而细心地誊摹那几张。他算不得造假的好手，可半个钟头下来，也摹成了四张过得去的仿件。两分钟后，他呼吸畅快了：仿卡插回了卡槽，真卡进了他的口袋。他赶忙重新画起图来。

===Original===

French’s work amounted to genius in the infinite pains he took with detail. In twenty minutes his sketches were complete and he effectually banished any suspicion which his actions might have aroused in Gurney’s mind by showing them to him when he rejoined him in the boiler-house. Like an artist he proceeded to establish the deception.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇办事，对细节肯下无穷的苦功，直追天才二字。二十分钟后草图告成；回到锅炉房与格尼会合时，他把草图递给老头儿过目，把自己方才的举动可能勾起的一星半点疑心，也一并消解干净。接着他像个老艺人似的，着手把戏法做圆。

===Original===

“Copies of these sketches sent to the men who are searching for the duplicator will help them to recognise parts of it if it has been taken to pieces,” he explained. “You see the idea?”

===Chinese===

「这些草图誊送一份给搜寻那台复印机的人，机器若是已被拆散，他们也好认出部件。」他解释道。「你明白这层用意吧？」

===Original===

Gurney appreciated the point, and French, after again warning him to be circumspect, left the works.

===Chinese===

格尼会意。弗伦奇再次叮嘱他行事谨慎，随后离开工厂。

===Original===

The problem of what he should do next was solved for French by the receipt of a letter by the early post. It was written on a half sheet of cheap notepaper in an uneducated hand and read:

===Chinese===

接下来做什么——这个难题，由头一班邮差送来的一封信替弗伦奇解了。信写在半张廉价信纸上，一笔没受过多少教养的字，全文如下：

===Original===

Ashburton.

===Chinese===

阿什伯顿。

===Original===

					12th October.

===Chinese===

					10月12日。

===Original===

Dear Sir,

===Chinese===

亲爱的先生：

===Original===

If you would come round some time that suits you I have something I could tell you that would maybe interest you. It’s better not wrote about.

===Chinese===

您要是能得空过来一趟，日子拣您方便的，我有件事可以告诉您，兴许您会有兴趣。这种事还是不写在纸上的好。

===Original===

Lizzie Johnston.

===Chinese===

莉齐·约翰斯顿。

===Original===

French had received too many communications of the kind to be hopeful that this one would result in anything valuable. However, he thought he ought to see the ex-parlourmaid and once again he made his way to her cottage.

===Chinese===

这类来信弗伦奇见得太多，不指望这一封能问出什么有价值的东西。不过他想，那位从前在伯林家当差的女仆，自己总该见上一见，于是又一路朝她的村舍走去。

===Original===

“It’s my Alf,” she explained. “Alf Beer, they call him. We’re being married as soon as he gets another job.”

===Chinese===

「是我的阿尔夫。」她解释道。「人家都叫他阿尔夫·比尔（Alf Beer）。等他寻着新差事，我们就成亲。」

===Original===

“He’s out of a job, then?”

===Chinese===

「这么说，他眼下失着业？」

===Original===

“Yes, he was in the sales department in the works; a packer, he was. He left there six months ago.”

===Chinese===

「是的，他先前在厂里销售部做事，是个装箱工。六个月前离开的。」

===Original===

“How was that?” French asked, sympathetically.

===Chinese===

「那是为了什么呢？」弗伦奇语带关切地问。

===Original===

“He wasn’t well and he stayed home a few mornings, and Mr. Berlyn had him up in his office and spoke to him something wicked. Well, Alf wouldn’t take that, not from no man living, so he said what relieved his feelings and Mr. Berlyn told him he could go.”

===Chinese===

「他身子不舒坦，有几天早上歇在了家里，伯林先生就把他叫上办公室，劈头盖脸骂了个歹毒。阿尔夫哪吃这一套——天底下随便哪个活人他都不受这气——就痛快回敬了几句出口气，伯林先生便叫他卷铺盖走人。」

===Original===

“And has he been doing nothing since?”

===Chinese===

「打那以后他就一直没活儿干？」

===Original===

“Not steady, he hasn’t. Just jobbing, as you might say.”

===Chinese===

「长活儿是没有。只能说东一天西一天，打个零工。」

===Original===

“Hard lines, that is. You say he had something to tell me?”

===Chinese===

「那可够倒霉的。你是说他有话要告诉我？」

===Original===

The girl nodded. “That’s right,” was her original reply.

===Chinese===

姑娘点点头。「可不。」她这一句回答，倒真是别出心裁。

===Original===

“What is it, do you know?”

===Chinese===

「什么事，你知道吗？」

===Original===

“He wouldn’t say. I told him you was in asking questions and he seemed sort of interested. ‘Wants to know about Berlyn and Pyke and Mrs. Berlyn’s goings-on with Pyke, does he?’ he sez. ‘I thought someone would be wanting to know about that before long. Well, I can tell him something,’ he sez.”

===Chinese===

「他不肯说。我告诉他，您正在四处问话，他好像来了点兴致。『想打听伯林和派克、还有伯林太太跟派克那档子事，是吧？』他说。『我早想着，过不了多久就该有人来打听这个了。哼，我能告诉他点东西。』他说。

===Original===

“But he didn’t mention what it was?”

===Chinese===

「可他没提是什么事？」

===Original===

“No. I asked him and he sez ‘Value for cash,’ he sez. ‘He puts down the beans and I cough up the stuff. That’s fair, ain’t it?’ he sez. ‘Don’t be a silly guff, Alf,’ I sez. ‘He’s police and if he asks you questions, why, you don’t half have to answer them.’ ‘The devil I have,’ he sez. ‘I ain’t done no crime and he hasn’t nothing on me. You tell him,’ he sez, ‘tell him I know something that would be worth a quid or two to him.’ And so I wrote you that note.”

===Chinese===

「没提。我问他，他就说：『钱货两讫。』他说。『他拍下钱来，我就倒出干货。这才叫公平，不是吗？』他说。『别犯傻了，阿尔夫。』我说。『人家是警察，他要问你话，你可不得不乖乖回答。』『去他的。』他说。『我又没犯什么罪，他抓不着我半点把柄。你告诉他，』他说，『告诉他我知道点事儿，对他至少值一两个英镑。』我就给您写了那张便条。」

===Original===

“Tell me why you thought I was police,” French invited.

===Chinese===

「跟我说说，你怎么想到我是警察的？」弗伦奇循循引她。

===Original===

Miss Johnston laughed scornfully.

===Chinese===

约翰斯顿小姐轻蔑地笑了。

===Original===

“Well, ain’t you?” she parried.

===Chinese===

「哼，您不是吗？」她把话头一挡。

===Original===

“That’s hardly an answer to my question.”

===Chinese===

「这可算不得回答了我的问题。」

===Original===

“Well, everybody knows what you’re after. They say you think Pyke was murdered on the moor and that Berlyn murdered him. Leastways, that’s what I’ve heard said.”

===Chinese===

「这个嘛，您来干什么，全镇谁人不知。人家都说，您认定派克是在荒原上被人谋害的，下手的就是伯林。反正人家是这么传的，我是这么听来的。」

===Original===

This was something more than a blow to French, and his self-esteem reeled under it. For the *n*th time he marvelled at the amazing knowledge of other people’s business to be found in country districts. The small country town, he thought, was the absolute limit! There he was, moving continually among the townspeople, none of whom gave the least sign of interest in his calling, yet evidently they had discussed him and his affairs to some purpose. The garrulous landlady, Mrs. Billing, was no doubt responsible for the murder of Pyke becoming known, but the belief that he, French, suspected Berlyn of murdering him was really rather wonderful.

===Chinese===

这对弗伦奇不止是当头一棒，直打得他自尊心一阵摇晃。乡下地方对旁人私事的那种惊人消息灵通，他已是第 *n* 次为之惊叹。小乡镇，他想，真算得上登峰造极！他整日在镇民中间来来往往，没有谁对他的行当露过一丝感兴趣的迹象，可他们分明早把他和他的事议论了个透，还议出了结果。派克死于谋杀一事传扬开去，多半要归功于那位多嘴的房东太太比林太太；可他们竟认定他弗伦奇疑心伯林是凶手，这可真真了不得。

===Original===

“It seems to me,” he said with a rather sickly smile, “that your townspeople are better detectives than ever came out of Scotland Yard. So your young man thinks I’m police and wants to turn an honest penny, does he? Where am I to find him?”

===Chinese===

「依我看，」他挂着一副颇为难堪的笑，说道，「你们镇上人当侦探，苏格兰场出来的哪一个也比不了。这么说，你那位未婚夫认定我是警察，想挣几个干净钱，是不是？我上哪儿找他去？」

===Original===

“He’ll be at home. He’s living with his father at the head of East Street﻿—a single red house on the left-hand side just beyond the town.”

===Chinese===

「他这会儿准在家。他跟他爹住在东街街口——出了镇子没多远，左手边一栋孤零零的红房子。」

===Original===

In the leisurely, holiday-like way he had adopted, French crossed the town and half an hour later had introduced himself to Mr. Alfred Beer. Lizzie’s Alf was a stalwart young man with a heavy face and a sullen, discontented expression. French, sizing him up rapidly, decided that the suave method would scarcely meet the case.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇仍以他那副闲适的度假做派穿过镇子，半小时后，已同阿尔弗雷德·比尔先生（Mr. Alfred Beer）互通了姓名。莉齐的这位阿尔夫是个结实的青年，脸盘粗重，神色阴沉不忿。弗伦奇飞快把他掂量了一遍，断定怀柔一路对这人怕是不济事。

===Original===

“You are Alfred Beer, engaged to Lizzie Johnston, the former servant at Mr. Berlyn’s?” he began.

===Chinese===

「你是阿尔弗雷德·比尔，同伯林先生家从前的女仆莉齐·约翰斯顿订了婚的那位？」他开口道。

===Original===

“That’s right, mister.”

===Chinese===

「正是，先生。」

===Original===

“I am a police officer investigating the deaths of Mr. Berlyn and Mr. Pyke. You have some information for me?”

===Chinese===

「我是警务人员，正在调查伯林先生和派克先生的死亡案。你有点情况要向我交代？」

===Original===

“I don’t altogether know that,” Beer answered, slowly. “Just wot did you want to know?”

===Chinese===

「这我可想不准。」比尔慢吞吞地答道。「您到底想打听什么？」

===Original===

“What you have to tell me,” French said, sharply. “You told Miss Johnston you had some information and I’ve come up to hear it.”

===Chinese===

「就是你有话要讲的那件事。」弗伦奇厉声说。「你对约翰斯顿小姐说你握着情况，我正是上这儿来听它的。」

===Original===

The man looked at him calculatingly.

===Chinese===

那人打量着他，心里的算盘拨得山响。

===Original===

“Wot do you think it might be worth to you?” he queried.

===Chinese===

「您估摸着，这能值您几个钱？」他探问。

===Original===

“Not a brass farthing. You should know that witnesses are not paid for their evidence. Don’t you misunderstand the situation, Beer, or you’ll find things mighty unpleasant. Come along now. Out with it.”

===Chinese===

「一个铜子儿也不值。你该知道，证人作证是不收钱的。别把处境掂量岔了，比尔，不然有你好受的。行了，痛快交代。」

===Original===

“How can I tell you if you won’t say wot you want?”

===Chinese===

「您不说想听什么，我怎么知道该讲什么？」

===Original===

“I wouldn’t talk to you any more, Beer, only, I think you don’t understand where you are,” French answered, quietly. “This is a murder case. Mr. Pyke has been murdered. If you know anything that might help the police to discover the murderer and you don’t tell it, you become an accessory after the fact. Do you realise that you’d get a good spell of years for that?”

===Chinese===

「比尔，要不是看你还没弄明白自己的处境，我犯不上再跟你多费唇舌。」弗伦奇不紧不慢地答道。「这是桩谋杀案。派克先生是被人谋害的。你要是知道什么有助于警方缉拿凶手的线索却知情不举，你就是事后从犯。你可明白，单这一条就够你蹲上好些年？」

===Original===

Beer gave an uncouth shrug and turned back to his digging.

===Chinese===

比尔粗野地耸了耸肩，转身接着刨他的土。

===Original===

“I don’t know nothing about no murder,” he declared, contemptuously. “I was just pulling Lizzie’s leg.”

===Chinese===

「什么谋杀不谋杀的，我什么都不知道。」他不屑地声言。「我不过是逗莉齐玩儿。」

===Original===

“You’ve done it now,” French said, producing his card. “There’s my authority as a police officer. You’ve wasted my time and kept me back from my work. That’s obstruction and you’ll get six months for it. Come along to the station. And unless you want a couple of years you’ll come quietly.”

===Chinese===

「这下你可把自己搭进去了。」弗伦奇说着，掏出证件。「这就是我警务身份的凭证。你浪费了我的时间，耽误了我的正事。这叫妨碍公务，够你蹲六个月。跟我去警局。除非你想再添上两年，不然就乖乖地走。」

===Original===

This was not what the man expected.

===Chinese===

这一招大出那人意料。

===Original===

“Wot’s that?” he stammered. “You ain’t going to arrest me? I ain’t done nothin’ against the law, I ain’t.”

===Chinese===

「什……什么？」他结巴起来。「您不是要抓我吧？我没犯法，真没犯。」

===Original===

“You’ll soon find out about that. Look sharp now. I can’t spend the day here waiting for you.”

===Chinese===

「你有得明白的。麻利点。我可不能在这儿站上一天等你。」

===Original===

“Aw!” The man shifted nervously. “See, mister, I ain’t done no harm, I ain’t. I don’t know nothing about no murder. I don’t, honest.”

===Chinese===

「嗐！」那人局促地挪了挪。「您听我说，先生，我没干过坏事，真没有。什么谋杀我一概不知。真的，不骗您。」

===Original===

“I don’t want to be hard on you,” French answered. “If you tell your story without any more humbugging I’ll let the rest go. But I warn you, you needn’t start inventing any yarn. What you say will be gone into, and Heaven help you if it’s not true.”

===Chinese===

「我并不想难为你。」弗伦奇答道。「你要是不再耍花腔，痛痛快快把话讲完，其余的一概不究。可我把丑话说在前头，你不必打算现编一套瞎话。你讲的每一句都要查对，若有一字不实，到时老天爷也救不了你。」

===Original===

“I’ll take my davy it’s true, mister, but it ain’t about no murder.”

===Chinese===

「这话千真万确，我敢对天起誓，先生，可它跟谋杀不沾边。」

===Original===

“Well, get along sharp and let’s hear it.”

===Chinese===

「那就快点，说来听。」

===Original===

“It was one night about six months ago,” said Beer, now speaking almost eagerly. “Me and Lizzie were walking out at that time. Well, that night we’d fixed up for to go for a walk, and then at the last minute she couldn’t get away. Mrs. Berlyn was goin’ out or somethin’, and she couldn’t get off. We’d ’ad it fixed up that when that ’appened Lizzie would come down to the shrubbery after the rest ’ad gone to bed. Well, I wanted to see ’er that night for to fix up some little business between ourselves, so I went up to the ’ouse and gave the sign﻿—three taps with a tree branch at ’er window. You understand?”

===Chinese===

「大约六个月前的一个晚上，」比尔说，这会儿他讲得几乎带劲起来。「那阵子我跟莉齐正处对象。那天夜里我们约好了出去散步，可到末了她脱不开身——伯林太太要出门还是怎么的，她告不了假。我们原先讲定，赶上这种时候，等其余人都上了床，莉齐就下楼到灌木林子里来。偏那晚我非要见她不可，我俩有桩小私事要当面定夺，我就上那宅子打了个信号——拿根树枝在她窗上敲三下。您明白吗？」

===Original===

French nodded.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇点头。

===Original===

“Well, I went back into the shrubbery for to wait for her. It was dark, but a quiet night. An’ then I ’eard voices an’ steps comin’ along the path. So I got behind a bush so as they’d not see me. There was a man and a woman, an’ when they came close I knew them by their voices. It was Pyke and Mrs. Berlyn. I stayed still an’ they passed me close.”

===Chinese===

「于是我就退回灌木林子里等她。天黑着，夜倒安静。跟着我听见小径上有说话声和脚步声过来，就闪到一丛灌木后头，免得叫他们瞧见。来的是一男一女，走近了，我听嗓音就认出了他们——是派克和伯林太太。我待着没动，他们贴着我身边走了过去。」

===Original===

“Go ahead. Did you hear what they said, or what are you getting at?”

===Chinese===

「往下讲。你是听见了他们说的话，还是要说别的什么？」

===Original===

“I ’eard wot they said when they were passing. ‘I tell you ’e knows,’ she said. ‘I’m frightened,’ she said. ‘You don’t know him. If ’e once thinks you’ve played ’im false ’e’ll make a ’ell of a trouble.’ An’ then Pyke says: ‘Nonsense!’ ’e said. ‘ ’E’s not that sort. Besides,’ ’e said, ‘ ’e don’t know anything. ’E knows we’re friends, but that’s all.’ ‘No,’ she said, ‘I’m sure ’e knows or ’e guesses, anyway. We’ll ’ave to separate,’ she said. ’E said they ’ad been careful enough, and then they went past an’ I didn’t ’ear no more.”

===Chinese===

「他们走过我跟前时说的话，我听见了。『我跟你说，他知道。』她说。『我心里害怕。』她说。『你不了解他。他但凡疑心你对他不忠，那可要闹出天大的乱子。』派克就说：『胡说！』他说。『他不是那种人。再说，』他说，『他什么也不知道。他知道咱们是朋友，仅此而已。』『不，』她说，『我敢说他知道，要不也猜着了。咱们只好分开。』她说。他说他们俩向来够小心，跟着他们就走过去了，往后的我就没听着了。」

===Original===

“That all?”

===Chinese===

「就这些？」

===Original===

“That’s all,” said Beer, disgustedly. “Ain’t it enough?”

===Chinese===

「就这些。」比尔怏怏地说。「这还不够？」

===Original===

“Nothing to boast about,” French replied, absently. He remembered that the man had been dismissed by Berlyn and he wondered if this statement was merely the result of spite. He therefore questioned him closely. But he was unable to shake him and he formed the opinion that the story was true.

===Chinese===

「没什么可夸口的。」弗伦奇心不在焉地答道。他记得这人叫伯林辞退过，心里犯嘀咕：这番话莫不是挟怨泄愤的编派？于是他细细盘问了一遍，却问不出破绽，他由此认定这故事属实。

===Original===

If so, it certainly had a pretty direct bearing on the theory he was trying to evolve, for there could be little doubt as to who “ ’e” was. As he considered the matter he was surprised to find how complete that theory was and how much of it had been definitely established. There were gaps, of course, but there was no doubt as to its general correctness.

===Chinese===

若果真如此，这话同他正着力构筑的那套推断，可就有相当直接的干系了，因为话里那个「他」指的是谁，几乎不容置疑。他把这事掂来掂去，惊讶地发觉这套推断已然何等完整，其中又有多少环节已被坐实。缺口自然还有，但大方向错不了，这已无可怀疑。

===Original===

As French now saw it, the affair stood as follows:

===Chinese===

在弗伦奇此刻看来，全案脉络如下：

===Original===

Stanley Pyke and Phyllis Berlyn, friends during childhood, find that they love each other when they renew their acquaintance in later years. But it is then too late for the course of true love to run smooth and a clandestine attachment follows. Berlyn learns of this some four months before the tragedy and as a result of his interference the two decide to discontinue their meetings﻿—in public, at all events. The flirtation with Colonel Domlio is possibly deliberately undertaken by Mrs. Berlyn to prove to her husband that her interest in Pyke is over.

===Chinese===

斯坦利·派克与菲莉斯·伯林本是总角之交，多年后重叙旧谊，才发觉彼此相爱。但那时为时已晚，真爱之路再难平坦，一段地下情就此开始。惨案前约四个月，伯林察觉了此事；由于他的干预，两人决定中止会面——至少不再公开往来。伯林太太与多姆利奥上校的那段调情，或许正是她有意为之，好向丈夫证明自己对派克已断了念想。

===Original===

But the two find that they cannot give each other up and the intrigue is continued secretly. Berlyn, however, is not hoodwinked. He sees his friend betraying him and he determines on vengeance.

===Chinese===

然而两人发觉谁也舍不下谁，私情便又暗中续上了。伯林却没有被蒙在鼓里。他眼见朋友背叛自己，决意复仇。

===Original===

His first move is to get an accomplice to assist in the details. Here French admitted to himself that he was out of his depth. He could not imagine who the accomplice was or why he should have been required. But if Berlyn were guilty, the murder was clearly a two-man job. Simultaneous activities in different places proved it.

===Chinese===

他的头一步棋，是寻个帮手协办种种细节。到了这一步，弗伦奇自认捉襟见肘：他想象不出帮凶是谁，也想不出为何非用此人不可。但倘若伯林确系凶手，这桩谋杀明摆着是两个人的活计——两地同时进行的勾当，便是铁证。

===Original===

The arrangements about the crate are next made. French was aware that these had not yet been properly followed up; other matters had been more urgent. But they represented a second string to his bow which he would develop if necessary.

===Chinese===

接下来是板条箱那头的种种安排。弗伦奇清楚，这条线自己还没正经追过；旁的事更急。但这是他弓上的第二根弦，果真需要，仍可补上一手。

===Original===

Then comes the night of the crime. While Berlyn and Pyke are at Tavistock, the accomplice drugs the watchman’s food. He then waits for the car. Pyke is sandbagged and his body carried into the works. One of the men then unpacks the crate, and taking the duplicator to pieces, returns the larger parts to stock. He has already doctored the cards, as well, necessarily, as the corresponding books. He then strips the recognisable clothes off the body, puts the latter in the crate, smashes in the face, closes the crate, and leaves all as before. Finally he escapes with Berlyn’s outer clothes and the smaller parts of the duplicator. He has only to get rid of these and his part in the ghastly business is complete.

===Chinese===

而后便是案发之夜。趁伯林和派克在塔维斯托克开会，帮凶在守卫的饭食里下了药，随后静候汽车。派克遭闷棍击毙，尸体被抬进工厂。两人中的一个随即拆开板条箱，把复印机大卸八块，将大件归回库存。库存卡他早已改过，与之相应的账册自然也非动不可。接着他剥去尸体上那身认得出主人的衣裳，把尸体装进板条箱，砸毁面容，封好箱子，一切恢复原状。最后他带上伯林的外衣和复印机的小件零件遁走。只消把这些处理干净，他在这桩可怖勾当里的份内事便算办完。

===Original===

In the meantime his confederate has driven the car out to a lonely part of the moor, changed the magneto, and made the tracks leading from the road.

===Chinese===

与此同时，他的同伙已把汽车开到荒原上一处僻静所在，换下磁电机，并伪造出从大路那头走来的脚印。

===Original===

The facts which pointed to Berlyn’s guilt were sixfold:

===Chinese===

指向伯林罪嫌的事实共有六条：

===Original===

- 
					

Berlyn in all probability was consumed by jealousy, one of the strongest of human motives for crime.

===Chinese===

- 伯林十之八九正受妒火煎熬，而妒忌正是驱人犯罪的最强烈动机之一。

===Original===

- 
					

Berlyn had an unparalleled opportunity for the deed, which only he could have arranged.

===Chinese===

- 伯林握有作案的无双良机，而这机会唯有他安排得出来。

===Original===

- 
					

It was not easy to see how anyone but Berlyn could have handled the magneto affair.

===Chinese===

- 磁电机那桩手脚，除伯林之外，很难想见还有何人操办得来。

===Original===

- 
					

Berlyn had the necessary position in the Veda Works to carry out the watchman and stock-card episodes.

===Chinese===

- 伯林在维达工厂的身份地位，才办得成守卫失守与库存卡做假这两桩事。

===Original===

- 
					

Berlyn answered the description of the man who had called for the crate.

===Chinese===

- 前来提取板条箱的那名男子的相貌，与伯林相吻合。

===Original===

- 
					

Berlyn had disappeared, an incomprehensible action if he were innocent.

===Chinese===

- 伯林失踪了——他若清白，此举便无从索解。

===Original===

As French thought again over the accomplice, he recognised that here was the snag in his theory. Motives of personal jealousy and private wrong leave no room for an accomplice. Moreover, it was incredible that a man who had shown such ingenuity could not have devised a scheme to carry out the crime single-handed.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇再把帮凶一事从头思量，便看出这正是他这套推断里的绊马索。出于私妒私怨的杀人动机，容不下什么帮手。再者，一个能设下这般巧妙机关的人，竟筹不出一套独自成事的法子，这也令人难以置信。

===Original===

But though French recognised that there were points in the case as yet unexplained, he saw that his own procedure was clear. He must start the search for Berlyn and he must learn the identity of the accomplice.

===Chinese===

不过，弗伦奇虽明白案中尚有未解之点，却也看清了自己该走的路：他必须着手搜捕伯林，必须查明帮凶的身份。

===Original===

The first of these was easy. He had compiled a pretty accurate description of the junior partner and Daw had got hold of his photograph. A note in the *Police Gazette* would start every police officer in the country on the search.

===Chinese===

头一件容易。他已拟好一份相当准确的小合伙人相貌通缉单，道警士也弄到了他的照片。在《警察公报》（*Police Gazette*）上登一条启事，全国的警务人员便都会投入搜捕。

===Original===

The second problem he found more difficult. Rack his brains as he would, he could think of no one who might have helped Berlyn.

===Chinese===

第二桩难题他却觉得棘手。任凭绞尽脑汁，也想不出有谁可能帮衬过伯林。

===Original===

He thought his next plan would be an enquiry into the whereabouts at ten o’clock, on the night of the crime, of everyone whom it was possible to suspect. That, coupled with an investigation as to who was in London when the various letters were posted, should yield results.

===Chinese===

他想，下一步该查一查：案发当晚十点整，凡可能沾嫌的人各在何处。再配上一项调查——那几封信分头投寄时谁在伦敦——应当能有所收获。

===Original===

The fact that a number of possible suspects had been at Mrs. Berlyn’s party from eight to eleven on the fatal evening seemed to rule them out. But French thought he should get some more definite information on the point. Accordingly, he went up to the works and asked for Mr. Fogden, one of those whom Lizzie Johnston had mentioned as being present.

===Chinese===

案发当晚八点至十一点，一批可能的嫌疑人都身在伯林太太的聚会，这一层似乎洗清了他们。但弗伦奇觉得，这一点还该摸得更实些。于是他上工厂求见福格登先生——莉齐·约翰斯顿提到过，此人当晚在场。

===Original===

“I heard a peculiar story about Mrs. Berlyn,” he said apropos of nothing special when they had talked for some time. “I was told she had a premonition of Mr. Berlyn’s death and was miserable and upset all that evening of the crime. A peculiar thing, if true, isn’t it?”

===Chinese===

「我听到一桩关于伯林太太的怪事。」两人谈了一阵之后，他忽然没头没脑地说。「有人说，她对伯林先生的死早有不祥的预感，案发那天整个晚上都愁苦不安、心神不宁。若真有其事，可就蹊跷了，是不是？」

===Original===

“Who told you that?” Mr. Fogden asked, sceptically.

===Chinese===

「这是谁跟您说的？」福格登先生将信将疑地问。

===Original===

“A chance remark in the bar of the Silver Tiger; I don’t know the speaker’s name nor, of course, do I know if his story was true.”

===Chinese===

「银虎旅馆酒吧间里偶然听来的一句话；说话的人姓甚名谁我不知道，他讲的是真是假，我自然也无从知道。」

===Original===

“Well, you may take it from me that it wasn’t. I was at Mrs. Berlyn’s that evening and there was nothing wrong with her that I saw.”

===Chinese===

「这个嘛，您信我的话没错——那是假的。那天晚上我就在伯林太太府上，依我看，她好好的，什么不对都没有。」

===Original===

This gave French his lead. When he left the office he had obtained all the details of the party that he wanted. On the day before the crime Mr. Fogden had had a telephone call from Mrs. Berlyn saying that Berlyn was to be out on the following evening and that she would be alone, and asking if he and one or two of the others would come and keep her company. Eight people had turned up, including himself, Cowls and Leacock from the works, a Dr. Lancaster and his wife, and two Miss Pyms and a Miss Nesbitt from the town. All these people were very intimate and the party was quite informal. Some of them had played billiards, and the others bridge.

===Chinese===

这话正给了弗伦奇入手之处。走出办公室时，那场聚会的种种详情他已尽收囊中。案发前一天，福格登先生接到伯林太太的电话，说伯林先生第二天晚上要出门，她一人在家，问他可愿约上一两位同事过来陪陪她。到场的共八位：除了他自己，有厂里的考尔斯和利科克，有兰开斯特医生夫妇，还有镇上皮姆家两位小姐（the Miss Pyms）和一位内斯比特小姐（Miss Nesbitt）。这几位彼此都极熟，聚会也全无客套。有人打了台球，其余的人打桥牌。

===Original===

This information seemed to French to eliminate Fogden, Cowls, and Leacock, as well, of course, as Mrs. Berlyn herself. He spent the remainder of the day in racking his brains for other possible accomplices and in thinking out ways to learn their movements on the night in question.

===Chinese===

在弗伦奇看来，这些情况似乎排除了福格登、考尔斯和利科克——当然，也排除了伯林太太本人。当天余下的工夫，他都用来苦苦搜寻旁的可能帮凶，并琢磨用什么法子查明他们在案发当夜的行踪。

===Original===

Next morning he took up the matter of the whereabouts of all suspects when the incriminating letters were posted in London.

===Chinese===

第二天早晨，他着手查证：那几封构成罪证的信在伦敦投寄时，全体嫌疑人各在何处。

===Original===

Fortunately, the enquiry presented but little difficulty. A further application to Mr. Fogden revealed the fact there was an attendance book at the works which all the officers signed, from Mr. Fogden himself down. This book showed that everyone concerned was in Ashburton on the dates of posting. Even Stanley Pyke, who was absent five days out of six on his rounds, had been there. Further, Mr. Fogden’s diary showed that he had had interviews with Colonel Domlio on the critical days. From Lizzie Johnston, French learned that Mrs. Berlyn had also been at home during the period.

===Chinese===

所幸这项查问没费多少周折。再向福格登先生一打听便知：厂里有一本签到簿，上自福格登先生本人，下至各职员，人人签名。簿上显示，凡与此案沾边的人，投信那几天都在阿什伯顿。就连六天里倒有五天出门跑差的斯坦利·派克，那几日也在。再者，福格登先生的记事簿显示，在那几个要紧的日子，他同多姆利奥上校有过会晤。又从莉齐·约翰斯顿口中，弗伦奇得知那段日子伯林太太同样一直在家。

===Original===

French was more puzzled than ever. It looked as if someone must have been mixed up in the affair of whose existence he was still in ignorance.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇比先前更犯了难。看来此案之中，必定还卷着一个他至今不知其存在的人。

===Original===

Just as he was about to step into bed that night an idea struck him which gave him sharply to think. As he considered it he began to wonder if his whole view of the crime were not mistaken. He suddenly saw that the facts could bear a quite different interpretation from that which he had placed upon them, an interpretation, moreover, which would go far towards solving the problem of the accomplice.

===Chinese===

那天夜里，他正要上床，脑中忽地掠过一个念头，令他猛然凝神。越琢磨，他越怀疑自己对此案的全部看法莫非一开始就错了。他蓦地看出：这些事实完全可以作另一种与他所下的判语全然不同的解读——而且那种解读，还能大大推进帮凶之谜的破解。

===Original===

Once again he swung from depression to optimism as, chuckling gently to himself, he decided that next morning he would embark on a line of enquiry which up to the present he had been stupid enough entirely to overlook.

===Chinese===

他再一次从消沉荡向振作，独自轻轻笑着，打定主意：明早便着手一条查案的路子——那条路，他此前竟是蠢得浑然不曾放在心上。

## XIII The Accomplice? / XIII 帮凶？

===Original===

French’s new idea had been subconsciously in his mind from the very first, but probably owing to his theory of the guilt of one of the two men supposed to be lost, he had never given it the consideration he now saw that it deserved.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇的这个新念头，其实打从一开始就潜伏在他潜意识里，只是大约因为他先前认定罪在两个据信失踪者之一，始终没给它应有的掂量——如今他才看出，这念头原值得细想。

===Original===

Suppose that on the night of the tragedy the lines of footprints had not been faked. Suppose that after leaving the car the two men had walked across the moor *and reached Domlio’s*. Suppose that Domlio was the moving spirit in the affair and Berlyn merely the accomplice.

===Chinese===

假定惨案当晚那几行脚印并非伪造。假定两人下车之后，当真徒步穿过了荒原，*走到了多姆利奥家*。假定此案的主谋是多姆利奥，伯林不过是个帮凶。

===Original===

This idea, French thought, would account not only for the facts which his previous theory had covered, but also for nearly all of those which the latter had failed to meet.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇想，这一设想不仅能解释先前理论已经涵盖的事实，先前理论几乎全然解释不了的疑点，它也差不多都能解释。

===Original===

As before, the affair hinged on the fatal attractiveness of Phyllis Berlyn, but in this case Domlio was the victim. Suppose Domlio had fallen desperately in love with Phyllis and that she had encouraged him. So far from this being unlikely, the facts bore it out. Different witnesses had testified to the flirtation and Mrs. Berlyn herself had not denied it.

===Chinese===

和先前一样，整个案子仍系于菲莉斯·伯林那致命的魅力，只是这一回，落入情网的是多姆利奥。假定多姆利奥不顾一切地爱上了菲莉斯，而她也对他加以鼓励。这非但并非不近情理，事实反倒处处印证：不同的证人都证实过那段调情，伯林太太自己也未尝否认。

===Original===

Domlio then would see that there was a double barrier to the realisation of his desires. There was of course Berlyn, but if Berlyn were out of the way there was still Pyke. How far Mrs. Berlyn loved Pyke, Domlio might not know, but their “affair” was common knowledge and he would want to be on the safe side. If murder were the way out in one case, why not in both? The risk was probably no greater, and once both his rivals were out of the way, his own happiness was secured.

===Chinese===

那么，多姆利奥便会看到，横在他心愿之前的是双重障碍。伯林固然是一个，可即便伯林没了，还有派克。伯林太太对派克究竟用情多深，多姆利奥未必知道，但他俩的「私情」尽人皆知，他自然要图个万全。既然除掉一个可以用谋杀来解决，为什么不能两个一起解决？风险未必更大，而一旦两个情敌都消失，他自己的幸福便稳稳到手了。

===Original===

His plan decided on, he would approach his friend Berlyn with insidious suggestions as to the part Pyke was playing with his wife. Gradually he would let it be known that he also had occasion to hate Pyke﻿—obviously for some quite different reason. He would feed the other’s jealousy until at last Berlyn would be as ready for the crime as he was himself. Then he would put forward his proposals.

===Chinese===

计议定下之后，他便去接近朋友伯林，话里话外阴险地暗示派克在他妻子身上扮演着怎样的角色。再渐渐透露，自己也有怨恨派克之处——那理由自然全然是另一码事。他会不断给对方的妒火添柴，直到伯林同他自己一样巴不得下手。到那时，他才亮出提议。

===Original===

Pyke was a cause of misery in both their lives; they would combine to remove his evil influence.

===Chinese===

派克是搅得他们两人都不得安生的祸根；他们要联手除掉这个恶人。

===Original===

Between them they would obtain and damage the spare magneto, then arrange the visit to Tavistock and the ordering of the crate and crane lorry. Berlyn would require Pyke to accompany him to Tavistock. All would be done without raising suspicion.

===Chinese===

两人会合力把那台备用磁电机弄到手并将它弄坏，再安排好塔维斯托克之行，订好板条箱和吊车。然后伯林会要派克陪他同去塔维斯托克。一切都做得不露半点疑心。

===Original===

On the fatal night Domlio would go to the works and drug Gurney’s supper. Later on, during the run back from Tavistock, Berlyn would stop the car and pretend to Pyke that it had broken down. He would suggest looking up Domlio, who would certainly run them into Ashburton in his own car. A light in the colonel’s study would lead them direct to his French window and Domlio would admit them without letting his servants know of their call.

===Chinese===

到了那个致命的夜晚，多姆利奥先到工厂，在格尼的晚饭里下药。稍后，在从塔维斯托克返程的路上，伯林把车停下，向派克佯称车子出了毛病。他会提议去找多姆利奥，而上校必定会用自己的车把他们捎进阿什伯顿。书房里的一盏灯会把他们径直引到上校的落地长窗前，多姆利奥会放他们进屋，而不让仆人知道他们来过。

===Original===

Domlio would immediately get out his car and they would start for the town. A sandbag would be in the car and on the way Pyke would be done to death. The two men would then leave the car in some deserted place, and carrying the body to the works, would pack it in the crate. When the ghastly work was done they would return to the car, taking with them Pyke’s suit and the small parts of the duplicator. These they would get rid of later. Lastly they would change the magneto on Berlyn’s car.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥随即开出自己的车，三人动身往镇上去。车里会备着一条沙袋，半路上派克便会被结果性命。随后两人把车撂在某处荒僻地方，抬着尸体到工厂，装进板条箱。这桩骇人的勾当做完，他们便回到车上，随身带走派克的西装和复印机的小部件，留待日后再处置。最后，他们换掉伯林车上的磁电机。

===Original===

So far French was well pleased with his new theory, but he realised that it contained a couple of nasty snags.

===Chinese===

到这里，弗伦奇对新理论颇为满意，但他也看出其中藏着两处恼人的漏洞。

===Original===

In the first place, it did not account satisfactorily for the disappearance of Berlyn. Presumably Domlio had manoeuvred his colleague into such a position that he could give him away to the police with safety to himself. Berlyn would therefore have to do the other’s bidding, which would be to disappear and to get rid of the crate. This was possible, but there was not a shred of proof that it had happened.

===Chinese===

首先，它没能圆满解释伯林的下落不明。想来是多姆利奥把同伙摆布到了这样一个境地：他既能把伯林拱手交给警方，自己又安然无虞。伯林只得听对方摆布，也就是销声匿迹，并把板条箱处理掉。这并非不可能，却连一星半点它确曾发生的证据也没有。

===Original===

Secondly, the theory did not explain how the letters were posted in London. However, though French was not entirely satisfied, he grew more and more convinced that he was on sure ground in suspecting Domlio. At all events, his next job must be to test the point.

===Chinese===

其次，这理论也解释不了那些信怎会在伦敦投寄。不过，弗伦奇虽不全然满意，却越来越确信，怀疑多姆利奥是站得住脚的。无论如何，他的下一件事就是验证这一点。

===Original===

First he decided to find out what Sergeant Daw could tell him about the colonel and early next morning saw him at the police station. The sergeant greeted him with a peculiar smile.

===Chinese===

他决定先弄清道警士能讲出些上校的什么，于是第二天一早便到警察局见他。警士迎上来时，脸上挂着一丝异样的笑。

===Original===

“I suppose, sir, you’ve heard the rumor that’s going round?” he asked at once.

===Chinese===

「先生，想必您已经听见外头传的风声了吧？」他开门见山地问。

===Original===

“What’s that, Sergeant?”

===Chinese===

「什么风声，警士？」

===Original===

“They say you’ve found out that Mr. Berlyn murdered Mr. Pyke out on the moor that night. Mrs. Billing, Pyke’s landlady, is supposed to have recognised the underclothes.”

===Chinese===

「他们说，您已经查明那天夜里是伯林先生在荒原上害死了派克先生。据说派克的房东比林太太认出了那身内衣。」

===Original===

French smiled.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇笑了。

===Original===

“Well it’s quite true,” he admitted. “I didn’t mean to keep it from you, Sergeant, but I went off to London as soon as I discovered it. I warned Mrs. Billing not to talk, but I hardly believed she could help herself.”

===Chinese===

「嗯，确有其事。」他坦承。「我不是有意瞒你，警士，只是一发现这事我就赶去伦敦了。我叮嘱过比林太太别往外说，可我也没指望她真能管得住嘴。」

===Original===

The sergeant was evidently upset.

===Chinese===

警士显然心里很不好受。

===Original===

“I’m sorry about the whole thing, Mr. French. I should have thought Mr. Berlyn was the last man who would do such a thing.”

===Chinese===

「这整桩事真叫我难过，弗伦奇先生。我原以为，伯林先生是天底下最不可能干出这种事的人。」

===Original===

“You may be right. Indeed, it’s a matter arising out of that very point that I want to see you about. I have a notion there was a second person in it﻿—someone who might even have taken the lead. Tell me”﻿—French’s voice became very confidential﻿—“what sort of a man is Colonel Domlio?”

===Chinese===

「你也许说得对。说起来，我要见你，正是为着从这一点上生出的一件事。我有个念头：这案子里还有第二个人——说不定还是领头的。告诉我，」——弗伦奇的声音变得极尽机密——「多姆利奥上校是个怎样的人？」

===Original===

The sergeant looked shocked.

===Chinese===

警士面露愕然。

===Original===

“Colonel Domlio?” he repeated. “Surely, sir, you don’t mean to suggest that the colonel was mixed up in a murder?”

===Chinese===

「多姆利奥上校？」他重复了一遍。「先生，您总不是要说，上校卷进了一桩谋杀案吧？」

===Original===

“You don’t think it likely?”

===Chinese===

「你觉得不可能？」

===Original===

“I don’t, sir, and that’s a fact. The colonel’s a very quiet man and peculiar in some ways, but he’s well respected in the district.”

===Chinese===

「不可能，先生，这是实话。上校是个非常安静的人，有些地方脾气古怪，可在这一带极受敬重。」

===Original===

“So was many a murderer.”

===Chinese===

「受人敬重的杀人犯，多的是呢。」

===Original===

The sergeant was clearly sceptical, though anxious to be polite. He said he was sure Mr. French would not speak without good reason, but his own view was evident.

===Chinese===

警士分明是不信的，尽管还竭力保持客气。他说他相信弗伦奇先生无凭无据不会开口，但他自己的看法已经写在脸上了。

===Original===

“Well, tell me all you know about him, anyway.”

===Chinese===

「好吧，那不管怎样，把你对他的了解都讲给我听听。」

===Original===

Domlio, it appeared, was a man of about forty-five, short, thickset, and dark. (Not the man who called for the crate, thought French.) He was very well off, and since his wife had died some six years earlier, had lived alone with his servants in his house on the moor. He held sufficient Veda stock to give him a controlling interest in the firm, acted as consulting engineer, and was usually referred to as the senior partner. Entomology was his pet hobby and it was believed that he was writing a book on the insect life of the moor.

===Chinese===

据说道来，多姆利奥四十五岁上下，身量矮壮，肤色黝黑。（不是去领板条箱的那个人，弗伦奇心里想。）他极为富有，自六年前丧妻，便独自带着仆人住在荒原上的宅子里。他握有的维达股份足以控制公司，兼任顾问工程师，人们一般称他为大合伙人。昆虫学是他的心头好，据说他正在写一部关于荒原昆虫的书。

===Original===

He had four servants. Inside was John Burt, valet, butler, and general factotum, and his wife, Sarah Burt, who combined the offices of cook and general servant. Outside was an ex-service man named Coombe, who acted as chauffeur and general handy man, and an old gardener called Mee. Mee lived with his wife and daughter in the gate lodge and Coombe boarded with them. All, so far as the sergeant knew, were reliable people of good character.

===Chinese===

他雇着四个仆人。宅内是约翰·伯特，身兼贴身男仆、管家和诸般杂务的总管；还有他妻子萨拉·伯特，厨娘兼杂役一肩挑。宅外有个退伍军人库姆，当司机兼打杂的匠人，还有个上了年纪的园丁，唤作米。米带着妻女住在门房里，库姆也在他家搭伙。据警士所知，这几个人都是靠得住、品行端正之辈。

===Original===

“I’ll go out and see the colonel after lunch,” French announced. “Could you lend me a push bicycle? I don’t want all my movements reported on by the driver of a car.”

===Chinese===

「午饭后我就去拜会上校。」弗伦奇宣布。「能借我一辆自行车吗？我可不想自己的一举一动都让汽车司机给汇报上去。」

===Original===

“I can borrow one for you, but it’ll not be much use on these hilly roads.”

===Chinese===

「我可以替您去借一辆，只是这些坡冈子路，它派不了多大用场。」

===Original===

“It’ll do all I want.”

===Chinese===

「我要用它办的事，它都办得了。」

===Original===

A couple of hours later French set out. When near Colonel Domlio’s gate he hid the bicycle in the brushwood and approached the house on foot. It was a smallish, creeper-covered building, L-shaped, with thick walls and heavy overhanging eaves. At least a hundred years old, French thought. It stood some two hundred yards back from the road and was approached by a drive which wound between clumps of stunted trees and shrubs. In front was a small lawn of mown grass, while between the trees to the right French glimpsed the roofs of outbuildings. The place had a cared-for appearance. The woodwork of the house had been freshly painted, the flower beds were tidy, and the grass edges had recently been cut.

===Chinese===

两个钟头后，弗伦奇出发了。将近多姆利奥上校家大门口时，他把自行车藏进灌木丛，步行接近宅子。那是栋不大的房子，爬满了藤蔓，呈 L 形，墙身厚实，屋檐低垂。少说也有一百年了，弗伦奇想。房子离大路约二百码远，一条车道在丛丛矮树和灌木间蜿蜒通到门前。屋前是一片修剪过的小草坪，右边树隙间弗伦奇瞥见几间外屋的屋顶。这地方看得出有人悉心照管：房子的木作新漆过，花坛收拾得齐整，草皮边缘也是新近才修割的。

===Original===

The door was opened by an elderly man in butler’s dress, honest and kindly-looking, but rather stupid. John Burt, evidently. He asked French to step inside while he took his card to his master.

===Chinese===

开门的是个穿管家服的老者，模样忠厚和善，却透着几分迟钝。显然就是约翰·伯特。他请弗伦奇进屋稍候，自己拿着名片去回主人。

===Original===

The hall was of fair size, with a large, old-fashioned fireplace and lead-lighted windows. French had not much time to observe it, for Burt called him almost immediately into a room on the left of the hall door.

===Chinese===

门厅大小适中，有一座老式大壁炉和铅条镶拼的玻璃窗。弗伦奇没工夫细看，伯特几乎立刻就请他进了厅门左手边的一个房间。

===Original===

It was long, low, and delightfully furnished as a study. Bookcases lined the walls and a couple of deep saddlebag armchairs stood on the soft Chinese carpet in front of the fireplace. A collector’s entomological cabinet was in one corner, with close by a table bearing books and a fine microscope. The room was evidently in the corner of the house, for there were French windows in adjacent walls. In one of these was a leather-topped desk and at the desk was seated a shortish man with a strong, clean-shaven face, iron grey hair, and a not too amiable expression. He rose as French entered.

===Chinese===

房间长而低矮，布置成书房，雅致宜人。四壁排着书柜，壁炉前柔软的中国地毯上摆着两把深座的大扶手椅。一只藏家用的昆虫标本柜立在墙角，近旁一张桌上放着书和一台精良的显微镜。这房间显然位于宅子的拐角，因为相邻的两面墙上各有一排落地长窗。其中一扇窗前摆着一张皮面书桌，桌后坐着个矮个子男人，脸膛刚毅，刮得干干净净，头发铁灰，神色不怎么和悦。弗伦奇进屋，他站起身来。

===Original===

“Inspector French of Scotland Yard, is it not? I have heard that you were in the town.”

===Chinese===

「苏格兰场的弗伦奇探长，对吧？我听说您到了镇上。」

===Original===

“That’s correct, sir,” French answered, taking the chair to which the other pointed. “You’ve probably heard enough, then, to guess my business?”

===Chinese===

「正是，先生。」弗伦奇答道，在对方指的椅子上落了座。「那么您听到的想必不少，猜得出我的来意了？」

===Original===

Colonel Domlio squared his shoulders.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥上校挺了挺肩膀。

===Original===

“I heard you were investigating the deaths of Mr. Berlyn and Mr. Pyke. I don’t know the object of this call.”

===Chinese===

「我听说您在调查伯林先生和派克先生的死因。至于此番来访所为何事，我就不得而知了。」

===Original===

“I’ve come, Colonel Domlio, in connection with my investigation. I want to ask for your help in it.”

===Chinese===

「多姆利奥上校，我是为手头的调查来的，想请您帮个忙。」

===Original===

“What do you wish me to do?”

===Chinese===

「您要我做什么？」

===Original===

“Two things, sir. In the first place, I want any information you can give me about either of the two gentlemen you mentioned or anything which might throw light on the tragedy. Secondly, I would be obliged if you would answer the purely formal question that we inspectors have to ask all who were in any way connected with the victim of such a tragedy. Where were you yourself at the time of the occurrence?”

===Chinese===

「两件事，先生。第一，关于您提到的那两位先生，凡您能提供的情况，或任何有助于查明这桩惨案的材料，我都要请教。第二，想请您回答一个纯属例行的问题——凡与这类惨案的死者有一丝一毫关联的人，我们做探员的都要照例一问：案发之时，您本人在哪里？」

===Original===

The colonel raised his eyebrows.

===Chinese===

上校扬了扬眉毛。

===Original===

“Do you suspect me of murdering Mr. Pyke?” he asked, drily.

===Chinese===

「您是怀疑我谋杀了派克先生？」他冷冷地问。

===Original===

“I think, sir, you needn’t take up that line.” French’s tone was also a trifle dry. “I have explained that my question is a formal one, invariably put. You are not bound to answer it unless you wish.”

===Chinese===

「我看您大可不必来这一套，先生。」弗伦奇的语气也带了点干巴巴的味道。「我说过了，我的问题是例行公事，逢人必问。您若不愿回答，尽可不答。」

===Original===

“If I don’t you will suspect me in reality, so I don’t see that I have much option. I was here, in this room.”

===Chinese===

「我要不答，您心里就真要起疑了，所以我看也没什么可挑的。我当时就在这儿，在这个房间里。」

===Original===

“Between what hours?”

===Chinese===

「具体是几点到几点？」

===Original===

“During the whole evening. I finished dinner about eight or a quarter past. Then I came in here and stayed here until I went to bed between one and two.”

===Chinese===

「整个晚上都在。我八点或八点一刻左右用完晚餐，随后就进了这屋子，一直待到一两点之间上床睡觉。」

===Original===

“And no one came in during that time?”

===Chinese===

「这段时间没有人来过？」

===Original===

“No one came in. I take nothing after dinner except a little whisky going to bed, and I have everything I want in the cupboard there. I’m writing a book at present and I don’t like to be disturbed in the evenings.”

===Chinese===

「没有人来。晚饭后我什么都不用，只是睡前喝一点威士忌，要的东西那边的柜子里都有。我眼下正在写书，晚上不喜欢被人打扰。」

===Original===

“Then in the face of what you’ve said I presume I needn’t ask you if you heard any sound at the door or windows?”

===Chinese===

「那么，听您这么一说，想必也用不着问您可曾听见门口或窗边有什么动静了？」

===Original===

“You need not.”

===Chinese===

「用不着。」

===Original===

“And﻿—excuse this question﻿—you didn’t sleep at all during the evening? No one could have knocked while you were asleep?”

===Chinese===

「还有——恕我冒昧问一句——您整晚都没打过盹？不会有人趁您睡着时来敲过门吧？」

===Original===

The Colonel smiled slightly.

===Chinese===

上校微微一笑。

===Original===

“Ingenious,” he remarked, “but unproductive. No, I didn’t sleep at all.”

===Chinese===

「问得机巧，」他评道，「可惜问不出结果。没有，我压根儿没睡。」

===Original===

“Thank you,” said French, “that disposes of one question. Now the other. Can you tell me anything likely to be helpful to me about either of the two gentlemen?”

===Chinese===

「多谢。」弗伦奇说，「这一问就此了结。再说另一件。关于那两位先生，您能告诉我些什么可能对我有帮助的情况吗？」

===Original===

The colonel regretted that in this case also he could do nothing to oblige. He would answer Mr. French’s questions so far as he could, but he had nothing to volunteer. And French found that after half an hour’s interrogation he had learnt just nothing whatever.

===Chinese===

上校表示遗憾，这一件他同样帮不上忙。弗伦奇先生问什么，他都会尽力作答，但要他主动提供，却什么也没有。而弗伦奇盘问了半个钟头，发觉自己简直一无所获。

===Original===

“There is one other matter to which I must refer,” he said. “I regret the necessity, as it’s somewhat delicate. Common report says that Mrs. Berlyn was on very intimate terms first with Mr. Pyke and then with yourself. Would you tell me how far that is true?”

===Chinese===

「还有一桩事，我必须一提。」他说。「实属无奈，因为此事颇有些微妙。外间传闻，伯林太太先是同派克先生过从甚密，后来又同您过从甚密。可否请您告诉我，这话有几分是真的？」

===Original===

The colonel squared his shoulders again and French presently saw that it was an unconscious nervous trick.

===Chinese===

上校又挺了挺肩膀，弗伦奇随即看出，这是他一种不自觉的紧张小动作。

===Original===

“Is it really necessary that Mrs. Berlyn’s name should be dragged in?” he asked, stiffly.

===Chinese===

「当真非把伯林太太的名字也拽进来不可？」他生硬地问。

===Original===

“I’m afraid so. You will recognise that I am trying to find motives.”

===Chinese===

「恐怕是如此。您想必明白，我要找的是动机。」

===Original===

“I don’t think you will find one there.”

===Chinese===

「我想您在那上面找不出动机来。」

===Original===

“On the contrary, Colonel Domlio, I have evidence that Mr. Berlyn was acutely jealous.”

===Chinese===

「恰恰相反，多姆利奥上校，我有证据表明，伯林先生妒火中烧，醋意极浓。」

===Original===

But the colonel was not to be drawn.

===Chinese===

但上校就是不肯上钩。

===Original===

“That is news to me,” he declared.

===Chinese===

「这我倒是头一回听说。」他断然道。

===Original===

“Well,” said French, doggedly, “I should like to have your definite statement as to whether such jealousy would or would not have been justified, in so far, at all events, as you yourself were concerned.”

===Chinese===

「那么，」弗伦奇不死心地追问，「至少就您自己这方面而言，这样的妒意究竟有没有道理，我想请您给一句明确的说法。」

===Original===

The colonel smiled sardonically.

===Chinese===

上校讥诮地笑了笑。

===Original===

“I should say that it would not have been justified.”

===Chinese===

「要我说，那是全无道理的。」

===Original===

“Very good, Colonel. I have now only one other request to make. I should like to interrogate your servants. Some of them may have seen or heard something which might be useful to me. Would you oblige me by calling them in and instructing them to reply to me?”

===Chinese===

「很好，上校。现在我只有最后一个请求了。我想讯问您的仆人。他们中间或许有人见过或听过什么对我有用的东西。可否劳驾您把他们叫来，吩咐他们有问必答？」

===Original===

For the first time an uneasy look appeared in the colonel’s eyes.

===Chinese===

上校眼里头一回掠过一丝不安。

===Original===

“Surely that is unnecessary?” he demurred. “What could they possibly tell you?”

===Chinese===

「那总该没有必要吧？」他推诿道。「他们能告诉您什么呀？」

===Original===

“Nothing, I very greatly fear,” French admitted. “But it is a routine enquiry and as such I dare not omit it.”

===Chinese===

「只怕是什么也告诉不了。」弗伦奇承认。「可这是例行查问，例行之事我不敢省略。」

===Original===

With an evident ill grace Colonel Domlio rang the bell. French, sensing his opposition, had become keenly alert. It seemed to him that he might be on the brink of learning something important. But instantly he decided that he would postpone serious examination of the staff until he had them to himself.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥上校老大不情愿地按了铃。弗伦奇觉出他的抵触，登时打起十二分警觉。他觉得自己也许正站在探悉重要内情的边上。但他当机立断：正式盘问仆役之事，推迟到撇开上校、单独面对他们的时候再说。

===Original===

The butler, Burt, answered the bell.

===Chinese===

管家伯特应铃而来。

===Original===

“This gentleman is Mr. French, Burt,” said the colonel. “He wants to ask you some questions. You might answer him so far as you can.”

===Chinese===

「伯特，这位先生是弗伦奇先生。」上校说。「他要问你几个问题。你尽你所知答他便是。」

===Original===

“It was only to know whether you heard or saw anything unusual on the night of the deaths of Mr. Berlyn and Mr. Pyke,” French explained.

===Chinese===

「只是想问问，伯林先生和派克先生丧命的那天夜里，您可曾听见或看见什么不寻常的事。」弗伦奇解释道。

===Original===

The man denied with what French thought was over-earnestness. Moreover, he looked acutely uneasy, even scared. French felt a sudden thrill, but he merely nodded and said:

===Chinese===

那人矢口否认，那副郑重其事的劲头，在弗伦奇看来未免过了头。不仅如此，他还显得惶惶不安，甚至是吓着了。弗伦奇心头猛地一激灵，面上却只点点头，说道：

===Original===

“You didn’t see any traces on the moor the next day?”

===Chinese===

「第二天您没在荒原上见到什么痕迹？」

===Original===

“Nothing whatever, sir,” said the man with evident relief.

===Chinese===

「什么也没有，先生。」那人答道，明显松了口气。

===Original===

“Thank you. That’s all I want. Now, Colonel, if I could see the others to put the same questions I should be finished.”

===Chinese===

「谢谢。我要问的就这些。那么上校，要是能见见其余几位，把同样的问题问一遍，我就完事了。」

===Original===

Mrs. Burt and the two outside men were produced in turn, and each denied having heard or seen anything unusual. Coombe and Mee, the chauffeur and gardener, were interested, but evidently nothing more. But Mrs. Burt reproduced all the signs of uneasiness which her husband had exhibited, only in an intensified degree. She was obviously terrified when French questioned her, and her relief when her ordeal was over was unmistakable.

===Chinese===

伯特太太和外面那两个男仆依次被带了上来，个个都否认听见或看见过任何异常。司机库姆和园丁米只是感兴趣，显见得再无其他。可伯特太太却把她丈夫那副心神不宁的迹象一一重演，只是程度更重。弗伦奇问她话时，她分明怕得厉害，一场盘问熬过去后那种如释重负，更是遮掩不住。

===Original===

But French apparently saw nothing amiss and when the quartet had gone he thanked Colonel Domlio for his assistance and apologised for the trouble he had given. And in the colonel’s manner he noticed the same repressed evidences of relief. That something had taken place that night of which the master of the house and the two domestics were aware, French was positive.

===Chinese===

可弗伦奇表面上浑然不觉，待这四人退下，他谢过多姆利奥上校的协助，又为给他们添了麻烦致歉。而在上校的举止里，他也察觉出同样强自按捺的宽慰之意。那天夜里发生过一桩事情，宅子的主人和这两个仆役都心中有数——这一点，弗伦奇确信无疑。

===Original===

He left the house and regained the clump of brushwood in which he had hidden the bicycle. But he did not withdraw the machine. Instead, after a quick glance round he crept in beside it, pulling the bushes over him to make sure that he was invisible from the road. From his hiding-place he could see the entrance to “Torview,” as the colonel had named his house.

===Chinese===

他出了宅子，回到藏自行车的那丛灌木旁。但他没有把车取走，反而迅速朝四下一扫，随即钻进车旁的灌木里，拉过枝条盖在身上，确保从大路上望不见自己。从这处藏身地，他能望见「托尔维尤」（Torview）——上校给宅子起的这个名字——的大门。

===Original===

He was waiting on a pure chance, but after an hour he found that his luck was in. He heard the sounds of an engine being started up, and presently saw a small green car turn out of the drive and disappear in the direction of Ashburton. In the car was Colonel Domlio.

===Chinese===

他等的纯属一个碰运气的机缘，可一个钟头后，好运真的来了。他听见引擎发动的声响，不久便见一辆绿色小车驶出车道，朝阿什伯顿方向去了。车里坐的正是多姆利奥上校。

===Original===

French allowed another twenty minutes to pass, then crawling out of the brushwood, he returned to the house. Burt again opened the door.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇又等了二十分钟，才从灌木丛里爬出来，回到宅子。开门的还是伯特。

===Original===

“I’m sorry to trouble you again, Mr. Burt,” he apologised, with his pleasant smile, “but I forgot to ask Colonel Domlio a question. Could I see him again just for a moment?”

===Chinese===

「真抱歉又来叨扰，伯特先生。」他带着讨人喜欢的笑容致歉，「我忘了一件事没问多姆利奥上校。能不能再见他一面，只要一小会儿？」

===Original===

“Colonel Domlio went out about half an hour ago, sir.”

===Chinese===

「多姆利奥上校半个钟头前出去了，先生。」

===Original===

“Ah, that’s very unfortunate.” French paused and looked disappointed, then brightened up. “Perhaps you could give me the information, if you would be so kind? I don’t want to have to come back another day.”

===Chinese===

「哎呀，那可太不凑巧了。」弗伦奇顿了顿，面露失望之色，随即又开朗起来。「您若肯行个方便，这情况由您告诉我可好？我可不想改天再专程跑一趟。」

===Original===

Burt was obviously disconcerted. But he tried to hide his feelings and reluctantly invited the caller into the study.

===Chinese===

伯特显然乱了方寸。但他竭力掩饰，勉勉强强把来客让进书房。

===Original===

“Yes, sir?” he said.

===Chinese===

「先生，您有何见教？」他说。

===Original===

French instantly became official and very stern. He swung round, frowning at the other and staring him full in the face. Then he said, harshly: “It is you I want to see, Burt. You lied to me this afternoon. I have come back to hear the truth.”

===Chinese===

弗伦奇霎时换上了公事公办的严厉腔调。他霍地转过身，皱眉逼视对方，直勾勾瞪着他的脸。然后他厉声说：「我要见的正是你，伯特。今天下午你对我撒了谎。我是回来听真话的。」

===Original===

The man started and fell back a pace, while dismay and something like terror showed on his features.

===Chinese===

那人猛地一惊，倒退一步，脸上现出惊慌失措，还有近乎恐惧的神色。

===Original===

“I don’t understand,” he stammered. “What do you mean?”

===Chinese===

「我不明白。」他讷讷地说。「您这是什么意思？」

===Original===

“It’s no use, Burt. You’ve given yourself away. You saw or heard something that night. What was it?”

===Chinese===

「没用的，伯特。你已经露了马脚。那天夜里你看见了什么、听见了什么。快说，是什么？」

===Original===

“You’re mistaken, sir,” he declared, with a look of relief. “I neither saw nor heard anything. I swear it.” And then gaining confidence: “I don’t know what right you have to come here and tell me I was lying. I’m sure﻿—”

===Chinese===

「您弄错了，先生。」他一口咬定，脸上还露出如释重负的神色。「我既没看见什么，也没听见什么。我可以起誓。」随即胆气渐壮：「我不明白您有什么权利跑到这儿来，说我撒谎。我敢说——」

===Original===

“Cut it out,” French said, sharply. “Look here, Burt, do you want to be arrested on a charge of conspiracy to murder?”

===Chinese===

「打住。」弗伦奇厉声道。「听着，伯特，你想不想以谋杀共谋的罪名被捕？」

===Original===

Burt’s jaw dropped, but French did not give him time to reply.

===Chinese===

伯特听得目瞪口呆，可弗伦奇没给他回话的工夫。

===Original===

“Because if you don’t you’ll tell what you know. Mr. Pyke was murdered that night, and perhaps Mr. Berlyn as well. They were not lost on the moor and it is believed they came here. If you keep back any information that might lead to the arrest of the murderer, it’s conspiracy﻿—accessory after the fact. Ten years penal for that, Burt! Come along, now. Make up your mind whether you’re going to tell or face the judge.”

===Chinese===

「你要是不想，就把知道的都说出来。派克先生那天夜里是被人谋杀的，说不定伯林先生也是。他们并不是在荒原上失踪遇难的，而且据信他们到过这里。你若隐瞒任何可能促成缉获凶手的消息，那就是共谋——事后从犯。那是要判十年徒刑的，伯特！来吧，快拿定主意：是肯说呢，还是去见法官。」

===Original===

Burt’s face had grown pale, but he stuck to it that he had neither seen nor heard anything. French cut his protestations short.

===Chinese===

伯特的脸色已经发白，可他仍咬定自己既没看见也没听见什么。弗伦奇打断了他连珠炮似的分辩。

===Original===

“Fetch your wife,” he ordered.

===Chinese===

「去把你太太叫来。」他命令道。

===Original===

The man’s manner as he heard these words, coupled with Mrs. Burt’s evident fear when originally questioned, assured French that this time he was on the right track. With evident unwillingness the woman appeared.

===Chinese===

那人听见这话时的态度，再联系到伯特太太先前受讯时那份明显的惧怕，使弗伦奇确信这一回自己走对了路。那妇人万般不愿地露了面。

===Original===

“Now, Mrs. Burt, I want to know what you heard or saw on the night of the tragedy. There is no use in telling me there was nothing. Now out with it!” And in terse language he explained what accessory after the fact meant, and its penalty.

===Chinese===

「好了，伯特太太，我要知道惨案那天夜里你听见了什么、看见了什么。跟我说什么都没有，那可不顶事。痛快招出来！」接着他用简截的语言讲明了事后从犯是什么罪名，又要受什么刑罚。

===Original===

Mrs. Burt was of less stern stuff than her husband. Under French’s examination she was soon in tears and presently, disjointed and in fragments, her story came out.

===Chinese===

伯特太太不如她丈夫硬气。弗伦奇一加讯问，她很快便哭了起来，不多时，东鳞西爪、断断续续地，她的事便倒了出来。

===Original===

It appeared that on the night of the tragedy she slept badly, owing to some small indisposition. Shortly after one she woke in considerable pain. She endured it for a time, then thinking that perhaps a hot drink would help her, she decided to go down to the kitchen and heat some milk. She got up quietly so as not to awake her husband, and left the bedroom. A quarter-moon dimly lit up the staircase and hall, so she carried no light. Just as she reached the head of the lower flight of stairs she heard the front door open. Startled, she drew back into the shadows, peering down at the same time into the hall. She was relieved to see that it was Colonel Domlio. He wore a hat and overcoat, and taking these off, he moved very quietly across the hall. Then she heard the click of the cloakroom door and slight sounds of movement as he approached the stairs. She slipped back into the passage which led to the servants’ quarters and in a few seconds the colonel’s bedroom door closed softly. This was a few minutes past two o’clock.

===Chinese===

原来惨案那夜，她因身子略感不适，睡得很不安稳。一点刚过，她便疼醒了，颇为难熬。她忍了一阵，心想兴许喝点热饮能好受些，便决定下楼到厨房热些牛奶。她轻手轻脚起了床，免得吵醒丈夫，走出卧室。半轮月亮淡淡照亮着楼梯和门厅，她便没有掌灯。刚走到下一段楼梯口，她听见前门开了。她吃了一惊，缩回暗影里，一面朝楼下的门厅张望。看清是多姆利奥上校，她才安下心来。他戴着帽子、穿着大衣，把这两样脱下，蹑手蹑脚地穿过门厅。跟着她听见衣帽间的门咔哒一响，又有轻微的动静朝楼梯这边过来。她悄悄退回通往仆役住处的那条过道，几秒钟后，上校卧室的门轻轻合上了。这时是两点过几分。

===Original===

It was unusual for the colonel to be out at night and her woman’s curiosity led her to examine the hat and coat. They were soaking wet. Rain was falling, but only very slightly, and she realised, therefore, that he must have been out for a considerable time.

===Chinese===

上校夜里外出是少见的事，她那妇道人家的好奇心促使她去查看那顶帽子和那件大衣。两样都湿得透透的。当时正落着雨，却只是稀稀微微，因此她明白，他必定在外面待了好一阵子。

===Original===

She thought no more of the incident, and having had her hot milk, returned to bed. But she had not slept, and soon Sergeant Daw appeared with his story of the missing men. This excited but did not perturb her, but when, a few minutes later, she heard Colonel Domlio assuring the sergeant that he had spent the whole evening in his study until going up to bed, she felt that something was wrong. But it was not until the next day, when she had learnt the full details of what had happened and had talked the matter over with her husband, that any possible sinister significance of her master’s action occurred to her. Burt, however, had pointed out that it was not their business and that their obvious policy was silence.

===Chinese===

这事她没有再放在心上，喝了热牛奶便回床去了。可她并未睡着，不久道警士就带着两人失踪的消息来了。这让她惊异，却未让她慌乱；可几分钟后，她听见多姆利奥上校向警士担保，自己整个晚上都待在书房里，直到上床安歇——她便觉得事情有些不对劲了。但也直到第二天，她把事情的原委打听得明明白白，又同丈夫把这桩事细细商量过后，才想到主人的举动或许有某种不祥的意味。不过伯特当时就点破了：这不干他们的事，他们该走的路显然是缄口不言。

===Original===

Mrs. Burt did not state that she had coupled the colonel’s nocturnal excursion with the tragedy, but French could sense that this was in both her and her husband’s minds. He wondered what motive they could have suspected and further questions showed that it was connected with the colonel’s intimacy with Mrs. Berlyn. According to Mrs. Burt this had been more serious than he had imagined. Mrs. Berlyn had spent several afternoons and an occasional evening with the colonel in his study and they were known to have had many excursions together on the moor. Since the tragedy, moreover, both the Burts noticed a change in their master. He had developed fits of abstraction and brooding and acted as if he had a weight on his mind.

===Chinese===

伯特太太没有明说她已把上校的夜行同惨案挂上钩，但弗伦奇感觉得出，这念头在她和她丈夫心里都有。他寻思他们会疑心到什么动机，再一盘问便知，那同上校与伯林太太的亲密交情有关。据伯特太太说，这段交情比他想象的更进一步。伯林太太曾在上校的书房里消磨过好几个下午，偶尔还有晚上，而且众所周知，两人一同在荒原上远足过许多回。再说，自惨案之后，伯特夫妇俩都发觉主人变了性子。他动辄出神发怔、郁郁沉吟，行事就像心头坠着一块重石。

===Original===

Believing he had got all he could from the couple, French warned them to keep his visit to themselves and immensely comforted Mrs. Burt by assuring her that she had told him little that he had not known before. Then saying he wished to have another word with the two outside men, he left the house and walked round the outbuildings.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇认定从这对夫妇身上再问不出什么了，便叮嘱他们对他此行守口如瓶，又着实宽慰了伯特太太一番，向她担保：她告诉他的，几乎没有一样是他先前不知道的。随后他说还想同外面那两个男仆讲几句话，便离开宅子，绕到外屋那边去了。

===Original===

At the back of the main house was a large walled yard with an old-fashioned stone-built well in the center and farm buildings along one side. Wheel tracks leading into one of these indicated that it was the garage, and there, polishing up some spare parts, was Coombe.

===Chinese===

正屋背后是一座围有高墙的大院子，院子正中一口老式石砌水井，一侧排着几间农舍般的库房。其中一间有车轮印迹通入，可见是车库，库姆正在里面擦拭一些零备件。

===Original===

French repeated his explanation about having forgotten to ask Colonel Domlio a question, then, after chatting for some moments, he returned to the night of the tragedy. Putting up a bluff, he asked at what hours the colonel had taken out and brought back the car.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇又把那套忘问多姆利奥上校一个问题的说辞讲了一遍，闲谈几句之后，便把话题引回惨案那夜。他使了个诈，问上校是几点把车开出去、又是几点开回来的。

===Original===

Coombe was considerably taken aback by the question and said at once that he knew nothing about it.

===Chinese===

这话问得库姆着实一愣，他立刻说自己一概不知。

===Original===

“But,” said French, in apparent surprise, “you must have known that the car was out?”

===Chinese===

「可是，」弗伦奇故作惊讶地说，「车出去过，你总该如何道吧？」

===Original===

To his delight, the man did not deny it. Oh yes, he knew that, but he had not heard it pass and he didn’t know when it had left or returned.

===Chinese===

让他喜出望外的是，那人并没有否认。哦，这个他知道，可他没听见车走，车是几点出去、几点回来的，他也不清楚。

===Original===

“Then how did you know it had been out? Did Colonel Domlio tell you?”

===Chinese===

「那你怎么知道车出去过？是多姆利奥上校告诉你的？」

===Original===

“No, he didn’t say naught about it. I knew by the mud on the car and the petrol that had been used.”

===Chinese===

「没，他半个字也没提。俺是瞧出车上的泥，还有用掉的汽油，才知道的。」

===Original===

“Pretty smart of you, that,” French said, admiringly. “So there was mud on her? Was she clean the night before?”

===Chinese===

「你可真机灵。」弗伦奇夸赞道。「这么说，车身上有泥？头天晚上它是干净的吗？」

===Original===

“No. He had her out in the afternoon and got her a bit dirty. But he said it was late and for me not to bother with her till the next day, and so I let her alone.”

===Chinese===

「不干净。下晌他把她开了出去，弄上了一点泥。可他说天晚了，叫俺第二天再打理她，俺就由她去了。」

===Original===

“Naturally. And was much petrol gone?”

===Chinese===

「那是自然。汽油烧掉得多吗？」

===Original===

“ ’Bout two gallons.”

===Chinese===

「两加仑上下。」

===Original===

“Two gallons,” French repeated, musingly. “That would run her about forty miles, I suppose?”

===Chinese===

「两加仑。」弗伦奇若有所思地重说了一遍。「这够她跑上四十英里光景吧？」

===Original===

“Easy that and more.”

===Chinese===

「跑这点路，轻轻松松，只多不少。」

===Original===

“You live at the lodge gate, don’t you, Coombe?”

===Chinese===

「你住在门房那儿，是吧，库姆？」

===Original===

“That’s right.”

===Chinese===

「是这么回事。」

===Original===

“Then the car must have passed you twice in the night. Surely you would have heard it?”

===Chinese===

「那么夜里车子必定打你门前过了两回。你总不至于没听见吧？」

===Original===

“I might not. Anyhow, I wouldn’t if she went out the back way.”

===Chinese===

「兴许听不见。反正，她要是打后头那条道出去，俺是听不见的。」

===Original===

All this was excessively satisfactory to French. The theory he had formed postulated that Domlio had secretly run his car to the works on the night of the tragedy. And now it looked as if he had done so. At least, he had taken the car out. And not only had he denied it, but he had arranged that the machine should be left dirty so that the fresh mud it might gather should not show. Furthermore, the hour at which he returned exactly worked in.

===Chinese===

这一切让弗伦奇满意到了极点。他立出的理论正假定：惨案当夜，多姆利奥曾悄悄把车开到工厂。如今看来，他果真这么做了。至少，他把车开了出去。他不但对此矢口否认，还特意安排把车撂着不擦，好叫它新沾的泥显不出来。而且，他回来的时刻，也分毫不差地对得上。

===Original===

For a moment French was puzzled about the quantity of petrol which had been used. Forty miles or more was enough for two trips to the works. Then he saw that to carry out the plan Domlio must have driven there twice. First he must have been at the works about ten to drug Gurney’s tea. Then he must have gone in about midnight with Berlyn and Pyke. So this also fitted in.

===Chinese===

有一刻，弗伦奇对耗掉的汽油量有些纳闷。四十英里出头，足够跑工厂两趟。随即他明白了：要把计划付诸实施，多姆利奥必定开车去了两趟。头一趟，他得在十点来钟到工厂，在格尼的茶里下药；第二趟，他得在午夜前后同伯林和派克一道进厂。这一节也对上了。

===Original===

French, always thorough, interviewed Mee. But he was not disappointed when he found the man could tell him nothing. Keenly delighted with his progress, he renewed his directions to keep his visit secret, and took his leave.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇行事一贯周全，又讯问了米。发现这人提供不出什么，他也不觉失望。他为自己的进展满心欢喜，重新叮嘱众人对此行保密，然后起身告辞。

## XIV French Turns Fisherman / XIV 弗伦奇改行当渔夫

===Original===

On reaching the road, French returned to his clump of brushwood and once more concealed himself. He was anxious to intercept Domlio before he reached home and received the account of the afternoon’s happenings. A question as to the man’s nocturnal activities would be more effective were it unexpected.

===Chinese===

一到大路上，弗伦奇便回到那丛灌木后面，重新藏好身形。他急着赶在多姆利奥到家、听闻下午种种变故之前拦住他。关于此人夜间活动的盘问，若能出其不意，收效才会更大。

===Original===

Though French enjoyed moorland scenery, he had more than enough of this particular view as he sat waiting for the colonel’s appearance. Every time he heard a car he got up hopefully, only to turn back in disappointment. Again and again he congratulated himself that he had found a position which commanded the entrances of both front and back drives, or he would have supposed that his quarry had eluded him. For two hours he waited and then at last the green car hove in sight. He stepped forward with upraised arm.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇虽然喜欢荒原风光，可坐着等上校露面，这一处景致却叫他看了个够。每回听见汽车声，他都满怀希望地站起来，结果总是失望地缩回去。他一再暗自庆幸，幸亏自己找的位置正扼着前后两条车道的入口，否则真要疑心猎物已经溜掉了。他足足等了两个钟头，那辆绿色小车终于驶入眼帘。他迎上前去，扬起手臂。

===Original===

“Sorry to stop you, Colonel,” he said, pleasantly, “but I have had some further information since I saw you and I wish to put another question. Will you tell me, please, where exactly you took your car on the night of the tragedy?”

===Chinese===

「抱歉拦您的车，上校，」他客客气气地说，「不过自上次见面后，我又得到了一些新的情况，想再问一个问题。能否请您告诉我，惨案当晚，您究竟把车开到了哪里？」

===Original===

The colonel was evidently taken aback, though not so much as French had hoped.

===Chinese===

上校显然吃了一惊，只是不像弗伦奇指望的那么厉害。

===Original===

“I thought I had explained that I wasn’t out on that night,” he answered, with only a very slight pause.

===Chinese===

「我记得已经说过，那天晚上我没有出门。」他答道，前后只顿了极短的一瞬。

===Original===

“To be candid,” French rejoined, “that’s why I am so anxious to have an answer to my question. If there was nothing in the trip which would interest me, why should you try to hide it?”

===Chinese===

「老实说，」弗伦奇应道，「这正是我急着想得到回答的缘故。倘若这趟出行里没有我感兴趣的东西，您何苦遮遮掩掩？」

===Original===

“How do you know I was out?”

===Chinese===

「你怎么知道我出过门？」

===Original===

“You may take it from me, sir, that I am sure of my ground. But if you don’t care to answer my question I shall not press it. In fact, I must warn you that any answers you give me may be used against you in evidence.”

===Chinese===

「您尽管信我的话，先生，我手里是有底的。您若不愿回答，我也不强逼。只是我必须提醒您：您给我的任何回答，日后都可能成为对您不利的证据。」

===Original===

In spite of evident efforts the colonel looked uneasy.

===Chinese===

上校尽管竭力稳住自己，神色间仍见不安。

===Original===

“What?” he exclaimed, squaring his shoulders. “Does this mean that you really suppose I am guilty of the murder of Mr. Pyke?”

===Chinese===

「什么？」他扬声喝道，端起了肩膀，「这么说，你当真以为派克先生是我谋杀的？」

===Original===

“It means this, Colonel Domlio. You’ve been acting in a suspicious way and I want an explanation. I’m not making any charges, simply, I’ve got to know. Whether you tell me now or not is a matter for yourself.”

===Chinese===

「我的意思是这么回事，多姆利奥上校：您行事可疑，我要一个解释。我不是要指控您什么，只不过我非得弄清楚不可。现在说不说，全在您自己。」

===Original===

“If I don’t tell you, does it mean that you will arrest me?”

===Chinese===

「我要是不说，是不是就意味着你要逮捕我？」

===Original===

“I don’t say so, but it may come to that.”

===Chinese===

「我没这么说，但事情可能走到那一步。」

===Original===

The colonel gave a mirthless laugh.

===Chinese===

上校干笑了一声。

===Original===

“Then I’m afraid I have no alternative. There is no mystery whatever about my taking out the car that night and I have no objection to telling you the whole thing.”

===Chinese===

「那就恐怕别无选择了。我那晚开车出去，本来就毫无隐秘可言，我不介意把事情从头到尾讲给你听。」

===Original===

“But you denied that you had done so.”

===Chinese===

「可你否认过自己出过门。」

===Original===

“I did, and there I admit having made a foolish blunder. But my motive in doing so must be obvious.”

===Chinese===

「我否认过，这一点我承认，是干了件蠢事。可我那样做的动机，想必是明摆着的。」

===Original===

“I’m afraid not so obvious as you seem to think. However, having regard to my warning, if you care to answer my question I shall be pleased to hear your statement.”

===Chinese===

「恐怕没有您想的那么明显。不过，考虑到我方才的提醒——您若愿意回答我的问题，我倒乐于听听您的陈述。」

===Original===

“I’ll certainly answer it. Possibly you know that I am interested in entomology? I think I told you I was writing a book about the insects of the moor?

===Chinese===

「我当然要回答。您也许知道，我素来喜好昆虫学？我记得跟您提过，我正在写一部关于荒原昆虫的书？

===Original===

“In order to get material for my book I make expeditions all over the moor. I made one on that day of the tragedy. I went to a little valley not far from Chagford where there are numbers of a certain kind of butterfly of which I wanted some specimens for microscopic purposes. While chasing one of these I had the misfortune to get a severe fall. My foot went into a rabbit hole and I crashed, as the airmen say. I was winded and it was some time before I could get up, but I was thankful not to have broken my leg, as I might easily have done. That put me off running for one day and I crawled back to the car and drove home.

===Chinese===

「为了给书搜集材料，我常到荒原各处考察。惨案那一天我便出去了一趟。我去的是查格福德附近的一道小山谷，那里有为数不少的某一种蝴蝶，我想弄几只标本，作显微镜观察之用。追赶其中一只的时候，我不幸重重摔了一跤。一只脚踩进了兔子洞，用飞行员的话说，我『坠机』了。我摔得背过气去，好一阵子才爬得起来，所幸腿没有摔断——本来是很容易断的。这一跤叫我那一天跑不动了，我一路蹭回车里，开车回了家。

===Original===

“I was feeling a bit shaken and I went up to bed early that night, just before eleven. When I began to undress I found I had lost a miniature which I always carry and which I value extremely, not so much because of its intrinsic worth, but for sentimental reasons. Here it is.”

===Chinese===

「我摔得有些发慌，当晚早早便上了床，刚到十一点的模样。开始脱衣服时，我发觉丢了一件随身携带的微缩画像。我极其珍视它——倒不在于它本身值多少钱，而是出于情感上的缘由。喏，就是它。」

===Original===

He took a small gold object from his pocket and passed it across. It was of a charming design, exquisitely chased and set with diamonds, and French saw at once that it was of considerable value. It contained the portrait of a woman﻿—a beautiful haunting face, clear cut as a cameo. The whole thing was a wonderful example of artistic skill.

===Chinese===

他从口袋里取出一件小小的金器，递了过来。那物件设计雅致，雕工细腻，镶着钻石，弗伦奇一眼便看出价值不菲。里面是一幅女子的画像——一张美丽得萦绕人心的脸，轮廓分明，宛如贝雕。整件东西堪称艺术匠心的一件杰作。

===Original===

“My late wife,” Colonel Domlio explained as he replaced it in his pocket. “As you can imagine, I was distressed by the loss. I could only account for it by supposing it had dropped out of my pocket when I fell. I thought over it for some time and then I decided to go out to the place then and there and have a look for it, lest some shepherd or labourer might find it in the early morning. I did so. I took out the car and a strong electric torch and went back, and on searching the place where I fell I found it almost immediately. I came straight back, arriving shortly after two. Does that satisfy you?”

===Chinese===

「我的亡妻。」多姆利奥上校一面解释，一面把它放回口袋。「您可以想见，丢了它我多么懊丧。我只能这样解释：它是摔倒时从我口袋里滑落出去的。我琢磨了一阵，当即决定连夜赶回原处找一找，免得哪个牧人或庄稼人一大早捡了去。我便这么办了。我开车出门，带上一支强光手电筒，赶回原处，在摔倒的地方一搜，几乎立刻就找到了它。我径直回家，到家刚过两点。这下您满意了吗？」

===Original===

“No,” said French. “Not until you explain why you denied having been out when Sergeant Daw asked you.”

===Chinese===

「不。」弗伦奇说，「除非您解释清楚：道警士问您的时候，您为什么否认出过门。」

===Original===

“That, as I have said, was a mistake. But you can surely understand my motive. When I heard the sergeant’s story I recognised at once that my having taken out the car was a very unfortunate thing for me. I felt sure that foul play would sooner or later be suggested and I thought I should be suspected. I couldn’t prove where I had been and I was afraid I should not be believed when I explained.”

===Chinese===

「那个，我说过了，是个错误。可我的动机，您总该体谅。一听警士讲的情况，我立刻明白，那晚把车开出去，对我说来是件很倒霉的事。我断定迟早会有人提出谋害的说法，也会怀疑到我头上。我拿不出证据证明自己当时在哪里，又怕解释了也没人肯信。」

===Original===

“I’m afraid that is not very clear. Why did you imagine that foul play would be suspected?”

===Chinese===

「恐怕这话还不大明白。您为什么会想到会有人提出谋害？」

===Original===

Domlio hesitated.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥迟疑起来。

===Original===

“I suppose,” he said at last, “things have gone so far there is no use in trying to keep anything back. I knew that there was bad blood between Berlyn and Pyke. The sergeant’s news at once suggested to me that the trouble might have come to a head. I hoped not, of course, but the idea occurred to me.”

===Chinese===

「我想，」他终于开口，「事情到了这个地步，再瞒什么也没有用了。我知道伯林和派克之间早有嫌隙。警士带来的消息，立时叫我想到，两人的积怨可能已经闹到了摊牌的地步。我当然希望不是这样，可这个念头确实冒了出来。」

===Original===

“Even yet I don’t understand. What was the cause of the bad blood between those two and how did you come to know of it?”

===Chinese===

「我还是不明白。这两人之间的嫌隙因何而起？您又是怎么知道的？」

===Original===

“Surely,” Domlio protested, “it is not necessary to go into that? I am only accounting for my own actions.”

===Chinese===

「这个总不必细究了吧？」多姆利奥分辩道，「我不过是在解释自己的行为。」

===Original===

“It is necessary in order to account for your own actions.”

===Chinese===

「要解释您的行为，就必须细究。」

===Original===

Domlio squared his shoulders.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥端起了肩膀。

===Original===

“I don’t think I should tell you, only that, unfortunately, it is pretty well common property. I hate dragging in a lady’s name, though you have already done it, but the truth is that they had had a misunderstanding about Mrs. Berlyn.”

===Chinese===

「这本不该对你说，只可惜这事早就尽人皆知了。我不愿把一位太太的名字牵扯进来——虽说你已经牵扯了——但事实是，他们两人曾为伯林太太起过一场误会。」

===Original===

“About Mrs. Berlyn?”

===Chinese===

「为伯林太太？」

===Original===

“Yes. She and Pyke saw rather too much of each other. I don’t for a moment believe there was the slightest cause for jealousy, but Berlyn was a bit exacting and he probably made a mountain out of a molehill. I knew Mrs. Berlyn pretty well myself, and I am certain that Berlyn had no real cause for complaint.”

===Chinese===

「是的。她同派克来往得未免多了些。我丝毫不信这里头有什么值得吃醋的地方，可伯林这人有点苛求，多半是小题大做了。我自己同伯林太太相当熟，我可以肯定，伯林并没有真正值得抱怨的理由。」

===Original===

“You haven’t explained how you came to know of the affair?”

===Chinese===

「您还没说明，您又是怎么知道这段纠葛的？」

===Original===

“It was common property. I don’t think I can tell you where I first heard of it.”

===Chinese===

「那是尽人皆知的事。最初在哪儿听来的，我自己也说不上了。」

===Original===

French considered for a moment.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇思忖了片刻。

===Original===

“There is another thing, Colonel Domlio. You said that when you heard the sergeant’s story you suspected the trouble between the two men had come to a head?”

===Chinese===

「还有一件事，多姆利奥上校。您方才说，一听警士的消息，您就疑心两人之间的积怨已经闹到摊牌的地步？」

===Original===

“Might have come to a head. Yes.”

===Chinese===

「可能闹到摊牌的地步。是的。」

===Original===

“Suppose it had. Why, then, did you fear that the sergeant might have suspected you?”

===Chinese===

「假定确实如此。那您为什么害怕警士会怀疑到您头上？」

===Original===

Again Domlio hesitated.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥又迟疑起来。

===Original===

“That is a nasty question, Inspector,” he said at last, “but from what you asked me in my study you might guess the answer. As a matter of fact, I had myself seen a good deal of Mrs. Berlyn for some time previously. About this there was nothing in the slightest degree compromising. All through we were merely friends. Not only that, but Berlyn knew of our meetings and excursions. When he could he shared them, and he had not the slightest objection to our intimacy. But Daw wouldn’t know that. For all I could tell, the excellent scandalmongers of the district had coupled Mrs. Berlyn’s name with mine. Berlyn was dead and gone and he could not state his views. My word would not be believed, nor Mrs. Berlyn’s, neither, if she were dragged into it. I thought, at all events, I had better keep secret a mysterious excursion which might easily be misunderstood.”

===Chinese===

「这个问题可不好回答，探长。」他终于说，「不过，从您在我书房里的那些问话，您或许猜得到答案。事实上，此前有一段日子，我自己同伯林太太的来往也相当多。这事没有丝毫见不得人的地方。自始至终，我们只是朋友。不但如此，伯林也知道我们会面、出游的事，能来的时候他还一同参加，对我们过从甚密没有半点异议。可道警士不知道这些。说不定，本地那些高明的长舌汉，早把伯林太太的名字同我的名字连在一起传开了。伯林已经死了，没法再表白自己的看法。我的话不会有人信，伯林太太的也一样——倘若她被拖进这案子的话。我想，无论如何，一次容易招人误解的神秘出行，还是秘而不宣为好。」

===Original===

Not very convincing, French thought, as he rapidly considered what the colonel had told him. However, it *might* be true. At all events, he had no evidence to justify an arrest. He therefore pretended that he fully accepted the statement, and, wishing the colonel a cheery good evening, stood aside to let the car pass.

===Chinese===

不大可信，弗伦奇一面飞快掂量着上校的这番话，一面想。不过，它*可能*是真的。至少，他手里没有足以实施逮捕的证据。于是他装出全盘相信的样子，向上校道了声愉快的晚安，闪身让开车子。

===Original===

As he cycled slowly into Ashburton he kept turning over in his mind the question of whether there was any way in which he could test the truth of Colonel Domlio’s statement. Frankly, he did not believe the story. But unbelief was no use to him. He must prove it true or false.

===Chinese===

他骑着自行车缓缓驶进阿什伯顿，一路上心里翻来覆去地琢磨：有没有什么法子，能验证多姆利奥上校那番话的真假。老实说，他不相信这个故事。可光是不信，对他毫无用处。他必须证明它是真是假。

===Original===

All the evening he puzzled over the problem, then at last he saw that there was a line of research which, though it might not solve the point in question, yet bade fair to be of value to the enquiry as a whole.

===Chinese===

整个晚上他都在为这道难题绞尽脑汁，最后终于看出一条调查的路子——它虽然未必能解开眼下这个疑点，但对整个案情的调查却大有希望派上用场。

===Original===

Once again it concerned a timetable﻿—this time for Domlio’s presumed movements. Assume that Berlyn and Pyke reached the point at which the car was abandoned about 11:30. To convince Pyke of the bona fides of the breakdown, Berlyn would have to spend some time over the engine, say fifteen minutes. In the dark they could scarcely have reached Torview in less than another fifteen; say that by the time Domlio had admitted them it was close on midnight. Some time would then be consumed in explaining the situation and in getting out the car; in fact, the party could scarcely have left Torview before 12:10. Running to the works would have occupied the most of another half hour; say arrive 12:40. Domlio reached his home about 2:10, which, allowing half an hour for the return journey, left an hour unaccounted for. In this hour Pyke’s murder must have been committed, the duplicator taken to pieces and the parts left in the store, fresh tea put into Gurney’s flask, Pyke’s clothes and the small parts of the duplicator got rid of, and the magneto on Berlyn’s car changed.

===Chinese===

又是一张时刻表——这回是多姆利奥推定行程的时刻表。假定伯林和派克在11:30前后到达弃车地点。要让派克相信车子当真抛了锚，伯林就得在发动机上花费些工夫，算它十五分钟。摸黑赶路，他们再快也难在十五分钟之内走到托尔维尤；就算等上校放他们进门时已近午夜。接着说明情况、把车开出来，又要耗去一些工夫；实际上，一行人最早也得12:10才能离开托尔维尤。跑去工厂还得再花上大半个钟头；就算12:40到达。多姆利奥到家约在2:10，回程算它半个钟头，中间便空出一个钟头无从交代。就在这一个钟头里，必须干完这些事：谋杀派克，把复印机拆开、零件留在库房，把新茶装进格尼的保温瓶，处理掉派克的衣物和复印机的小零件，再换掉伯林车上的磁电机。

===Original===

French wondered if all these things could have been done in the time. At last, after working out a detailed timetable, he came to the conclusion that they could, on one condition: that the clothes and duplicator parts were got rid of on the way to Torview; that is, if no time were lost in making a detour.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇捉摸着，这么多事在这一点时间里是否做得完。最后，他排出一张详尽的时刻表，得出结论：做得完，但有一个条件——衣物和复印机零件必须在去托尔维尤的途中就处理掉；也就是说，绕这段路，一分钟也不能耽搁。

===Original===

Where, then, could this have been done?

===Chinese===

那么，这事能在哪里办呢？

===Original===

French took his map and considered the route. The Dart River was crossed three times and a part of the way lay through woods. But he believed that too many tourists strayed from the road for these to be safe hiding-places, though he realised that they might have to be searched later.
