===Chinese===

弗伦奇摊开地图，把路线研究了一遍。沿途三次横跨达特河，还有一段路穿行林间。但他认为，离路闲逛的游客太多，这些地方算不得安全的藏匿处——尽管他明白，日后或许免不了要搜上一搜。

===Original===

There remained two places, either of which he thought more promising﻿—the works and Domlio’s grounds.

===Chinese===

剩下的只有两处地方，他认为哪一处都更有指望——工厂，和多姆利奥的宅园。

===Original===

The fact that elaborate arrangements had been made to get Pyke’s body away from the works indicated that the disposal of it there was considered impossible. Nevertheless, French spent the next day, which was Sunday, prowling about the buildings, though without result.

===Chinese===

当初费了那许多周章把派克的尸体从工厂运走，这一事实表明，在那儿毁尸已被认定不可能。尽管如此，第二天是个星期天，弗伦奇还是在厂房四周转悠了一整天，却一无所获。

===Original===

This left Domlio’s little estate, and early the following morning French borrowed the sergeant’s bicycle and rode out to his former hiding-place outside the gates. History repeated itself, for after waiting for nearly two hours he saw Domlio pass out towards Ashburton.

===Chinese===

剩下的就是多姆利奥那座小宅园了。次日一早，弗伦奇借了警士的自行车，骑到大门外原先那处藏身之地。历史重演：等了差不多两个钟头，他望见多姆利奥出门，朝阿什伯顿方向驶去。

===Original===

As the car had not been heard by either Coombe or Mee on the night of the tragedy, it followed that it had almost certainly entered by the back drive. French now walked up this lane to the yard, looking for hiding-places. But there were none.

===Chinese===

惨案当晚，库姆和米谁也没有听见汽车声，可见那辆车几乎必定是打后面那条车道进来的。弗伦奇便沿着这条小径走上场院，一路寻找可以藏东西的地方。可是没有。

===Original===

He did not see any of the servants about and he stood in the yard, pondering over his problem. Then his glance fell on the old well, and it instantly occurred to him that here was the very kind of place he was seeking. There was an old wheel pump beside it, rusty and dilapidated, working a rod to the plunger below. He imagined the well was not used, for on his last visit he had noticed a well-oiled force pump a hundred yards away at the kitchen door.

===Chinese===

四下不见一个仆人，他便站在场院里，琢磨着自己的难题。忽然，他的目光落在了那口老井上，立时想到：这不正是自己在找的那类地方吗？井旁有一台老式轮泵，锈迹斑斑，破烂不堪，靠一根连杆带动井底的活塞。他猜想这口井早已废弃不用，因为上次来时，他注意到一百码开外厨房门口有一台上足了油的压力泵。

===Original===

The well was surrounded by a masonry wall about three feet high, coped with roughly dressed stones. On the coping was a flat wooden grating, old and decaying. Ivy covered about half of the wall and grating.

===Chinese===

井口围着一圈石砌井栏，约三英尺高，栏顶压着凿工粗糙的石块。压顶石上架着一面扁平的木格栅，年深日久，朽坏不堪。常春藤爬满了大约半个井栏和半面格栅。

===Original===

French crossed the yard and, leaning over the wall, glanced down. The sides were black with age and he could distinguish no details of the walls, but there was a tiny reflection from the water far below. Then suddenly he noticed a thing which once again set him off into a ferment of delight.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇穿过场院，俯身在井栏上朝下张望。井壁年久发黑，什么细节也辨认不出，只有深处的水面泛起一点微光。突然，他注意到一样东西，顿时又欢喜得心头发热。

===Original===

The crossbars of the grating were secured by mortar into niches cut in the stone. All of these bore signs of recent movement.

===Chinese===

格栅的横木用灰浆固定在石头凿出的凹槽里。而每一处凹槽，都留着新近挪动过的痕迹。

===Original===

Satisfied that he had at last solved his problem, French quietly left the yard and, recovering his bicycle, rode back to the police station at Ashburton.

===Chinese===

确信难题终于解开，弗伦奇悄悄退出场院，取回自行车，骑回阿什伯顿的警察局。

===Original===

“I want your help, Sergeant,” he said as Daw came forward. “Can you get some things together and come out with me to Colonel Domlio’s tonight?”

===Chinese===

「我需要你帮个忙，警士。」道迎上前来时，他说道，「你能不能收拾几样东西，今晚跟我到多姆利奥上校家去一趟？」

===Original===

“Of course, Mr. French.”

===Chinese===

「当然可以，弗伦奇先生。」

===Original===

“Good. Then I want a strong fishing-line and some hooks and some twenty-five or thirty yards of strong cord. I should like also a candle-burning lantern and, of course, your electric torch. I want to try an experiment.”

===Chinese===

「好。那么，我要一根结实的钓线、几只钓钩，再来二十五到三十码结实的细绳。另外还要一盏点蜡烛的风灯，当然，还有你的手电筒。我想做个试验。」

===Original===

“I’ll have all those ready.”

===Chinese===

「这些东西我都会备齐。」

===Original===

“I want to be there when there’s no one about, so, as the Colonel sits up very late, I think we’ll say three o’clock. That means we ought to leave here about one-fifteen. Can you borrow a second bicycle?”

===Chinese===

「我要趁宅里没人的时候去。上校睡得很晚，我看就定在三点钟。这就是说，我们一点一刻左右就得动身。你能再借一辆自行车吗？」

===Original===

The sergeant looked completely mystified by these instructions, but he answered, “Certainly,” without asking any questions. It was agreed that they should meet in the evening at his house, sitting up there until it was time to start.

===Chinese===

这一番吩咐听得警士满头雾水，但他只应了声「没问题」，一句也没多问。两人商定晚上在他家碰头，坐在那儿等到出发的时候。

===Original===

Having explained at the hotel that he had to go to Plymouth and would be away all night, French started out for a tramp on the moor. About eleven he turned up at Daw’s cottage, and there the two men spent the next couple of hours smoking and chatting.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇在旅馆里说明自己要去普利茅斯、整夜不归，随后便动身到荒原上徒步游荡。十一点来钟，他到了道的小屋，两人在那里抽着烟、聊着天，打发了随后的两个钟头。

===Original===

Shortly before three they reached Torview. They hid their bicycles in the brushwood and walked softly up the back drive to the yard. The night was fine and calm, but the sky was overcast and it was very dark. Not a sound broke the stillness.

===Chinese===

三点差一刻，两人到了托尔维尤。他们把自行车藏进灌木丛，轻手轻脚地沿后车道走上场院。夜色晴好无风，只是天阴得很，四下漆黑。万籁俱寂，没有一点声息。

===Original===

Silently they reached the well, and French, with his electric torch, examined the wooden cover.

===Chinese===

两人悄没声儿地摸到井边，弗伦奇用手电筒细看那面木盖。

===Original===

“I think if we lift together we can get it up,” he whispered. “Try at this side and use the ivy as a hinge.”

===Chinese===

「我看咱俩一齐使劲，能把它抬起来。」他低声说，「在这边用力，把常春藤当合页使。」

===Original===

They raised it easily and French propped it with a billet of wood.

===Chinese===

他们轻而易举地掀起了盖子，弗伦奇用一截短木把它支住。

===Original===

“Now, Sergeant, the fishing line.”

===Chinese===

「来，警士，上钓线。」

===Original===

At the sergeant’s cottage they had tied on a bunch of hooks and a weight. French now let these down, having passed the line through one of the holes in the grating to ensure its swinging free from the walls. Gradually he paid out the cord until a faint plop announced that the water had been reached. He continued lowering as long as the cord would run out; then he began jerking it slowly up and down.

===Chinese===

在警士的小屋里，他们早已在钓线一头拴好了一束钓钩和一个坠子。弗伦奇此刻把它往下放，钓线先穿过格栅的一个孔洞，好让钓钩悬空、不蹭井壁。他徐徐放线，直到轻轻一声「扑通」，知道够着水面了。绳子能放多长就放多长；随后他开始缓缓地把线上下提动。

===Original===

“Swing it from side to side, Sergeant, while I keep jerking it. If there’s anything there we should get it.”

===Chinese===

「你把线左右摆动，警士，我这边不住地提。底下要是有东西，咱们就该钩得着。」

===Original===

For twenty minutes they worked, and then, just as French was coming to the conclusion that a daylight descent into the well would be necessary, the hooks caught. Something of fair weight was on the line.

===Chinese===

两人忙了二十分钟。就在弗伦奇渐渐认定非得趁白天下到井里去不可的时候，钓钩挂住了什么。线上吊着件颇有分量的东西。

===Original===

“Let it stay till it stops swinging, or else we shall lose the hooks in the wall, Mr. French,” the sergeant advised, now as keenly interested as was French himself.

===Chinese===

「先别收，等它不晃了再说，不然钓钩要刮在井壁上，弗伦奇先生。」警士提醒道，此刻他同弗伦奇一样兴致勃勃。

===Original===

“Right, Sergeant. The water will soon steady it.”

===Chinese===

「好，警士。井水很快就会叫它稳下来。」

===Original===

After a few seconds, French began to pull slowly up, the drops from the attached object echoing loudly up the long funnel. And then came into the circle of the sergeant’s torch a man’s coat.

===Chinese===

几秒钟后，弗伦奇开始缓缓往上收线，钩住之物滴下的水珠，顺着这条长长的井筒激出响亮的回声。随后，一件男式上装进了警士手电筒的光圈。

===Original===

It was black and sodden and shapeless from the water, and slimy to the touch. They lifted it round the well so that the wall should be between them and the house and examined it with their electric torches.

===Chinese===

那衣裳浸得乌黑透湿，走了原形，摸上去黏糊糊的。两人把它提到井栏另一侧，让井栏隔在他们与宅子之间，用手电筒照着察看。

===Original===

In the breast pocket was a letter case containing papers, but it was impossible to read anything they bore. A pipe, a tobacco pouch, a box of matches, and a handkerchief were in the other pockets.

===Chinese===

胸前口袋里有一只信件夹，里头装着纸片，可上面写的什么全然辨认不出。其余口袋里有一只烟斗、一个烟丝袋、一盒火柴和一方手帕。

===Original===

Fortunately for French, there was a tailor’s tab sewn into the lining of the breast pocket and he was able to make out part of the legend: “R. Shrubsole & Co., Newton Abbot.” Beneath was a smudge which had evidently been the owner’s name, but this was undecipherable.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇运气不错，胸袋衬里上缝着一张裁缝标签，他还认得出其中一部分字样：「R. 施拉布索尔公司，牛顿阿伯特。」标签下方是一团污渍，显然原是衣主的姓名，却已无法辨认。

===Original===

“We’ll get it from the tailor,” French said. “Let’s try the hooks again.”

===Chinese===

「找裁缝就能问出来。」弗伦奇说，「咱们再下钩试试。」

===Original===

Once again they lowered their line, but this time without luck.

===Chinese===

他们又把钓线放了下去，这一回却没有那么走运。

===Original===

“No good,” French declared at last. “We’ll have to pump it out. You might get the depth, and then close up and leave it as we found it. We’d better bring a portable pump, for I don’t suppose that old thing will work.”

===Chinese===

「不行。」弗伦奇最后说道，「只好把井水抽干了。你去量一量井的深度，然后把一切复原，照我们发现时的样子留下。咱们最好带一台便携水泵来，我料想那台老家伙是转不动了。」

===Original===

They replaced the grating and the billet of wood, and stealing silently out of the yard, rode back to Ashburton.

===Chinese===

两人放回格栅和那截短木，蹑手蹑脚地溜出场院，骑车返回阿什伯顿。

===Original===

With the coat wrapped in paper and packed in his suitcase, French took an early bus to Newton Abbot. There he soon found Messrs. Shrubsole’s establishment and asked for the proprietor.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇把那件上装用纸包好，装进手提箱，搭头班公共汽车去了牛顿阿伯特。他很快找到了施拉布索尔公司的铺面，求见店主。

===Original===

“It’s not easy to say whose it was,” Mr. Shrubsole declared when he had examined the coat. “You see, these labels of ours are printed﻿—that is, our name and address. But the customer’s name is written and it would not last in the same way. I’m afraid I cannot read it.”

===Chinese===

「不好说这衣裳是谁的。」施拉布索尔先生验看之后说，「您瞧，我们的这种标签是印上去的——印的是我们的店名和地址。可顾客的姓名是手写的，浸了水就留不住。恐怕我认不出来了。」

===Original===

“If it had been possible to read it, I should not have come to you, Mr. Shrubsole. I want you to get at it from the cloth and size and probable age and things of that kind. You can surely find out all those things by examination.”

===Chinese===

「要是认得出来，我就不必来找您了，施拉布索尔先生。我要您从衣料、尺寸、大致年头这类东西入手。这些，您仔细查验一番，总归能弄清楚吧。」

===Original===

This appeared to be a new idea to Mr. Shrubsole. He admitted that something of the kind might be done, and calling an assistant, fell to scrutinising the garment.

===Chinese===

这在施拉布索尔先生听来似乎是个新主意。他承认这法子或许行得通，便唤来一名伙计，动手细细察看这件衣裳。

===Original===

“It’s that brown tweed with the purple line that we sold so much of last year,” the assistant declared. He produced a roll of cloth. “See, if we lift the lining here it shows clear enough.”

===Chinese===

「就是那种带紫线的棕色粗花呢，去年我们卖出去不知多少。」伙计说。他取来一匹布。「瞧，把这儿衬里掀起一角，看得清清楚楚。」

===Original===

“That’s right,” his employer admitted. “Now can we get the measurements?”

===Chinese===

「不错。」店主承认道，「现在能量出尺寸来吗？」

===Original===

“Not so easy,” said the assistant. “The thing will be all warped and shrunk from the water.”

===Chinese===

「不那么容易。」伙计说，「这东西泡过水，全走了形、缩了水。」

===Original===

“Try,” French urged, with his pleasant smile.

===Chinese===

「试试看。」弗伦奇面带他那讨人喜欢的微笑，怂恿道。

===Original===

An orgy of measuring followed, with a subsequent recourse to the books and much low-voiced conversation. Finally Mr. Shrubsole announced the result.

===Chinese===

接下来是一场测量的大忙乱，随后又翻账本，又低声商量了半天。最后，施拉布索尔先生宣布了结果。

===Original===

“It’s not possible to say for sure, Mr. French. You see, the coat is shrunk out of all knowing. But we think it might belong to one of four men.”

===Chinese===

「不敢说死，弗伦奇先生。您瞧，这件上装缩得面目全非了。不过我们认为，它可能属于四位顾客当中的一个。」

===Original===

“I see your difficulty, Mr. Shrubsole, but if you tell me the four it may help me.”

===Chinese===

「我明白您的难处，施拉布索尔先生，不过您把那四位告诉我，也许对我有用。」

===Original===

“I hope so. We sold suits of about this size to Mr. Albert Cunningham of Twenty-seven, Acacia Street, Newton Abbot; Mr. John Booth of Lyndhurst, Teignmouth; Mr. Stanley Pyke, of East Street, Ashburton; and Mr. George Hepworth, of Linda Lodge, Newton Abbot. Any of those any good to you?” Mr. Shrubsole’s expression suddenly changed. “By Jove! You’re not the gentleman that’s been making these discoveries about Mr. Berlyn and Mr. Pyke? We’ve heard some report that some Scotland Yard man was down and had found out that that tragedy was not all it was supposed to be. That it, sir?”

===Chinese===

「希望如此。卖出去这个尺寸的西装，有这么几位：牛顿阿伯特阿卡西亚街二十七号的阿尔伯特·坎宁安先生；廷茅斯市林德赫斯特的约翰·布斯先生；阿什伯顿东街的斯坦利·派克先生；还有牛顿阿伯特琳达庐的乔治·赫普沃斯先生。这几位里有您要找的吗？」施拉布索尔先生的神色忽然一变。「天哪！您莫非就是那位一直在查伯林先生和派克先生这桩案子的先生？我们听了风声，说苏格兰场来了个人，查出那桩惨案并不是原先想的那样。是这么回事吧，先生？」

===Original===

“That’s it,” French replied, feeling that it was impossible to keep his business private. “But I don’t want it talked about. Now you see why I should like to be sure whether that was or was not Mr. Pyke’s coat.”

===Chinese===

「正是。」弗伦奇答道，只觉得自己的差事反正瞒不住了，「不过我不希望这事传出去。现在您该明白，我为什么想弄清楚，那件上装到底是不是派克先生的了。」

===Original===

But in spite of the tailor’s manifest interest, he declared that the point could not be established. He was fairly sure that it belonged to one of the four, but more than that he could not say.

===Chinese===

可是，尽管裁缝兴致盎然，他仍旧声明这一点无从断定。他相当有把握，那是四人中某人的衣服，但再多就说不出了。

===Original===

But French had no doubt whatever, and, well pleased with his progress, he left the shop and took the first bus back to Ashburton.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇却已没有半点疑问。他为自己的进展满心欢喜，出了铺子，搭头班车返回阿什伯顿。

## XV Blackmail / XV 敲诈

===Original===

“Have you been able to get the pump, Sergeant?” asked French as he reached the police station that afternoon.

===Chinese===

「水泵弄到了吗，警士？」那天下午，弗伦奇一回到警察局便问道。

===Original===

“I’ve got the loan of one, Mr. French, or at least I’ll get it first thing tomorrow. From a quarry close by. It’s a rotary hand-pump, and Mr. Glenn, the manager, tells me that it will throw far faster than anything we’ll want.”

===Chinese===

「借到一台，弗伦奇先生，至少明天一早准能到手。就在附近一家采石场借的。是台手摇回转泵，经理格伦先生告诉我，它出水的快慢，远远够咱们使的。」

===Original===

“We shall have to fix it down in the well?”

===Chinese===

「咱们得把它安装在井里？」

===Original===

“Yes, the well’s forty-two feet deep. It’s thirty to the water and there’s twelve feet of water. But there’ll be no trouble about that. The beams that carry the old pump will take it, too.”

===Chinese===

「是的，这口井深四十二英尺。到水面是三十英尺，井里还存着十二英尺的水。不过这不成问题，架着旧泵的那几根横梁，照样吃得住它。」

===Original===

“You think they’re strong enough?”

===Chinese===

「您看那些横梁够结实？」

===Original===

“We’ll just have to try them.”

===Chinese===

「试过才知道。」

===Original===

“What about ladders?”

===Chinese===

「梯子呢？」

===Original===

“I’ve got a fifty-foot length of rope ladder from the same quarry.”

===Chinese===

「我从那家采石场还借来一条五十英尺长的绳梯。」

===Original===

“Good. What time can we start tomorrow?”

===Chinese===

「好。明天几点能动身？」

===Original===

“I shall have the pump by half past eight.”

===Chinese===

「八点半我就能把泵取到手。」

===Original===

“Then we should be at the colonel’s shortly after nine.”

===Chinese===

「那么九点刚过，我们就该到上校家了。」

===Original===

This time French thought it would be wise to have Domlio present at their experiment. He therefore rang him up and made an appointment for nine-thirty.

===Chinese===

这一次，弗伦奇认为让多姆利奥在场看着他们做试验才是上策。于是他打电话过去，约在了九点半。

===Original===

Early next morning a heavily loaded car left Ashburton. In addition to the driver it contained French, Daw, and two constables in plain clothes, as well as a low, squat pump with detachable handle, an immense coil of armoured hose, another huge coil of rope ladder, and several tools and small parts.

===Chinese===

第二天清早，一辆满载的汽车驶出阿什伯顿。车上除司机外，坐着弗伦奇、道和两名便衣警察，还装着一台矮墩墩、手柄可卸的水泵，一大卷铠装水龙带，另外一大卷绳梯，以及若干工具和零配件。

===Original===

“Another rush to Klondyke,” said French, at which priceless pearl of humour Daw smiled and the plain clothes men guffawed heavily.

===Chinese===

「又一趟克朗代克大淘金。」弗伦奇说道。这颗幽默的无价明珠，换来了道的一笑和两名便衣的放声大笑。

===Original===

“I should have thought that tailor could have fixed up the ownership of the coat,” Daw remarked, presently. “Shouldn’t you, sir?”

===Chinese===

「我原以为那裁缝总能认出上装的主人呢。」过了一会儿，道说道，「您不这么看吗，先生？」

===Original===

“Of course, Sergeant. But we shall get it, all right, even if we have to do all the work ourselves. I thought it wasn’t worth troubling about. It’s pretty certain the coat is not the only thing that was thrown into the well and we shall get our identification from something else.”

===Chinese===

「当然认得出，警士。不过就算事事都得我们自己动手，这一层也终归能弄清。我觉得犯不上为它多费周章。那件上装十有八九不是唯一扔进井里的东西，身份总会从别的东西上认出来。」

===Original===

The car was run into the yard, unloaded, and dismissed, while French went to the hall door and asked for Colonel Domlio.

===Chinese===

汽车开进场院，卸下东西，打发走了；弗伦奇则走到正门，求见多姆利奥上校。

===Original===

“Sorry to trouble you at this hour, Colonel, but I want you to be present at a small experiment I am carrying out.” He watched the other keenly as he spoke. “Will you please come out into the yard, where I have left Sergeant Daw and some men?”

===Chinese===

「抱歉这个时候来打扰您，上校，不过我想请您在场看看我做的一个小小的试验。」他说话时，目光锐利地注视着对方。「请随我到场院里来，道警士和几个人我已留在那儿了。」

===Original===

Surprise showed on the colonel’s face, but not, so far as French could see, apprehension.

===Chinese===

上校脸上露出惊讶之色，但就弗伦奇看来，并无惧意。

===Original===

“This is very interesting, Inspector. I’m glad I’m at least being informed of what is taking place on my own ground. I shall certainly see what you are doing.”

===Chinese===

「这可真有意思，探长。我很高兴，自己地界上发生的事，总算还有人来知会一声。你们要做什么，我自然要看个明白。」

===Original===

As they turned the corner and the purpose of the visit became apparent to Domlio, his surprise seemed to deepen, but still there was no appearance of uneasiness. The police had lifted the cover of the well and were getting the pump rigged. Coombe and Mee had joined the others and stood speechlessly regarding the preparations.

===Chinese===

拐过墙角，此来的目的在多姆利奥眼里一明，他的惊讶似乎更深了一层，却仍不见半点不安的迹象。警察们已掀起井盖，正在架设水泵。库姆和米也加入了人群，一声不响地望着这些准备。

===Original===

“Ah, an invasion? I presume, Inspector, you have adequate authority for these somewhat unusual proceedings?”

===Chinese===

「啊，这是入侵么？我姑且问一句，探长，这般不同寻常的举动，您想必有充分的授权吧？」

===Original===

“I think you’ll find that’s all right, sir. With your permission we’re going to pump out the well.”

===Chinese===

「我想这一层您尽可放心，先生。如今就请您恩准，我们要把这口井的水抽干。」

===Original===

“The removal of the well cover and the pump rather suggested something of the kind, but for the moment I can’t quite recall the permission.”

===Chinese===

「井盖被撬、水泵进场，倒也早叫人猜着了几分。只是一时之间，我怎么也想不起几时给过这份恩准。」

===Original===

“I feel sure that under the circumstance you won’t withhold it. Better lower that lantern with the candle, Sergeant, before you send a man down. We want to be sure the air is good.”

===Chinese===

「我确信在此情形之下，您是不会拒绝的。先把手点蜡烛的风灯放下去，警士，再叫人下去。得先弄准井里的空气没有问题。」

===Original===

“If it’s not an impertinence,” Domlio remarked, with ironic politeness, “I should be interested to know why you are not using the existing pump.”

===Chinese===

「倘若不算冒昧的话，」多姆利奥带着讥讽的客气说道，「我倒很想知道，你们为什么不用现成的那台泵。」

===Original===

“I didn’t think it was in working order. Is the well used?”

===Chinese===

「我看它早已不能用了。这口井还使用吗？」

===Original===

“An explanation, complete, no doubt, but scarcely satisfying. It did not occur to you to try it?”

===Chinese===

「好一个解释，想必滴水不漏，只是难以令人心服。您竟没想到试一试？」

===Original===

“No, sir. Too noisy. But what about the well?”

===Chinese===

「没想到，先生。太吵了。可这井到底用不用？」

===Original===

“Ah yes, the well. The well is used﻿—in summer. We have a gravity supply from the hill behind the house, but it fails in summer; hence pumping from the well.”

===Chinese===

「啊，对了，这口井。这井是用——夏天用。宅子后面山上有自流水源引下来，可一到夏天便断了流，所以才从井里泵水。」

===Original===

This statement was very satisfactory to French. It cleared up a point which had been worrying him. If it were possible to get rid of the clothes by throwing them down the well, why had Pyke’s body not been disposed of in the same way? But now this was explained. The condition of the water in the following summer would have led to investigation.

===Chinese===

这个回答让弗伦奇大为满意。它解开了压在他心头的一个疑团：既然把衣裳扔进井里就能了事，派克的尸体为什么不照此办理？如今有了答案——来年夏天井水一变坏，势必招来追查。

===Original===

“Try the fixed pump, Sergeant. It may save us rigging the other.”

===Chinese===

「试试那台固定泵，警士。兴许能省得再架新的。」

===Original===

But a test showed that the valve leathers were dry and not holding, and they went on with their original program.

===Chinese===

可一试之下，阀门的皮垫早已干透，封不住水，众人便仍照原定计划进行。

===Original===

French had been puzzled by the colonel’s attitude. If beneath his cynical manner he were consumed by the anxiety which, were he guilty, he could scarcely help feeling, he was concealing it in a way that was little short of marvellous. However, the preparations would take time and it was impossible that if the man knew what would be found he could hide all signs of tension.

===Chinese===

上校的态度让弗伦奇捉摸不透。倘若在这副愤世的做派之下，他真怀着那份焦灼——他若当真有罪，这焦灼几乎难免——那他掩饰的功夫简直神了。不过，准备工作总要费些时间，倘若此人知道井底会捞出什么，他绝不可能不露出一丝紧张的痕迹。

===Original===

The candle, lowered to the surface of the water, burned clearly, showing that the air was fresh. The rope ladder was then made fast to the stonework and Sergeant Daw climbed down. Presently he returned to say that the beams on which the old pump rested were sound. The new pump was therefore lowered and one of the constables sent down to begin work.

===Chinese===

蜡烛吊到水面，依然烧得明亮，可见井下空气新鲜。绳梯随即牢牢拴在井壁石架上，道警士爬了下去。片刻他回上来说，架旧泵的横梁结实着呢。新泵便吊了下去，又派一名警察下去动工。

===Original===

Getting rid of the water turned out a bigger job than French had anticipated. Slowly the level dropped. At intervals the men spelled each other, French and Daw taking their turns. By lunchtime the water had gone down seven feet, though during the meal it rose six inches. After that they worked with renewed energy to get the remaining five feet six inches out before dusk.

===Chinese===

抽水这活计，比弗伦奇预想的繁重。水位缓缓下降。众人轮番换班，弗伦奇和道也各领了自己的班。到午饭时分，水下去七英尺——只是吃饭那阵又涨回六英寸。饭后大家鼓足干劲，赶在天黑前把剩下那五英尺六英寸抽干。

===Original===

“You have a second well, have you not, Colonel?” French enquired. “I noticed a pump near the kitchen door.”

===Chinese===

「您还有第二口井，对吧，上校？」弗伦奇问道，「我注意到厨房门口有台泵。」

===Original===

“Yes. We use it for drinking purposes. This is only good enough for washing the car and so on.”

===Chinese===

「是的。那口井的水供饮用。这口井的，洗洗汽车之类的还凑合。」

===Original===

On more than one occasion Domlio had protested against what he called the waste of his time in watching the work. But French insisted on his remaining till the search was complete.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥不止一次提出抗议，说眼睁睁看着做工、白白糟蹋他的光阴。可弗伦奇执意要他留到搜查结束为止。

===Original===

About four o’clock the water was so far lowered as to allow an investigation of the bottom, and the sergeant, squeezing past the man at the pump, went down with his electric torch. French, leaning over the wall, anxiously watched the flickering light. Then came the sergeant’s voice: “There’s a waistcoat and trousers and shoes here, Mr. French.”

===Chinese===

四点来钟，水位低得可以查看井底了。道警士侧身挤过守泵的人，带着手电筒下去。弗伦奇俯在井栏上，焦灼地望着那团晃动的光。随即传来警士的声音：「这儿有一件背心、一条裤子和一双鞋，弗伦奇先生。」

===Original===

“That all?” called French.

===Chinese===

「就这些？」弗伦奇朝下喊道。

===Original===

“That’s all that I see. I’ve got everything of any size, anyhow.”

===Chinese===

「眼下瞧见的就这些。反正像点样的东西都捞上来了。」

===Original===

“Well, tie them to the rope and we’ll pull them up.”

===Chinese===

「好，把它们拴在绳上，我们拉上来。」

===Original===

How Domlio would comport himself when he saw the clothes was now the important matter. French watched him keenly as the dripping bundle appeared and was carried to a bench in the garage.

===Chinese===

此刻要紧的是，多姆利奥见了这些衣裳会如何自处。那滴滴答答的一包东西吊出井口、被抬到车库长凳上的时候，弗伦奇目光锐利地盯住了他。

===Original===

Though the day’s work had prepared the man for some denouement, he certainly appeared to French to be genuinely amazed when the nature of the find was revealed.

===Chinese===

这一天的动静固然让他对某种收场有了思想准备，可捞到的东西一揭开真相，他在弗伦奇眼里分明是货真价实地惊呆了。

===Original===

“Good Heavens! Inspector! What does this mean?” he cried, squaring his shoulders. “Whose are these and how did you know they were there?”

===Chinese===

「天哪！探长！这是怎么回事？」他喊道，端起了肩膀，「这些东西是谁的？你们又怎么知道它们在这儿？」

===Original===

French turned to the plainclothes men. “Just wait outside the door, will you,” he said, then went on gravely to the other: “That is what I have to ask you, Colonel Domlio.”

===Chinese===

弗伦奇转向两名便衣。「到门外稍等一下，好吗。」他说罢，又神情严肃地转向对方：「这正是我要问您的，多姆利奥上校。」

===Original===

“Me?” The man’s sardonic calm was at last broken. “I know nothing about them. The thing is an absolute surprise to me. I swear it.” His face paled and he looked anxious and worried.

===Chinese===

「问我？」此人讥诮的镇定终于绷不住了。「我对这些一无所知。这件事对我来说全然出乎意料。我发誓。」他脸色发白，神色焦灼不安。

===Original===

“There is something I should tell you,” French continued. “On considering this Berlyn-Pyke case I formed a theory. I don’t say it is correct, but I formed it from the facts I had learnt. According to that theory you took out your car on the night of the tragedy, drove into Ashburton, picked up Mr. Pyke’s coat, waistcoat, trousers, shoes, and certain other things, brought them here and threw them into the well. A moment, please.” He raised his hand as Domlio would have spoken. “Rightly or wrongly, that was my theory. But there was a difficulty. You had stated to the sergeant that you had not gone out that night. I came here and found that that statement was not true. You had been out. Then I made further enquiries and learnt that you had taken out your car. You explained that, but I regret to say that I was unable to accept your explanation. I thought, however, that the presence or absence of these objects in the well would settle the matter. I looked at the well and saw that the cover had recently been moved. Two nights ago Sergeant Daw and I came out, and after trying with a line and fishhooks, we drew up a coat﻿—Pyke’s coat. Now, Colonel, if you wish to make a statement I will give it every consideration, but it is my duty again to warn you that anything you say may be used in evidence against you.”

===Chinese===

「有件事我该告诉您。」弗伦奇接着说，「在琢磨伯林—派克这桩案子时，我形成了一套推想。我不敢说它正确，但它是我根据已知的事实推出来的。按照那套推想，惨案当晚您把车开了出去，驶进阿什伯顿，取了派克先生的上装、背心、裤子、鞋子和另外几样东西，带回这里，扔进了井里。请稍等。」多姆利奥正要开口，他抬手止住。「不管对错，那便是我的推想。可其中有个疙瘩。您先前对警士说过，那晚没有出门。我到这里来，发现那句话不实。您出去过。随后我进一步查访，得知您开过车。您作了解释，但恕我直言，我无法接受。不过我想，井里这些东西的有无，终能一锤定音。我察看了水井，发现井盖新近挪动过。两天前的夜里，我和道警士来过，用钓线和钓钩试了一阵，钓上来一件上装——派克的上装。现在，上校，您若想作陈述，我必定逐一斟酌，但我有责任再次提醒您：您说的任何话，都可能成为对您不利的证据。」

===Original===

“What? Are you charging me with a crime?”

===Chinese===

「什么？你要指控我犯罪？」

===Original===

“Unless you can satisfy me of your innocence you will be charged with complicity in the murder of Stanley Pyke.”

===Chinese===

「除非您能使我相信您是清白的，否则您将以同谋谋杀斯坦利·派克的罪名受到指控。」

===Original===

The Colonel drew a deep breath.

===Chinese===

上校深深吸了一口气。

===Original===

“But, good Heavens! How can I satisfy you? I don’t even know what you have against me, except this extraordinary business which I can make neither head nor tail of. You must know more about it than you have said. Tell me the rest.”

===Chinese===

「可是，老天爷！我怎么能使你相信？我甚至不明白你凭什么抓着我的把柄，只除了这桩莫名其妙的怪事——我实在摸不着一点头脑。你一定还知道些没说出口的。把其余的都告诉我吧。」

===Original===

“You tell me this: Was your statement about the loss of the locket on that night true?”

===Chinese===

「那么您先告诉我：那晚关于丢失小画像的说法，是真是假？」

===Original===

Colonel Domlio did not reply. He seemed to be weighing some problem of overwhelming difficulty. French waited patiently, wondering how far his bluff would carry. At last the colonel spoke.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥上校没有作声。他似乎正在掂量一桩天大的难题。弗伦奇耐心等着，心里琢磨着自己这手虚张声势还能撑多远。上校终于开了口。

===Original===

“I have lied to the sergeant and to you, Inspector, with what I now believe was a mistaken motive. I have been turning over the matter in my mind and I see that I have no alternative but to tell you the truth now or to suffer arrest. Possibly things have gone so far that this cannot be avoided. At all events, I will tell you everything.”

===Chinese===

「我对警士和您都撒了谎，探长。如今想来，那动机是打错了算盘。这件事我在心里翻来覆去想了又想，明白摆在自己面前的只有两条路：现在说出真相，或者束手就擒。事情或许已闹到这一步，在所难免了。也罢，我把一切都告诉您。」

===Original===

“You are not forgetting my warning, Colonel Domlio?”

===Chinese===

「您没有忘记我的提醒吧，多姆利奥上校？」

===Original===

“I am not forgetting it. If I am acting foolishly it is my own lookout. I tried to put you off, Inspector, to save bringing Mrs. Berlyn’s name further into the matter, because, though there was nothing against her character, I was sure you would have bothered her with annoying questions. But, though I thought it right to lie with this object, I don’t feel like risking prison for it.”

===Chinese===

「我没有忘记。我若是行事愚蠢，后果也由我自家承担。我先前想把您敷衍过去，探长，为的是别再让伯林太太的名字牵进这桩案子——她的人品无可指摘，可我断定您准会用些缠人的问题去烦扰她。不过，虽说我以为为着这个目的撒谎不算错，我却犯不上为它去蹲监狱。」

===Original===

“I follow you,” said French.

===Chinese===

「我明白你的意思。」弗伦奇说。

===Original===

“You will remember then what I told you about Mrs. Berlyn, that she had been seeing a good deal first of Pyke and then of myself. I’m sorry to have to drag this in again, but otherwise you wouldn’t understand the situation.

===Chinese===

「那么您总还记得我同您说过的伯林太太的情形——她先是同派克过往甚密，后来又同我过从颇多。我很抱歉不得不再把这事扯进来，可不然您就摸不清这其中的局面。

===Original===

“About﻿—let me see﻿—four months before the tragedy Mrs. Berlyn came out here one afternoon. She said that she had been in London to a lecture on entomology and that she had been so much interested that she had read one or two books on the subject. She said that she knew I was doing some research in it and she wondered whether I would let her come and help me and so learn more. I naturally told her I should be delighted, and she began to come out here quite often. On different occasions she has accompanied me on the moor while I was searching for specimens, and she has spent several afternoons with me in my library mounting butterflies and learning to use the microscope. This went on until the day of the tragedy.”

===Chinese===

「大约——让我想想——惨案前四个月，一天下午，伯林太太到了我这儿。她说她去伦敦听了一场昆虫学的讲座，听得入了迷，又找了一两本这方面的书来读。她说她知道我正在从事这项研究，不知我能否让她来帮帮忙，也好多学一点。我自然告诉她乐意之至，此后她便常到这儿来。她不止一次陪我在荒原上采集标本，也曾在我的书房里消磨过好几个下午，跟我一起制作蝴蝶标本，学着使用显微镜。这样一直持续到惨案发生的那一天。」

===Original===

Colonel Domlio paused, squared his shoulders, and continued:

===Chinese===

多姆利奥上校顿了顿，端起肩膀，继续说道：

===Original===

“On that morning I had received by post a letter addressed in a strange hand and marked ‘Personal.’ It was signed ‘X.Y.Z.’ and said that the writer happened to be walking about four p.m. on the previous Tuesday in the Upper Merton glen at a certain point which he described; that he had seen me with Mrs. Berlyn in my arms; that, having a camera, he had at once taken two photographs, one of which had come out; and that if I cared to have the negative he would sell it for fifty pounds. If I wished to negotiate I was to meet him on the Chagford﻿–﻿Gidleigh road at the gate of Dobson’s Spinney at one o’clock that night. Should I not turn up, the writer would understand that I was not interested and would take his picture to Mrs. Berlyn, who, he thought, would prefer to deal rather than have it handed to Mr. Berlyn.

===Chinese===

「就在那天早上，我收到一封来信，字迹陌生，信封上注明『亲启』。落款是『X.Y.Z.』，信上说，写信人上星期二下午四点钟前后恰好在上默顿山谷里散步，走到了他描述得很清楚的一处地方；说他看见我把伯林太太搂在怀里；说他带着照相机，当即拍了两张照片，其中一张已经冲印出来；又说，倘若我想要那张底片，他可以五十英镑出让。若有意商谈，须于当晚一点钟，到查格福德—吉德利路上多布森小树林的栅门口同他碰面。倘若我不到，写信人便会明白我无意成交，把照片送给伯林太太——依他看来，伯林太太宁可花钱买下，也不愿它落到伯林先生手里。

===Original===

“At first I could not think what was meant; then I remembered what had taken place. We had been, Mrs. Berlyn and I, searching for a certain butterfly at the place and time mentioned. Suddenly she had cried out that she had seen a specimen and she had rushed after it past where I was standing. Just as she reached me she gave a cry and lurched against me. ‘Oh, my ankle!’ she shouted and clung to me. She had twisted her foot in a rabbit hole and she could not put her weight on it. I supported her in my arms for a few seconds, and it must have been at this moment that my blackmailer came on the scene. I laid Mrs. Berlyn down on the grass. She sat quiet for some minutes, then with my arm was able to limp to the car. She said her ankle was not sprained, but only twisted, and that she would be all right in a few hours. Next day when I rang up to enquire she said it was still painful but a good deal better.

===Chinese===

「起初我想不起这是指什么，随后才记起了那天的情形。伯林太太和我，确实在信中所说的时间地点寻找一种蝴蝶。她忽然叫了一声，说看见了一只标本，便从我站立的地方一冲而过追了上去。刚奔回我身边，她叫了一声，整个人跌进我怀里。『哎呀，我的脚踝！』她喊着，紧紧抓住我。她在兔子洞里崴了脚，一点劲也吃不上。我用双臂扶了她几秒钟——想必正是这个时候，我那位敲诈的朋友撞见了这一幕。我把伯林太太放到草地上。她静坐了几分钟，随后靠着我的一条胳膊，一瘸一拐地挪到了汽车旁。她说脚踝没有伤着筋骨，只是拧了一下，几个钟头后就会没事。第二天我打电话去问候，她说还疼，但好多了。

===Original===

“At first I was doubtful whether I should act on the letter; then I thought that if there was a genuine photograph it would be better for me to deal with the owner. I therefore went out to the rendezvous at the time mentioned. But I might have saved myself the trouble, for no one was there. And from that day to this I never heard another word about the affair, nor did I mention it to a soul. Indeed, the Berlyn-Pyke tragedy put it out of my head, and the same thing, I suppose, robbed the photograph of its value.”

===Chinese===

「起初我拿不准该不该照信上办；转念一想，倘若真有那么一张照片，还是同它的主人做个了断为好。我便在信上所说的时间赶到约定的地点。可这趟真是白跑，那儿根本没有人。从那天直到今天，这事我再没听到半点音信，也不曾对任何人提起。说实在的，伯林—派克的惨案一闹，我早把它忘到了脑后；想必也正是那桩惨案，叫那张照片变得一文不值了。」

===Original===

“You told me,” said French, “something about Mrs. Berlyn’s relations with Mr. Pyke and yourself, saying that I would not understand your story otherwise. Just what was in your mind in that?”

===Chinese===

「您方才对我说起伯林太太同派克先生和您自己的交往，还说不讲这些我就听不懂您的故事。」弗伦奇说，「您这么讲，心里究竟是何用意？」

===Original===

“Because these relations complicated the whole situation. Do you not see that? Had everything been normal I could have treated the thing as a joke and shown the photograph to Berlyn. As things were, he would have taken it seriously.”

===Chinese===

「因为这些交往把整个局面搅复杂了。您看不出来吗？倘若一切如常，我尽可以把这事当个笑话，把照片拿给伯林看。可眼下的情形，他是会当真的。」

===Original===

French felt a little puzzled by this statement. If the man were lying, it was just the sort of story he would expect to hear, except for one thing. It was capable of immediate confirmation. If it were not true he would soon get it out of Mrs. Berlyn.

===Chinese===

这番话让弗伦奇有点犯疑。倘若此人在撒谎，这正是他料想中会听到的那套说辞——只有一处例外：它是可以当场查证的。倘若不是真话，他很快就能从伯林太太口中套出真相。

===Original===

“I don’t want to be offensive, Colonel,” he said, “but by your own admission you have twice lied about what took place that night. Can you give me any proof that your present statement is true?”

===Chinese===

「我不想冒犯您，上校，」他说，「可按您自己承认的，关于那晚的事您已经撒过两次谎了。您能否拿出任何证据，证明您这回说的是真话？」

===Original===

Domlio squared his shoulders.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥端起了肩膀。

===Original===

“I can’t,” he admitted. “I can show you the letter and you can ask Mrs. Berlyn, but I don’t know that either of those would constitute proof.”

===Chinese===

「拿不出。」他承认道，「我可以把那封信给您看，您也可以去问伯林太太，只是我说不好，这两样哪一样算得上证据。」

===Original===

“They wouldn’t in themselves, but from either I might get some point which would. Now I’ll tell you what we’ll do. We’ll see first whether Sergeant Daw has made any further discoveries; then you can show me the letter and we’ll drive out to the place where Mrs. Berlyn fell.”

===Chinese===

「单靠它们本身自然算不上，但从里面我或许能抠出某个算得上的要点来。这样吧，我告诉您咱们接下来怎么办。先看看道警士还有没有别的发现；然后您把信拿给我看，咱们再开车到伯林太太摔倒的地方走一趟。」

===Original===

“That’s easily done.”

===Chinese===

「这好办。」

===Original===

They returned to the well, and there, to French’s satisfaction, found the missing duplicator parts laid out on the coping of the wall.

===Chinese===

两人回到井边，只见失踪的复印机零件已摆在井栏的压顶石上——弗伦奇见了，正中下怀。

===Original===

“Excellent, Sergeant. That’s all we want. I take it you will get the pump away? You needn’t wait for me. I’m going out with Colonel Domlio.”

===Chinese===

「好极了，警士。我们要的都已经齐了。泵由你们运回去，对吧？你们不必等我。我同多姆利奥上校要出去一趟。」

===Original===

While Coombe was bringing round the car the two men went to the study for the letter. It was just as the colonel had described, and French could see no clue to the sender.

===Chinese===

库姆去把车开出来的时候，两人到书房里去取那封信。信同上校描述的一模一样，弗伦奇从中找不出半点寄信人的线索。

===Original===

They ran out then to the Upper Merton glen and Domlio pointed out the spot at which the alleged incident had taken place. French insisted on his describing the occurrence in the most minute manner. He wished to form an opinion as to whether the man was relating what he had seen or inventing the details as he went along.

===Chinese===

随后两人驱车来到上默顿山谷，多姆利奥指认了那桩所谓事故发生的地点。弗伦奇坚持要他把经过一五一十、细到毫芒地讲了一遍。他想要有个判断：此人是在复述亲眼所见，还是边讲边现编细节。

===Original===

After half an hour of close questioning on the lines of the American third degree, French had to admit that the affair had either happened as Domlio had said or that it had been rehearsed with great care. On no point was he able to trip the colonel up, and knowing the difficulty of inventing a story in which every detail is foreseen and accounted for, he began to think the tale true. At all events, with the mass of detail he now possessed, a similar examination of Mrs. Berlyn should set the matter at rest.

===Chinese===

照着美国式疲劳讯问的路数紧逼细问了半个钟头之后，弗伦奇不得不承认：这件事要么正如多姆利奥所说发生过，要么就是经过精心排演的。他没有能在任何一点上逮住上校的破绽；又深知要编一个处处有伏笔、事事有交代的故事何其困难，他渐渐觉得这话是真的了。至少，凭他如今掌握的这大量细节，用同样的方式讯问一番伯林太太，此案也该水落石出了。

===Original===

French was in a thoughtful mood as they drove back to Torview. He was up against the same old question which had troubled him so many times in the past. Was his suspect guilty or was he the victim of a plot?

===Chinese===

返回托尔维尤的路上，弗伦奇心事重重。他又撞上了那个老问题——过去多少次困扰过他的老问题：这个嫌疑人究竟有罪，还是一场阴谋的受害者？

===Original===

The evidence against the man was certainly strong. Seven separate facts pointed to his guilt. French ran over them in his mind:

===Chinese===

对此人不利的证据确实雄厚。七件独立的事实都指向他的罪责。弗伦奇在心里一一过了一遍：

===Original===

- 
					

Domlio had the necessary qualifications for partnership in the crime. He knew the dramatis personae and he was acquainted with the works. He could have ordered the duplicator, and arranged for Berlyn and Pyke to visit Tavistock on the night in question.

===Chinese===

- 多姆利奥具备伙同作案的必要条件。他认识剧中一班人马，也熟门熟路进出工厂。复印机可以由他订购，伯林和派克案发当晚的塔维斯托克之行也可以由他安排。

===Original===

- 
					

He was out in his car on that night at the time and for the distance required.

===Chinese===

- 案发当晚，他恰在所需的时间把车开了出去，跑的又恰是所需的距离。

===Original===

- 
					

He had denied this.

===Chinese===

- 对此他曾矢口否认。

===Original===

- 
					

When cornered he had told a false story of a search for a lost locket.

===Chinese===

- 被问得走投无路时，他又编出了寻找丢失小画像的假话。

===Original===

- 
					

The clothes of the dead man had been found in the very place where French imagined Domlio would have hidden them.

===Chinese===

- 死者的衣裳，恰恰是在弗伦奇设想多姆利奥会藏匿的地方找到的。

===Original===

- 
					

There was a quite adequate motive if, as might well be, Domlio was really attached to Mrs. Berlyn.

===Chinese===

- 倘若多姆利奥当真心系伯林太太——这大有可能——那动机便十分充足。

===Original===

- 
					

There was no other person whom French knew of who could have been Berlyn’s confederate.

===Chinese===

- 弗伦奇所知的人中，再无别人能充当伯林的同伙。

===Original===

Many and many a man had been hanged on far less evidence than there was here. With this mass of incriminating facts an arrest was amply justifiable. Indeed, a conviction was almost assured.

===Chinese===

古往今来，多少人头顶的证据远不及这里的雄厚，照样上了绞架。手握这一大堆定罪的事实，实施逮捕绰绰有余。说真的，定罪几乎十拿九稳。

===Original===

On the other hand, every bit of this evidence was circumstantial and could be explained on the assumption of Domlio’s innocence, by supposing him to be the victim of a conspiracy on the part of the real murderer.

===Chinese===

可反过来讲，这证据桩桩件件都是旁证，只消假定多姆利奥清白无辜、是真凶阴谋构陷的替罪羊，便条条解释得通。

===Original===

French wondered if he could make the man reveal his own outlook on the affair.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇琢磨着，能否让此人亮出他自己对此案的看法。

===Original===

“Tell me, Colonel,” he said, “did it not strike you as a strange thing that Mrs. Berlyn should stumble at just the point which ensured her falling into your arms?”

===Chinese===

「跟我说说，上校，」他道，「伯林太太偏偏在那样一个地方绊倒——不早不晚，恰好一头跌进您怀里——您就不觉得这事蹊跷吗？」

===Original===

Domlio slackened speed and looked around aggressively.

===Chinese===

多姆利奥放慢车速，挑衅地回过头来。

===Original===

“Just what do you mean by that?”

===Chinese===

「你这话究竟是什么意思？」

===Original===

“As a matter of fact,” French answered, sweetly, “what I mean is, was the accident genuine or faked?”

===Chinese===

「说实在的，」弗伦奇甜丝丝地答道，「我的意思是：那场意外是真情还是做戏？」

===Original===

The colonel squared his shoulders indignantly.

===Chinese===

上校义愤地端起了肩膀。

===Original===

“I consider that a most unwarrantable remark,” he said, hotly, “and I shall not answer it. I can only suppose your abominable calling has warped your mind and made suspicion a disease with you.”

===Chinese===

「我认为这话无礼至极，」他气冲冲地说，「我不屑回答。我只能猜想，是你们那惹人厌的行当把你的心思扭曲了，疑神疑鬼成了你的痼疾。」

===Original===

French glanced at him keenly. The man was genuinely angry. And if so, it tended in his favour. Real indignation is difficult to simulate and would not be called forth by an imaginary insult.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇锐利地打量他。此人是真动了气。倘若果真如此，倒对他有利。真正的义愤难以伪装，凭空捏造的侮辱也惹不出这样的义愤。

===Original===

“If you think my remark unwarrantable, I shall withdraw it,” French said, with his pleasant smile. “I simply wanted to know whether you yourself believed in it. I think you do. Well, Colonel, I think that’s all we can do tonight. I’m sorry to have given you all this annoyance, but you can see I had no option.”

===Chinese===

「您若觉得我那话无礼，我收回便是。」弗伦奇说，脸上带着他那讨人喜欢的微笑。「我不过想知道，您自己信不信那一层。我想您是信的。好了，上校，今晚咱们能做的就到这里了。抱歉给您添了这许多烦恼，可您看得出，我别无选择。」

===Original===

They had reached the gate of Torview. Domlio stopped the car.

===Chinese===

两人已到托尔维尤的栅门前。多姆利奥停下车。

===Original===

“Then you are not going to arrest me?” he asked, with barely concealed anxiety.

===Chinese===

「那么，你不打算逮捕我了？」他问道，焦虑几乎掩藏不住。

===Original===

“No. Why should I? You have accounted in a reasonable way for the suspicious circumstances. So far as I can see, your explanation is satisfactory. I can’t expect any more.”

===Chinese===

「不。为什么呢？那些可疑的情节，您已经给出了说得通的解释。依我看，您的解释令人满意。我不能奢求更多了。」

===Original===

The colonel gave a sigh of relief.

===Chinese===

上校宽慰地舒了一口气。

===Original===

“To be quite candid,” he admitted, “I scarcely hoped that you would accept it. After what has occurred, I can’t expect you to believe me, but for what it’s worth I give you my word of honour that what I have told you this time is the truth. I may tell you that I have been afraid of this very development ever since the tragedy. How are you getting to Ashburton? Shall I run you in?”

===Chinese===

「老实说，」他坦承道，「我几乎不敢指望您会接受。经过这许多周折，您自然不能信我了——可不管值不值钱，我以名誉向您担保：我这一回告诉您的，句句是实。不妨告诉您，自惨案发生后，我一直怕的就是今天这个局面。您怎么回阿什伯顿？要不要我捎您一程？」

===Original===

“It would be very good of you.”

===Chinese===

「那就叨光了。」

===Original===

It was with considerable uneasiness that French saw Colonel Domlio drive off from the hotel in Ashburton. He had backed his judgment that the man was innocent, but he recognised that he might easily have made a mistake. At the same time Domlio could scarcely escape otherwise than by suicide, and he felt sure that his mind had been so much eased that he would not attempt anything so drastic. As soon, however, as the car was out of sight he walked to the police station and asked Daw to have a watch kept on the man’s movements.

===Chinese===

望着多姆利奥上校的汽车驶离阿什伯顿的旅馆，弗伦奇心里着实放不下。他赌上了自己的判断，认定此人是清白的，可他也明白，这判断很容易出错。好在此人若要脱身，除了自杀别无他途，而他确信上校如今心头一块石头落了地，决不会走这么极端的路。然而汽车刚一消失在视线里，他便走向警察局，要道派人暗中监视此人的动静。

## XVI Certainty at Last / XVI 终获确证

===Original===

That night as French was writing up his diary the question he had asked Domlio recurred to him. “Tell me, Colonel,” he had said, “did it not strike you as strange that Mrs. Berlyn should stumble at just the point which ensured her falling into your arms?” He had asked it to test the colonel’s belief in the incident. Now it occurred to him that on its merits it required an answer.

===Chinese===

那天夜里，弗伦奇正写着日记，先前问过多姆利奥的那个问题又浮上心头。「告诉我，上校，」他当时是这样说的，「伯林太太偏偏在那样一个位置绊倒，不偏不倚正好跌进您怀里，您不觉得蹊跷吗？」他问这话，原是要试探上校对那一幕信到几分。此刻他再一想，就事论事，这个问题本身也确实需要一个答案。

===Original===

Had the incident stood alone it might well have passed unquestioned. But it was not alone. Two other matters must be considered in conjunction with it.

===Chinese===

倘若这件事是孤零零发生的，很可能就此无人置疑。但它并非孤立。还有两桩事必须同它放在一起考量。

===Original===

First there was the coincidence that at the precise moment a watcher armed with a camera should be present. What accident should take a photographer to this secluded glen just when so compromising a tableau should be staged? Was there here an element of design?

===Chinese===

其一，恰好在那一刻，现场竟会有一名身怀照相机的旁观者，这未免太巧。什么样的偶然，会把一个摆弄相机的人，恰恰在这一幅足以败坏名节的画面上演之时，带进这条僻静的小山谷？这里面有没有人为设计的成分？

===Original===

Secondly, there was the consideration that if suspicion were to be thrown on Domlio he must be made to take out his car secretly on the fatal night. And how better could this be done than by the story of the photograph? Once again, did this not suggest design?

===Chinese===

其二，倘若要把嫌疑引到多姆利奥头上，就必须设法让他在案发当夜偷偷把车开出去。而要办到这一点，还有什么法子比那张照片的故事更顺手的？这岂不又一次透出人为设计的痕迹？

===Original===

If so, something both interesting and startling followed. Mrs. Berlyn was privy to it. And if she were privy to it, was she not necessarily implicated in the murder? Could she even be the accomplice for whom he, French, had been searching?

===Chinese===

倘若果真如此，一件既有趣又骇人的事便随之而来：伯林太太是知情的。而她既知情，岂不必然与这桩凶案有牵连？她甚至会不会正是他弗伦奇一直搜寻的那个同谋？

===Original===

There was, of course, her alibi. If she had been at the party at her house at ten o’clock she could not have drugged Gurney’s tea. But was she at her house?

===Chinese===

当然，她还有不在场证明。倘若十点钟时她确在自己家中的聚会上，就不可能在格尼的茶里下药。可她当时真在自己家里吗？

===Original===

Experience had made French sceptical about alibis. This one certainly seemed watertight, and yet was it not just possible that Mrs. Berlyn had managed to slip away from her guests for the fifteen or twenty minutes required?

===Chinese===

多年的经验使弗伦奇对不在场证明素来不敢轻信。这一条看上去固然滴水不漏，可伯林太太会不会恰恰就有本事从宾客中间抽身溜开那么一刻钟二十分钟呢？

===Original===

It was evident that the matter must be tested forthwith, and French decided that, having already questioned Mr. Fogden, he would interview the Dr. and Mrs. Lancaster whom Lizzie Johnston had mentioned as also being members of the party. They lived on the Buckland road half a mile beyond the Berlyns’, and next morning French called on them.

===Chinese===

此事显然必须立即查证。弗伦奇既已问过福格登先生，便决定再去走访莉齐·约翰斯顿提到过、当晚同样与会的兰开斯特医生夫妇。他们住在巴克兰路上，过了伯林家再行半英里。第二天一早，弗伦奇便登门造访。

===Original===

Dr. Lancaster, he had learned from Daw, was a newcomer to the town, a young LL.D. who had been forced by a breakdown in health to give up his career at the bar. He received French at once.

===Chinese===

他从道口中得知，兰开斯特医生是新近迁来本镇的，一位年轻的法律博士（LL.D.），因健康垮了而被迫弃了律师生涯。他立刻接待了弗伦奇。

===Original===

“I want to find out whether any member of the party could have left the house about ten o’clock for fifteen or twenty minutes,” French explained. “Do you think that you or Mrs. Lancaster could help me out?”

===Chinese===

「我想查明，当晚聚会的宾客中，有没有人可能在十点前后离开那座宅子一刻钟到二十分钟，」弗伦奇说明来意，「您和兰开斯特太太能不能帮我这个忙？」

===Original===

“I can only speak for myself,” Dr. Lancaster smiled. “I was there all the time, and I’m sure so was Mrs. Lancaster. But I’ll call her and you can ask her.”

===Chinese===

「我只能替自己作保，」兰开斯特医生微笑道，「我自始至终都在，兰开斯特太太肯定也一样。不过我去把她请来，您亲自问她。」

===Original===

“A moment, please. Surely you can speak for more than yourself? Were you not with the others?”

===Chinese===

「请稍等。您总不至于只替得了自己一个人吧？您不是同旁人在一起的吗？」

===Original===

“With some of them. You see, what happened was this. When we went in, Mrs. Berlyn said that she had been disappointed in that three London friends, who were staying at Torquay and whom she expected, had just telegraphed to say they couldn’t come. That made our numbers wrong. She had intended to have three tables of bridge, but now, as some of us played billiards, she suggested one bridge table and snooker for the other five. She and I and﻿—let me see﻿—Fogden and a Miss Pym, I think, and one other﻿—I’m blessed if I can remember who the other was﻿—played snooker. So I wasn’t with the other four between the time that we settled down to play and supper.”

===Chinese===

「同其中几位。是这样，当时的情形您听我说。我们进门之后，伯林太太说有一桩扫兴事：三位原定从托基赶来的伦敦朋友刚拍来电报，说来不成了。这一来人数就不凑了。她本打算摆三桌桥牌，如今既然我们中间有几位打台球，她便提议设一桌桥牌，其余五人打斯诺克。她同我，还有——让我想想——福格登，一位皮姆小姐，我记得是，再有一位——真见鬼，另一位究竟是谁，我实在想不起来了——就打斯诺克。所以从落座开打到夜宵之间，我都不曾同那四个人在一起。」

===Original===

“What hour was supper?”

===Chinese===

「夜宵是几点？」

===Original===

“About half past ten, I think. We broke up when it was over﻿—rather early, as a matter of fact. We reached home shortly after eleven.”

===Chinese===

「我想大约十点半吧。夜宵一完，聚会就散了——其实散得算早的。我们到家是十一点刚过。」

===Original===

“And you played snooker all the evening until supper?”

===Chinese===

「那么你们一晚上打的都是斯诺克，一直打到夜宵？」

===Original===

“No. After an hour or more we dropped it and played four-handed billiards.”

===Chinese===

「不是。打了一个多钟头之后我们就撂下了，改打四人台球。」

===Original===

“Then some player must have stood out?”

===Chinese===

「那就必定有人轮空了？」

===Original===

“Yes. Mrs. Berlyn said she must go and see how the others were getting along. She watched us play for some time, then went to the drawing room. She came back after a few minutes to say that supper was ready.”

===Chinese===

「是的。伯林太太说她得去看看其余那几位玩得怎么样。她瞧了我们一会儿，就到客厅去了。几分钟后她回来说，夜宵备好了。」

===Original===

“Now, Dr. Lancaster, just one other question. Can you tell me at what time Mrs. Berlyn went into the drawing room?”

===Chinese===

「好了，兰开斯特医生，再问最后一个问题。您能告诉我，伯林太太是几点进的客厅吗？」

===Original===

“I really don’t think I can. I wasn’t paying special attention to her movements. I should say perhaps half an hour before supper, but I couldn’t be sure.”

===Chinese===

「我看真说不上来。我并没有特别留意她的动向。要说嘛，大概是夜宵前半个钟头，可我不敢打包票。」

===Original===

“That’s all right,” said French. “Now if I could see Mrs. Lancaster for a moment I should be done.”

===Chinese===

「不要紧，」弗伦奇说，「现在若能见一见兰开斯特太太，我的事就齐了。」

===Original===

Mrs. Lancaster was a dark, vivacious little woman who seemed to remember the evening in question much more clearly than did her husband.

===Chinese===

兰开斯特太太是位肤色黝黑、活泼娇小的女人，对案发那晚的记忆，似乎比她丈夫清楚得多。

===Original===

“Yes,” she said, “I was playing bridge with Miss Lucy Pym, Mr. Cowls, and Mr. Leacock. I remember Mrs. Berlyn coming in about ten. She laughed and said: ‘Oh, my children, don’t be frightened. I couldn’t think of disturbing such a serious game. I’ll go back to our snooker.’ She went away, and presently came back and called us to the library to supper.”

===Chinese===

「记得，」她说，「我当时同露西·皮姆小姐、考尔斯先生和利科克先生打桥牌。我记得伯林太太十点钟左右进来。她笑着说：『哟，孩子们，别害怕。这么正经的一局，我怎么舍得搅扰。我还是回我们那桌斯诺克去。』她走了，过不多时又回来，招呼我们到书房用夜宵。」

===Original===

“How long was she away, Mrs. Lancaster?”

===Chinese===

「她离开了多久，兰开斯特太太？」

===Original===

“About twenty minutes, I should think.”

===Chinese===

「我想总有二十分钟吧。」

===Original===

This seemed to French to be all that he wanted. However, he thought it wise to get the key of the Berlyns’ house and have a look at the layout. The drawing room was in front, with the library behind it, but between the two there was a passage with a side door leading into the garden. He felt satisfied as to the use to which that passage had been put on the night in question. He could picture Mrs. Berlyn fixing up the uneven number of guests, among whom would be some who played billiards and some who did not. The proposals for snooker and bridge would almost automatically follow, involving the division of the party in two rooms. Mrs. Berlyn as hostess would reasonably be the odd man out when the change was made from snooker to billiards. The result of these arrangements would be that when she slipped out to the works through the side door, each party would naturally assume she was with the other, while if any question as to this arose, her reentry at suppertime would suggest to both that she had gone out to overlook its preparation.

===Chinese===

在弗伦奇看来，这似乎已是他要的一切。不过他行事周密，觉得还是应当取来伯林家的钥匙，把屋内格局看一看。客厅在前，书房在后，两者之间有一条过道，过道上开着一扇侧门通向花园。案发之夜那条过道派了什么用场，他心中已有几分了然。他可以想见伯林太太如何张罗这个不成双的客数——客人之中有会打台球的，也有不会的——于是斯诺克加桥牌的提议便几乎顺理成章地跟着出来，宾客也就分作两屋。等到斯诺克改打台球，身为女主人的伯林太太理所当然便是轮空的那一个。这一番安排的结局便是：她从侧门溜出去到工厂时，两屋人各自想当然，以为她在另一屋；而万一有人问起，她赶在夜宵时分回到屋来，又会叫双方都以为，她是出去照看夜宵的备置了。

===Original===

These discoveries justified French’s theory, but they did not prove it, and he racked his brains for some test which would definitely establish the point.

===Chinese===

这些发现为弗伦奇的推断提供了佐证，却还不足以证实它。他绞尽脑汁，想寻出一个能一锤定音的检验办法。

===Original===

At last an idea occurred to him which he thought might at least help.

===Chinese===

终于，他想出了一个主意，觉得它至少能帮上点忙。

===Original===

In considering Mrs. Berlyn as her husband’s accomplice he had been doubtful whether there would have been sufficient time for the various actions. If after Berlyn’s arrival at the works with the body Mrs. Berlyn had driven the car back to where it was found, changed the magneto, and made the footprints, he did not believe she could have walked home in time to wake the servants at the hour stated. Nor did he believe that Berlyn, after disposing of the body in the works, could have been able on foot to make Domlio’s in time to hide the clothes in the well before the sergeant’s call.

===Chinese===

在设想伯林太太充当丈夫的同谋时，他心里一直存着一个疑团：各项动作，时间够不够用。倘若伯林载着尸体到达工厂之后，伯林太太把车开回它被发现的地方，换掉磁电机，又伪造了那些脚印，他不信她还能来得及在所说的钟点走回家去叫醒仆人。他同样不信，伯林在厂里处理完尸体之后，还能靠双脚及时赶到多姆利奥家，赶在警士登门之前把衣裳藏进井里。

===Original===

He now wondered whether Mrs. Berlyn’s bicycle could have been pressed into the service. Could the lady have brought the machine to the works, lifted it into the tonneau of the car, carried it out on the moor, and ridden back on it to the works? And could her husband have used it to reach first Domlio’s and then Plymouth or some other large town from which he had escaped?

===Chinese===

他此刻想到，伯林太太那辆自行车会不会也被派上了用场。这位太太会不会把车带到工厂，搬进汽车后厢，载到荒原上，再骑着它返回工厂？她丈夫又会不会骑着它先到多姆利奥家，再前往普利茅斯或别的什么大城镇，从那里遁走？

===Original===

To test the matter, French returned to Lizzie Johnston and asked her if she knew what had become of the bicycle.

===Chinese===

为查证此事，弗伦奇又去找莉齐·约翰斯顿，问她可知道那辆自行车的下落。

===Original===

But the girl could not tell him. Nor could she recall when or where she had seen it last. She supposed it had been sold at the auction, but in the excitement of that time she had not noticed it.

===Chinese===

可姑娘说不上来。她连最后一次见到它是在何时何地也想不起来了。她估摸着是在拍卖会上卖掉了，只是那阵子乱哄哄的，她没留意。

===Original===

“Where did Mrs. Berlyn get it, do you know?”

===Chinese===

「伯林太太那辆车是从哪儿买的，你知道吗？」

===Original===

“From Makepeace’s. He has bicycles same as motors. He’ll tell you about it.”

===Chinese===

「从梅克皮斯那儿。他既卖汽车也卖自行车。他会跟您说清楚的。」

===Original===

Half an hour later French was talking to Mr. Makepeace. He remembered having some five years earlier sold the machine to Mrs. Berlyn. He looked up his records, and after considerable trouble found a note of the transaction. The bicycle was a Swift, and number 35,721. It had certain dimensions and peculiarities of which he gave French details.

===Chinese===

半个钟头后，弗伦奇已同梅克皮斯先生谈上了。他记得大约五年前卖过伯林太太一辆自行车。他翻查账目，费了老大一番劲，才找到那笔交易的记录。那辆车是斯威夫特牌（Swift），编号35,721。车身有几处尺寸和特征，他都向弗伦奇一一说明。

===Original===

French’s next call was on the auctioneer who had conducted the sale of the Berlyn effects. Mr. Nankivell appeared au fait with the whole case and was obviously thrilled to meet French. He made no difficulty about giving the required information. A bicycle had not been among the articles auctioned, nor had he seen one during his visits to the house.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇接着去拜访主持伯林家财物拍卖的拍卖商。南基维尔先生（Mr. Nankivell）对全案显然了如指掌，见到弗伦奇兴奋之情溢于言表。所需的情况，他毫无难色地和盘托出。拍卖品里没有自行车，他几次上门看货时，也不曾见过一辆。

===Original===

This was all very well as far as it went, but it was negative. French wanted to find someone who could say definitely what had happened to the machine. He consulted with Sergeant Daw and at last came to the conclusion that if Peter Swann, the gardener-chauffeur, could be found, he might be able to give the information. Daw believed he had gone to Chagford, and he telephoned to the sergeant there, asking him to make enquiries.

===Chinese===

事情到此都还算顺，只是全属消极的否定。弗伦奇要找的是一个能确切说出那辆车下落的人。他同道警士商量，最后得出结论：倘若能找到那个身兼司机的花匠彼得·斯旺，或许就能问出消息。道估计他去了查格福德，便打电话给那边的警士，请他代为查访。

===Original===

In the afternoon there was a reply to the effect that the man was employed by a market gardener near Chagford, and French at once took a car over to see him. Swann remembered the bicycle well, as he had had to keep it clean. He had seen it in the woodshed on the day before the tragedy, but next morning it was gone. He had looked for it particularly, as he wished to use it to take a message to the town and he had wondered where it could have got to. He had never seen it again. He had not asked about it, as he had not considered that his business.

===Chinese===

下午有了回音，说那人受雇于查格福德附近一位商品菜农。弗伦奇当即雇车前去见他。斯旺对那辆自行车记得清清楚楚，因为擦拭保养的活计归他管。案发前一天，他还见它在柴棚里，可第二天早晨就不见了。他还特地找过，因为想骑着它进城送个信，纳闷它究竟能跑到哪儿去。此后他再没见过它。他也没有问起，觉得那不关自己的事。

===Original===

Once again French experienced the keen delight of finding his deductions justified by the event. In this whole case he had really excelled himself. On several different points he had imagined what might have occurred, and on a test being made, his idea had been proved correct. Some work that! As he did not fail to remind himself, it showed the highest type of ability.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇又一次尝到推想被事实验证的锐利喜悦。在这桩案子里，他确乎超水平发挥。好几个不同的关节上，他都设想了可能发生过什么，而一经检验，他的想法便被证明无误。这可不是等闲的功夫！他也不忘提醒自己：这显示出的是最高一等的才干。

===Original===

The next thing was to find the bicycle. He returned for the night to Ashburton, and next morning went down to see the superintendent of police at Plymouth. That officer listened with interest to his story and promised to have a search made without delay. When he had rung up and asked for similar enquiries to be made in the other large towns within a cycle ride of the moor, French found himself at a loose end.

===Chinese===

下一步便是找到那辆自行车。他当夜返回阿什伯顿，第二天早晨南下普利茅斯，去见当地的警司。那位警官饶有兴味地听完他的叙述，答应即刻安排搜查。待他打过电话、请荒原自行车行程之内的其他大城镇一体查访之后，弗伦奇便闲了下来。

===Original===

“You should have a look round the place,” the superintendent advised. “There’s a lot to see in Plymouth.”

===Chinese===

「您该在这地方四处走走，」警司劝道，「普利茅斯可看的东西不少。」

===Original===

French took the advice and went for a stroll round the city. He was not impressed by the streets, though he admired St. Andrew’s Church, the Guildhall, and some of the other buildings in the same locality. But when, after wandering through some more or less uninteresting residential streets, he unexpectedly came out on the Hoe, he held his breath. The promenade along the top of the cliff was imposing enough, though no better than he had seen many times before. But the view of the Sound was unique. The sea, light blue in the morning sun, stretched from the base of the cliff beneath his feet out past Drake’s Island and the long line of the Breakwater to a clear horizon. On the right was Mount Edgcumbe, tree clad to the water’s edge, while far away out to the southwest was the faint white pillar of the Eddystone lighthouse. French gazed and admired, then going down to the Sutton Pool, he explored the older part of the town for the best part of an hour.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇从谏如流，到城里闲逛了一圈。街市没能让他动容，不过圣安德鲁教堂、市政厅（Guildhall）以及同一地段的另几座建筑，倒也博得他的赞赏。可是，在几条多少有些乏味的住宅街道间穿行之后，他出其不意地走上霍伊高地（the Hoe），不由屏住了呼吸。崖顶的滨海步道固然壮观，却也并不胜过他以往见过许多回的那些。唯独这海湾的景致无可匹俦。大海在晨光里泛着浅蓝，从脚下崖脚一路铺展开去，越过德雷克岛和防波堤的长线，直抵清晰的天际。右手是埃奇库姆山（Mount Edgcumbe），林木葱茏，直披水边；而远远的西南方，是埃迪斯通灯塔（Eddystone lighthouse）那根淡淡的白色柱身。弗伦奇眺望赞叹，随后下到萨顿湾（Sutton Pool），在老城区盘桓了大半个钟头。

===Original===

When he presently reached the police station he was delighted to find that news had just then come in. The bicycle had been found. It had been pawned by a man, apparently a labourer, shortly after the shop opened on the morning of Tuesday, the 16th August; the morning, French reminded himself delightedly, after the crime. The man had stated that the machine was his daughter’s and had been given two pounds on it. He had not returned since, nor had the machine been redeemed.

===Chinese===

待他回到警察局，喜滋滋地发现消息恰好刚到。自行车找到了。八月十六日星期二早晨，铺子刚开门不久，有个男人把它当掉了——看模样是个做工的；弗伦奇暗自欢喜地记起，那正是案发后的第一个早晨。那人声称车是他女儿的，当了两个英镑。此后他再没露面，车也没有赎回。

===Original===

“We’re trying to trace the man, but after this lapse of time I don’t suppose we shall be able,” the superintendent declared. “I expect this Berlyn abandoned the machine when he reached Plymouth, and our friend found it and thought he had better make hay while the sun shone.”

===Chinese===

「我们正尽力追查那个人，不过隔了这么久，我看是查不到了，」警司断言，「我料想这位伯林到达普利茅斯后就把车扔了，咱们这位朋友撞见了它，觉得机不可失，不如趁机捞上一把。」

===Original===

“So likely that I don’t think it matters whether you find him or not,” French returned.

===Chinese===

「十有八九是这么回事，所以找不找得到他，我看都无关紧要，」弗伦奇答道。

===Original===

“I agree, but we shall have a shot at it, all the same. By the way, Mr. French, it’s a curious thing that you should call today. Only yesterday I was talking to a friend of yours﻿—an ass, if you don’t mind my saying so, but married to one of the most delightful young women I’ve ever come across. Lives at Dartmouth.”

===Chinese===

「我同意，不过无论如何，我们总要试上一试。对了，弗伦奇先生，您今天来可真巧。就在昨天，我还同您的一位朋友聊天——恕我直言，那是头蠢驴，可娶了我平生所见最讨人喜欢的一位年轻女子。住在达特茅斯（Dartmouth）。」

===Original===

“Dartmouth?” French laughed. “That gives me a clue. You mean that cheery young optimist, Maxwell Cheyne? He is an ass right enough, but he’s not a bad soul at bottom. And the girl’s a stunner. How are they getting along?”

===Chinese===

「达特茅斯？」弗伦奇笑了起来，「这一说我就有数了。您是说那位乐呵呵的年轻乐天派，麦克斯韦·切恩（Maxwell Cheyne）吧？他确是一头蠢驴，不过骨子里人不坏。那姑娘可是个大美人。他们近来过得怎么样？」

===Original===

“Tip-top. He’s taken to writing tales. Doing quite well with them, too, I believe. They’re very popular down there, both of them.”

===Chinese===

「好得很。他搞起写小说来了。听说还写得挺不坏。他们俩在那边都极有人缘。」

===Original===

“Glad to hear it. Well, Superintendent, I must be getting along. Thanks for your help.”

===Chinese===

「听您这么说我很高兴。好了，警司，我该上路了。多谢帮忙。」

===Original===

French was full of an eager optimism as the result of these discoveries. The disappearance of the bicycle, added to the breakdown of the alibi, seemed definitely to prove his theory of Mrs. Berlyn’s complicity.

===Chinese===

这些发现让弗伦奇满心热切的乐观。自行车的不翼而飞，加上不在场证明的崩塌，似乎确凿地证实了他关于伯林太太涉案的推断。

===Original===

But when he considered the identity of the person whom Mrs. Berlyn had thus assisted, he had to admit himself staggered. That Berlyn had murdered Pyke had seemed an obvious theory. Now French was not so certain of it. The lady had undoubtedly been in love with Pyke. Surely it was too much to suppose she would help her husband to murder her lover?

===Chinese===

可是一想到伯林太太这般出力相助的究竟是谁，他就不能不承认自己被震住了。伯林谋杀派克——这本是摆在明面上的推断。此刻弗伦奇却不那么有把握了。这位太太无疑曾与派克相爱。要说她会帮着丈夫谋害自己的情人，未免太说不过去。

===Original===

Had it been the other way round, had Phyllis and Pyke conspired to kill Berlyn, the thing would have been easier to understand. Wife and lover against husband was a common enough combination. But the evidence against this idea was strong. Not only was there the identification of the clothes and birthmark, but there was the strong presumption that the man who disposed of the crate in Wales was Berlyn. At the same time this evidence of identification was not quite conclusive, and French determined to keep the possibility in view and test it rigorously as occasion offered.

===Chinese===

倘若倒过来，是菲莉斯同派克合谋杀掉伯林，事情倒还容易理解些。妻子加情人对付丈夫，本是十分常见的组合。可是反对这一设想的证据也很硬。不仅有衣裳和胎记的同一认定，而且有很强的推定：在威尔士处理板条箱的那人就是伯林。不过与此同时，这些同一认定的证据也并非全然铁案如山，弗伦奇决意把这个可能性放在心上，一有机会便严加检验。

===Original===

And then another factor occurred to him, an extremely disturbing factor, which bade fair to change his whole view of the case. He saw that even if Pyke had murdered Berlyn it would not clear up the situation. In fact, this new idea suggested that it was impossible either that Pyke could have murdered Berlyn or that Berlyn could have murdered Pyke.

===Chinese===

接着又有一个因素浮上心头——一个极其搅人的因素，眼看要把他对全案的看法整个翻个身。他看出，即便真是派克谋杀了伯林，也理不清这团乱麻。事实上，这个新念头表明：派克杀伯林不可能，伯林杀派克也不可能。

===Original===

What, he asked himself, must have been the motive for such a crime? Certainly not merely to gratify a feeling of hate. The motive undoubtedly was to enable the survivor to claim Phyllis as his wife and to live with her in good social standing and without fear of his rival. But the crime, French reminded himself, had a peculiar feature. The staged accident on the moor involved the disappearance of *both* actors, the murderer as well as the victim. If, then, the murderer disappeared, he could not live with Phyllis. If either Berlyn or Pyke were guilty, therefore, he had carried out the crime in a way which robbed him of the very results for which he had committed it.

===Chinese===

他问自己：这样一桩罪案，动机必然是什么？当然决不只是泄一时之恨。动机无疑是要让活下来的那一个得以名正言顺地娶菲莉斯为妻，体体面面地同她共同生活，又不必惧怕情敌。可是这桩罪案，弗伦奇提醒自己，有一个独特之处：荒原上那出假戏要求*两个*当事人一齐消失——凶手同被害人一起。而凶手既已消失，便无从同菲莉斯共同生活。因此，无论伯林还是派克谁是凶手，他作案的方式恰恰夺走了他犯罪所要换取的成果。

===Original===

French saw that he was up against a puzzling dilemma. If Berlyn had murdered Pyke, it was unlikely that Mrs. Berlyn would have assisted. If, on the other hand, Pyke had murdered Berlyn, Mrs. Berlyn’s action was clear, but not Pyke’s, for Pyke could get nothing out of it.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇明白，自己撞上了一道费解的难题。倘若是伯林谋杀了派克，伯林太太未必肯从旁协助；反过来，倘若是派克谋杀了伯林，伯林太太的所作所为说得通了，派克的却说不通，因为派克从中一无所得。

===Original===

French swore bitterly as he realised that in all probability his former view of the case was incorrect and that he was once again without any really satisfactory theory on which to work. Nor did some hours’ thought point the way to a solution of his problem.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇狠狠地咒骂了一声，因为他意识到，自己先前对案情的看法多半错了，他又一次拿不出任何真正站得住的推断来着手工作。苦思了几个钟头，也照不出一条解题的路。

===Original===

At least, however, he saw his next step. Mrs. Berlyn was the accomplice of *someone*. That someone was doubtless alive and biding his time until he thought it safe to join the lady. If so, she was pretty sure to know his whereabouts. Could she be made to reveal it?

===Chinese===

不过，下一步他至少是看清了。伯林太太是*某个人*的同谋。那个人无疑还活着，正蛰伏待时，只等自认为可以安然同这位太太会合。倘若如此，她多半准知道他的下落。能不能撬开她的口？

===Original===

French thought that if in some way he could give her a thorough fright, she might try to get a warning through. It would then be up to him to intercept her message, which would give him the information he required.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇想，倘若能设法把她狠狠吓上一吓，她多半会设法给对方递个风声。那时便该他上场，截下她的消息——那消息正能给他所要的情报。

===Original===

This meant London. He slept the night in Plymouth, and next day, which was Saturday, travelled up to Paddington.

===Chinese===

这就得去伦敦了。他在普利茅斯过了一夜，第二天是星期六，便乘车北上帕丁顿。

## XVII “Danger!” / XVII 「危险！」

===Original===

Before leaving Plymouth French had wired to Mrs. Berlyn, asking for an interview for the following Monday morning. On reaching the Yard he found a reply. If he called round about half past ten the lady would see him. He rang his bell for Sergeant Carter.

===Chinese===

离开普利茅斯之前，弗伦奇便给伯林太太拍了电报，求下星期一上午一见。他一到苏格兰场就看到了回电：倘若他十点半前后登门，那位太太愿意见他。他摇铃叫来卡特警士。

===Original===

“I shall want you with me today, Carter,” he explained. “Have a taxi ready at ten-fifteen.”

As they were driving toward Chelsea he explained the business.

===Chinese===

「卡特，今天我要你跟我一道去。」他交代道。「十点一刻备好一辆出租汽车。」

车子往切尔西驶去的路上，他把这桩差事交代了一遍。

===Original===

“It’s to help me to shadow a woman, a Mrs. Phyllis Berlyn. Lives at 70b Park Walk. There’s her photograph. When I go in, you keep this taxi and be ready to pick me up when I want you.”

===Chinese===

「是帮我盯一个女人的梢——菲莉斯·伯林太太，住在公园巷70b号。这是她的照片。我进去的时候，你守着这辆车子，我要用车随叫随到。」

===Original===

If he were to tap a possible SOS, he must begin by finding out if his victim had a telephone. He therefore got out at the end of Park Walk, and passing the house, turned into an entry leading to the lane which ran along behind the row. The absence of wires front and rear showed that the house was not connected up. Then he went to the door and knocked. Mrs. Berlyn received him at once.

===Chinese===

要想截获她可能发出的求救信号，第一步先得弄清目标家里有没有电话。于是他在公园巷巷口下了车，走过那宅子，拐进一条过道，通到沿这排屋后身的一条小巷。屋前屋后都不见电线，可见这宅子并没装电话。随后他才上前叫门。伯林太太立刻接待了他。

===Original===

“I am very sorry, madame,” he began, gravely, “to have to come on serious and unpleasant business. In my enquiries into the death of Mr. Pyke certain facts have come out. These facts require an explanation, and they point to you as being perhaps the only person who can give it. I have, therefore, called to ask you some questions, but I have to warn you that you are not bound to answer them, as in certain eventualities anything you say might be used against you.”

===Chinese===

「夫人，非常抱歉，」他神情凝重地开口道，「我这次登门，是为着一桩严重而不愉快的事。在我调查派克先生死亡一案的过程中，查明了若干事实。这些事实需要有人解释，而它们指向您——也许唯有您能作出解释。因此我前来向您提几个问题。但我必须提醒您，您没有义务回答，因为在某些情况下，您所说的任何话都可能被用来对您不利。」

===Original===

Mrs. Berlyn looked startled.

“Whatever do you mean, Inspector?” she demanded. “You don’t mean against me personally, I suppose, but against my husband? I do not forget the terrible suggestion you made.”

===Chinese===

伯林太太显出吃惊的样子。

「探长，您这话究竟是什么意思？」她质问道。「您总不会是指对我本人不利吧——我猜，是针对我丈夫的？您先前那个可怕的猜疑，我可没有忘记。」

===Original===

“I mean against you personally, madam. As I say, I want an explanation of certain facts. If you care to give it I shall hear it with attention, but if you would prefer to consult a solicitor first, you can do so.”

===Chinese===

「正是对您本人，夫人。我说了，我要的是对某些事实的解释。您若愿意作出解释，我会凝神细听；您若想先请教律师，也悉听尊便。」

===Original===

“Good gracious! Inspector, you are terrifying me! You are surely not suggesting that you suspect me of complicity in this awful crime?”

“I make no accusations. All I want is answers to my questions.”

===Chinese===

「天哪！探长，您这是要吓死我啊！您该不会是说，您怀疑我同这桩可怕的罪行有牵连吧？」

「我并不指控谁。我只要我的问题得到回答。」

===Original===

Mrs. Berlyn grew slowly dead white. She moistened her dry lips.

“This is terrible,” she said in low tones. French had some twinges of conscience, for, after all, he was only bluffing. He recognised, however, that the greater the effect he produced, the more likely he was to get what he wanted. He therefore continued his third degree.

===Chinese===

伯林太太的脸色渐渐变得死白。她舔了舔干涩的嘴唇。

「这太可怕了。」她低声说。弗伦奇良心上颇有些不安，毕竟他不过是在唬人。然而他也明白，唬得越狠，越有可能得到自己要的东西。于是他继续他的连番逼问。

===Original===

“If you are innocent, madam, I can assure you that you have nothing to fear,” he encouraged her, thereby naturally increasing her perturbation. “Now would you like to answer my questions or not?”

===Chinese===

「夫人，您若是清白的，我向您保证，您没有什么可怕的。」他这番宽慰的话，自然反倒加重了她的慌乱。「那么现在，您愿不愿意回答我的问题？」

===Original===

She did not hesitate. “I have no option,” she exclaimed in somewhat shaky tones. “If I do not do so your suspicions will be confirmed. Ask what you like. I have nothing to hide and therefore cannot give myself away.”

===Chinese===

她没有迟疑。「我别无选择。」她的声调有些发颤。「我不回答，您的怀疑就算坐实了。您想问什么就问吧。我没什么见不得人的事，也就谈不上露什么马脚。」

===Original===

“I am glad to hear you say so,” French declared, grimly. “First I want you to give me a more detailed account of your relations with Colonel Domlio.”

===Chinese===

「您肯这样讲，我很高兴。」弗伦奇冷冷说道。「首先，我要您更详细地谈谈您同多姆利奥上校的交往。」

===Original===

“Why,” the lady explained, “I told you all about that on your last visit. Colonel Domlio was very friendly, exceedingly friendly, I might say. But we had no relations”﻿—she stressed the word﻿—“in the sense which your question seems to indicate.”

===Chinese===

「咦，」这位太太解释道，「上回您来的时候，我不是原原本本都告诉您了吗？多姆利奥上校同我们很友好——可以说是极其友好。但我们之间并无——」她特意咬重了这个字眼——「『关系』，绝没有您这个问题似乎暗示的那种意思的关系。」

===Original===

“How did your friendship begin?”

“Through my husband. He and Colonel Domlio were old friends and it was natural that we should see something of him. He visited at our house and we at his.”

===Chinese===

「你们的友谊是怎么开始的？」

「通过我丈夫。他同多姆利奥上校是老朋友，我们两家有些来往是自然而然的事。他到我们家做客，我们也到他府上拜访。」

===Original===

“That was when you first went to Ashburton, was it not?”

“Not only then. It was so all the time I lived there.”

===Chinese===

「那是在你们初到阿什伯顿的时候，对不对？」

「不光是那时候。我住在那儿的整个期间，来往一直如此。」

===Original===

“But I don’t mean that. I understand that about four months before the tragedy your friendship became much more intense, if I may use the word?”

===Chinese===

「但我指的不是这个。据我了解，惨案发生前大约四个月，你们的友谊变得亲热得多了——请恕我使用这样的字眼？」

===Original===

“Intense is certainly not the word, but it is true that we met more frequently after the time you mention. I thought I had explained that. It was then that my husband became dissatisfied about my perfectly harmless friendship with Mr. Pyke. As I told you, Mr. Pyke and I decided to see less of each other. I was therefore thrown more on my own resources and frankly I was bored. I filled a little more of the time with Colonel Domlio than formerly. That is all.”

===Chinese===

「『亲热』二字绝对谈不到，不过在您说的那个时候以后，我们见面确实勤了些。这一点我自以为已经解释过了。正是在那个时候，我丈夫对我同派克先生那段全然无害的友谊起了不满。我告诉过您，派克先生和我商量好彼此少来往。这样一来，我的时间便更多要靠自己打发，坦白说，我闷得慌。我便比先前多匀些时间给多姆利奥上校。仅此而已。」

===Original===

“Who began this increased intimacy?”

“Our intimacy was not increased. We saw more of each other﻿—a very different thing. I began it; in this way. In London I heard some lectures on insect life. I was interested in the subject and I asked Colonel Domlio to let me see his collection. We began to talk about it, and it ended in my going out with him occasionally to look for specimens on the moor and also in my helping him to arrange them afterwards. That was the beginning and end of what you are pleased to call our ‘intimacy.’ ”

===Chinese===

「这份升温的亲密，是谁先起的头？」

「我们的亲密并没有升温。我们只是彼此见面多了——这完全是两码事。开头的是我；经过是这样：在伦敦的时候，我听过几场讲昆虫生活的讲座。我对这个题目有了兴趣，便请多姆利奥上校让我看看他的收藏。我们开始谈论这个，到后来，我便偶尔随他到荒原上去采集标本，事后还帮他整理。您乐于称作『亲密』的那段交往，从头到尾就这么多。」

===Original===

The look of fright had left Mrs. Berlyn’s eyes and she was speaking now with more of her usual assurance.

===Chinese===

伯林太太眼里的惧意已经褪去，此刻说话又恢复了平日那几分笃定。

===Original===

“You sprained your ankle one day?”

“I twisted it slightly. It was painful for a few hours, but not really much the worse.”

===Chinese===

「有一天您扭伤了脚踝？」

「只是轻轻崴了一下。疼了几个钟头，其实并不怎么碍事。」

===Original===

“You fell?”

“I did not fall. I should have done so, but Colonel Domlio sprang forward and caught me and helped me down on to the grass. In a few minutes I was better.”

===Chinese===

「您摔倒了？」

「我没有摔倒。本来是要摔的，可多姆利奥上校一个箭步抢上来扶住了我，搀我到草地上坐下。不消几分钟我就好多了。」

===Original===

“Now, Mrs. Berlyn,” and French’s voice was very grave, “what you have to do is to convince me that that fall into Colonel Domlio’s arms really was an accident.”

===Chinese===

「好了，伯林太太，」弗伦奇的嗓音十分凝重，「现在您要做的，是叫我相信，那一跤跌进多姆利奥上校怀里，实在是个意外。」

===Original===

For a moment the lady looked at him uncomprehendingly; then she flushed angrily.

“Oh,” she cried with a gesture of disgust, “how dare you? This is insufferable! I shall not answer you. If you are coming here to insult me I shall apply for protection to your superiors at Scotland Yard.”

===Chinese===

这位太太怔怔地望了他片刻，一脸茫然；随即气红了脸。

「哦！」她厌恶地一挥手，嚷了起来，「您怎么敢这样？这真是叫人忍无可忍！我不回答您了。您若是上这儿来侮辱我，我就向苏格兰场您的上司们要求保护。」

===Original===

“If I were you I should keep away from Scotland Yard as long as you can,” French advised, drily. “In a case like this heroics will not help you any. Tell me, did you know there was a photographer watching the incident?”

===Chinese===

「我要是您，就离苏格兰场越远越好，能避多久避多久。」弗伦奇干巴巴地进言。「这样的案子，耍这套把戏对您没有半点好处。告诉我，有个照相的人在场看着那一幕，您可知道？」

===Original===

“No,” she answered sullenly, while again her face showed fear.

“You knew there wasn’t?”

“I didn’t know anything about it.”

===Chinese===

「不知道。」她闷声答道，脸上又现出惧色。

「您原先就知道没有？」

「这事我什么都不知道。」

===Original===

“But you are not surprised to hear of the photographer?”

“I am. At least I should be if you assured me one was there.”

===Chinese===

「可是听说有那么个照相的人，您并不惊讶？」

「我惊讶呀。至少，您若能肯定当时真有那么一个人，我是该惊讶的。」

===Original===

“Did you know that the handwriting of Colonel Domlio’s letter has been identified?”

Once again the colour ebbed away from her face.

“What letter?” she cried, faintly. “I don’t know what letter you mean or what you are talking about. You have made me quite confused with your questions. I scarcely know what I am saying.”

===Chinese===

「您可知道，多姆利奥上校那封信的笔迹已经鉴定出来了？」

她脸上的血色又一次退了下去。

「什么信？」她声音发虚地叫道。「我不知道您指的是哪封信，也不知道您在说什么。您这些问题把我全弄糊涂了。我简直不知道自己在说些什么。」

===Original===

French felt that he had got the effect he wanted. He therefore reassured her by a few innocuous questions, then with a change of manner he apologised for having given unnecessary annoyance and took his leave.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇觉得要收的效果已经收到了。于是他换上几个无关痛痒的问题安抚她，随后又转了口气，为平白无故让她受惊赔了不是，起身告辞。

===Original===

The taxi was standing far down the street with the bonnet open and the driver bending over the engine. French got in and he and Carter sat watching the house.

For half an hour they waited; then Mrs. Berlyn appeared, and walking to King’s Road, turned in the direction of Sloane Square. Presently she hailed a taxi, causing French to congratulate himself on his prevision.

===Chinese===

那辆出租汽车停在街那头远远的地方，引擎盖敞开着，司机正俯身对着机器。弗伦奇上了车，同卡特一起坐在车里盯住那宅子。

他们等了半个钟头；伯林太太出来了，走到国王路，朝斯隆广场方向拐去。不多时她招手叫了一辆出租汽车，弗伦奇不由得暗自庆幸自己未雨绸缪。

===Original===

Mrs. Berlyn drove to Victoria, and hurriedly paying off their own man, the detectives followed her into the station. With a rapid look round she made her way to the telephone boxes and disappeared into one of them.

“I’ll drop out here, Carter,” French said. “You stick to the woman and as far as possible keep in touch with the Yard.”

===Chinese===

伯林太太的车开到维多利亚车站。两位侦探匆匆打发了自己的车，跟进站去。她飞快地四下一望，径直奔电话亭，闪身进了其中一间。

「我在这儿下车，卡特。」弗伦奇说。「你盯住那个女人，尽量同苏格兰场保持联络。」

===Original===

Approaching the boxes, French slipped into a convenient doorway and watched until Mrs. Berlyn reappeared. As soon as she was out of sight he entered the box she had left.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇蹭到电话亭近旁，闪进一个得劲的门洞里守着，直到伯林太太重新露面。等她走得没了影，他便钻进她方才用过的那间。

===Original===

“Inspector from Scotland Yard speaking,” he told the operator. “Keep the number of that last call. It’s wanted in connection with a murder case. I’ll get you the authority to divulge. Now give me Scotland Yard, please.”

===Chinese===

「我是苏格兰场的探长。」他对接线员说。「把刚才那通电话的号码记下来。这是一桩凶案要用的。准予透露的公文我随后就补来。现在请给我接苏格兰场。」

===Original===

He put through the request for the number, then returned to the Yard to wait for the reply. After a short delay he received both number and name: Thomas Ganope, news agent and tobacconist, 27 Oakley Street, off Russell Street.

In half an hour he reached the place. Ganope’s was a small, untidy shop, and Ganope a ruffianly-looking man with purple cheeks and a cast in his left eye. He was the only occupant of the shop.

===Chinese===

他办妥调取号码的申请，回苏格兰场等候回音。没过多久，号码和户名都到了：托马斯·加诺普，报贩兼烟贩，橡树街 27 号，在罗素街旁。

半个钟头后，他到了那地方。加诺普的铺子又小又乱，加诺普本人是个面目粗横的汉子，两颊紫红，左眼斜视。店里只有他一个人。

===Original===

“Can I use your telephone?” French asked, laying a shilling on the counter.

“Sure.”

===Chinese===

「能借用一下您的电话吗？」弗伦奇问着，把一枚先令搁在柜台上。

「行嘞。」

===Original===

French rang up his wife to say that he had mislaid Mr. Walker’s address and could she let him have it again, a code message designed for such occasions and to which no attention was paid, but which enabled him to use a telephone without arousing suspicion, as well as a writing pad, should such be available. For in this case his quick eye had seen such a pad on the instrument and from many a pad he had read the last message to be written from the impression left on the paper. On chance, therefore, he made a pretense of noting the mythical Mr. Walker’s address, and removing the top sheet, put it in his pocketbook. Then he turned to Mr. Ganope.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇给太太挂了个电话，说他把沃克先生的地址弄丢了，问她能不能再给自己一遍。这是专为这类场合设下的暗语，那边从不去理会，它却能让弗伦奇不惹疑心地用上电话——倘若现场碰巧有拍纸簿，还能顺带用上一本。因为这一回，他那双利眼已经瞧见电话机上正搁着这么一本，而从前不知多少本拍纸簿，他都凭纸页上留下的压痕，读出过最后写下的那条口信。于是他存着侥幸，装模作样记下那位子虚乌有的沃克先生的地址，揭走最上面那一页，揣进随身的记事本。然后他才转向加诺普先生。

===Original===

“Say,” he said, confidentially, “what would you charge for taking in telephone messages and sending them to an address? Private, you know.”

Mr. Ganope looked him over keenly with one of his shrewd little eyes.

===Chinese===

「我说，」他一副交心的口气，「替人收电话口信、再送到一个地址去，你收多少钱？是私事，你懂的。」

加诺普先生用一只精明的小眼睛，锐利地把他上上下下打量了一遍。

===Original===

“A bob a message, if it’s near by.”

“That’s a lot. Do you never do it for less?”

Mr. Ganope seemed disgusted.

===Chinese===

「一条口信一个先令，要是地方近的话。」

「这价钱可不小。就没有便宜些的时候？」

加诺普先生一脸嫌恶。

===Original===

“If you can get anyone to do it for less you’d better go to them,” he advised, sourly.

===Chinese===

「你要是能找到肯少收的，趁早找他去。」他酸溜溜地劝道。

===Original===

“I might manage the money if I was sure the thing would be done right,” French went on. “How do you send out the messages? I mean is your arrangement reliable? Do you do it yourself or have you a messenger?”

“Wot do you tyke me for, mister? Do you think the shop would run itself while I was away? You don’t need to worry. You pay your bob and you’ll get your message all right.”

===Chinese===

「钱倒好说，只要事情办得牢靠。」弗伦奇接着说。「口信都是怎么送出去的？我是说，你这套章程靠得住吗？你自己送，还是雇着送信的孩子？」

「你拿俺当什么人，先生？你以为俺一走，这铺子自己会转起来？你甭操心。先令照付，口信保险送到。」

===Original===

“Not good enough for me. I want to know what kind of messenger you’d send before I trust my business to you.”
