Softly closing the outer door, he passed into the living room, and standing in its centre, looked round. Opposite him was the fireplace with a gas fire turned low. In the right wall was the window and against the left stood a table with a chair at each end. Two wicker armchairs were drawn up to the fireplace, and to the right of the door was a dresser containing crockery. Some books lay on the floor in a corner, but the centre of the room was clear of furniture.

===Chinese===

他轻轻掩上外门，走进起居室，站在屋子正中环顾四周。迎面是壁炉，煤气炉火调得低低的。右墙上开着窗，靠左墙立一张桌子，两端各摆一把椅子。两只柳条扶手椅拢在壁炉前，门右手是一只搁着杯盘碗碟的碗柜。墙角地板上散着几本书，屋子当中倒空着，没摆家什。

===Original===

French could see everything in the room with one exception. At the side of the fireplace was a closed cupboard. Possibly this might contain something useful.

===Chinese===

屋里的东西，弗伦奇一览无余，只除了一处。壁炉旁边立着一只关着的橱柜。这里面兴许藏着什么有用的东西。

===Original===

He had stepped across the room and put his hand on the cupboard door knob, when the feel of a presence, rather than an actual sound, caused him to swing suddenly round. A man had entered and was watching him.

===Chinese===

他正跨过屋子、把手搭上橱柜的门把，忽然一种有人在场之感——并非什么实在的声息——叫他猛地回过身来。一个男人已经进了屋，正打量着他。

===Original===

French stared in his turn. This was not Pyke. This was a smaller man and hollow of cheek, dark in colouring, and with a pair of keen eyes uncovered by glasses. A friend of Pyke’s, no doubt.

===Chinese===

这回轮到弗伦奇瞪眼打量了。这不是派克。这人个子矮些，双颊凹陷，肤色黝黑，一双锐利的眼睛这回没让眼镜遮着。不用说，是派克的什么朋友。

===Original===

But this man was vaguely familiar. That he had seen him at no distant time French felt certain. Then the man moved slightly and French noticed the marks of pince-nez on his nose. As he did so he remembered where he had seen, not him, but his photograph, and he stared spellbound in speechless amazement.

===Chinese===

可这人又隐隐有些面熟。弗伦奇确信，不久之前见过他。接着那人微微一动，弗伦奇看见他鼻梁上夹鼻眼镜留下的压痕。这一看不打紧，他顿时想起在哪里见过——不是见过此人，而是见过他的照片——登时如中定身法，惊得说不出话来。

===Original===

For a moment neither moved, then French recovered himself. With a step forward he cried, “Stanley Pyke, I arrest you on a charge of﻿—”

===Chinese===

有片刻，两人都没动，随后弗伦奇定下神来。他抢前一步，喝道：「斯坦利·派克，我以——的罪名逮捕你！」

===Original===

He stopped. Had he gone mad? Wasn’t the dead man Stanley Pyke? How could he be charged with murdering himself?

===Chinese===

话到嘴边，他噎住了。他疯了吗？死者不是斯坦利·派克吗？一个人怎么能被控谋害他自己？

===Original===

French felt his brain reel. But he grew more and more convinced that the man was indeed Stanley Pyke. Therefore the victim must have been﻿—of course!﻿—Berlyn. How the whole thing had happened French could not form an idea, but he saw that this could be straightened out later. For the moment his course was clear. He must arrest this man.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇只觉脑子里天旋地转。可他越来越确信，此人正是斯坦利·派克。那么，死者必定是——对了！——伯林。这究竟是怎么一回事，他一时理不出半点头绪，不过他明白，这个往后总能捋清。眼下他的路是明摆着的：必须逮捕这个人。

===Original===

Though these thoughts flashed through French’s mind at lightning speed, in his extremity of surprise he remained for a moment speechless, his eyes fixed on the other’s face. Then a slight movement of the man’s right arm attracted his attention and he glanced downwards. Pyke had taken an automatic pistol from his coat pocket and was holding it steadily pointed at French’s heart.

===Chinese===

这些念头在弗伦奇脑中电光石火般掠过，可他惊愕已极，一时竟说不出话来，两眼死死盯住对方的脸。忽然，那人右臂微微一动，引得他朝下一瞥。派克已从外衣口袋里掏出一支自动手枪，稳稳地指着弗伦奇的心口。

===Original===

“No, Mr. French,” he said, quietly, “I don’t think so. You’ve not got me, but I’ve got you. Put up your hands.”

===Chinese===

「不行啊，弗伦奇先生，」他不动声色地说，「我看没这么容易。你没抓住我，我倒抓住你了。举起手来。」

===Original===

As he slowly obeyed, French saw that he was in imminent danger of his life. Pyke’s features were set in an expression of ruthless determination and there was murder in his eyes. He went on speaking in quiet, grim tones.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇缓缓依言举手，心里明白自己已命悬一线。派克的脸绷出一种斩尽杀绝的狠劲，眼里透着杀机。他继续用平静而阴冷的口气说着。

===Original===

“It’s true that I may not get away with it, but I’m going to have a try. You won’t, anyway. I suppose you have men posted below?”

===Chinese===

「不错，我未必逃得脱，可我总要试一试。横竖你是逃不脱的。我猜，你在楼下布了人吧？」

===Original===

“I’ve men coming up the stairs after me,” French lied.

===Chinese===

「我的人正顺着楼梯跟在我后头上来了。」弗伦奇扯了个谎。

===Original===

“That so? They’re not hurrying. I shall have plenty of time before they get to the top. I’m going to shoot you now, Mr. Joseph French. The upper part of this building is deserted; no one across the way and only a couple of old women on the floor below. The rest are all out at work. I shall be across the roof and down the next stairs before your men are halfway up these. I may carry it off and I may not, but I’ll not be taken alive.”

===Chinese===

「是吗？他们可不着急。等他们爬到顶上，我的工夫富余得很。我现在就要毙了你，约瑟夫·弗伦奇先生。这楼的上面几层都空着；对门没人，楼下也只住着两个老太婆。其余的全上班去了。等你的人爬到一半，我早已翻过屋顶，从那边的楼梯下去了。成不成难说，可我绝不活着落在你们手里。」

===Original===

“And Mrs. Berlyn?”

===Chinese===

「那伯林太太呢？」

===Original===

Pyke’s eyes flashed.

===Chinese===

派克眼里闪过一道凶光。

===Original===

“They’ll not get her, either. I know where she is and I’ll pick her up. Say your prayers, Mr. French. You’ve only got seconds to live.”

===Chinese===

「他们也休想抓到她。我知道她在哪儿，会去接她。念你的临终祷词吧，弗伦奇先生。你只剩几秒钟好活了。」

===Original===

He slowly raised the pistol from the level of his side pocket to that of his eyes, keeping it first directed to French’s heart and then to his head. In those few moments French tasted the bitterness of death. He knew instinctively that the man meant to carry out his threat and he was powerless to prevent him. Covered by Pyke’s steady gaze as well as by his pistol, no sudden spring would help him. They were only about five feet apart, but the man would fire before he could reach across half the distance. Carter and Harvey were at the bottom of a hundred feet of stairs. They wouldn’t even hear the shot. A numbing fear crept into French’s heart, while thoughts of his wife and visions of scenes in his past life floated before his mind’s eye. And all the time he was desperately, despairingly racking his brains to find a way of escape.

===Chinese===

他缓缓举枪，从胯侧衣袋的高度直举到眼前，枪口先指着弗伦奇的心口，又移向他的头颅。就在这几瞬之间，弗伦奇尝到了死亡的苦涩。他本能地知道，这人一准说到做到，而自己无力阻拦。派克的目光同枪口一道稳稳罩着他，任何突然的扑击都无济于事。两人相距不过五英尺上下，可他还没扑过一半的距离，枪早响了。卡特和哈维远在一百英尺长的楼梯底下，连枪声都听不见。一种麻人的恐惧悄悄爬进弗伦奇心里；妻子的面影、平生前尘的一幕幕景象，从他眼前浮过。而这期间，他一直在拼死地、绝望地绞着脑汁，想寻一条活路。

===Original===

An instinctive urge that he must gain time at all costs took possession of him. Then, as he was trying to evolve some further bluff, an idea shot into his mind which suggested a glimmering of hope. It was a terribly faint glimmering; the chances were a thousand to one against him. Almost a forlorn hope, but it was all he could think of.

===Chinese===

一种不管付出什么代价也要拖住工夫的本能冲动攫住了他。正当他搜肠刮肚，想再编些什么虚词，一个念头倏地射进脑海，透出一线微茫的希望。这线光微弱得可怜；成算是一比一千。几乎是一场九死一生的孤注，可他只想得出这个。

===Original===

Neither man had moved during the interview. French had swung round from the cupboard and was still facing the door through which the other had entered. Pyke on his part had his back to the door and was facing the cupboard.

===Chinese===

这场交手期间，两人谁都没有挪动。弗伦奇从橱柜那儿回过身之后，始终朝着对方进来的那扇门；派克则背朝着门，面对橱柜。

===Original===

French instantly began to act a part. First he wished to show fear. Here he had not to act﻿—the emotion was only too genuine. Indeed, had he let himself go he would have been paralysed with terror. Therefore, as he spoke his eyes were agonised, his features distorted, and his voice thick and trembling.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇立刻演起戏来。头一样，他要显出恐惧。这倒不用演——这份情感再真不过了。说实在的，若由着它去，他早吓瘫了。因此他开口时，眼里满是惶急，五官扭曲走样，嗓音又浊又颤。

===Original===

“Don’t be a fool, Pyke. You can do better than that. I’ve sense enough to know when I’m beaten. My life’s of more value to me than success in a case. You want your liberty and I want my life. I see a way in which we can each get what we want.”

===Chinese===

「别犯傻，派克。你大可干得漂亮些。我几时栽了，这点见识我还有。对我，性命比破案要紧。你要你的自由，我要我的性命。我瞧出一条道儿，叫咱俩各得其所。」

===Original===

Pyke did not relax his attitude.

===Chinese===

派克的架势分毫未松。

===Original===

“I believe you’re a damned liar,” he said, only with a stronger adjective. “However, shove ahead with your plan. And any tricks or movements and you’re a dead man.”

===Chinese===

「我看你是个彻头彻尾的骗子，」他说，用的字眼其实比这还重。「不过，把你的章程接着摆吧。敢耍花招、敢动一动，你就是个死人。」

===Original===

“If you were once clear of me,” French went on, evincing the most transparent evidences of terror, “you could walk out of the building past my men and they’d never suspect you. They’re here to look for a quite different man. And the mere fact that you walked quietly downstairs after I had gone up to look for Jefferson would show that you were not the man I was after. That all right so far?”

===Chinese===

「只要你先从我眼前脱了身，」弗伦奇接着说，把那副恐惧摆弄得毫发毕现，「你就能打我的人眼皮底下走出楼去，他们绝不起疑。他们来抓的是一个全然不同的人。我上楼找杰斐逊之后，你安安静静走下楼去，单这一节，便表明你不是我要拿的人。到此为止，对不对？」

===Original===

“Well?”

===Chinese===

「往下讲。」

===Original===

French allowed his eyes to roam over the room, but without making any change in his expression. After the briefest pause he went on:

===Chinese===

弗伦奇任凭目光在屋里扫了一圈，脸上却纹丝不变。极短地顿了一顿，他接着说：

===Original===

“Now to get away from me is the difficulty, for while I should be willing to give you my oath not to interfere, I don’t suppose you would accept it. Well, this is my plan.”

===Chinese===

「眼下难的是，怎样从我这里脱身。我固然情愿对你起誓不加阻拦，只怕你也不会信。这么着，这就是我的章程。」

===Original===

Calling all his histrionic powers to his aid, French again glanced round the room, suddenly staying his gaze on the door. Then with the whole strength of his will he pretended to himself that he saw Carter entering. On this he fixed his mind, with the result that his eyes took on the appearance of definitely looking at something, while an expression of the utmost thankfulness and relief showed on his features. But he was quick to add the idea that Pyke must not follow what was in his mind, and he at once looked away and back to Pyke’s face. With a fine effect of recovering a line of thought which had been disturbed, he continued, now trying to give the impression of faked fear.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇使出浑身演技，又朝屋里扫了一圈，目光倏地定在门上。跟着他凝起全部意志，哄骗自己看见了卡特进门。他把心思牢牢钉在这上头，于是那双眼当真现出望定了什么的神气，脸上也浮起说不尽的感激与宽慰。可他随即又添上一个念头：万不可叫派克窥破他心里的事，便立刻移开目光，回到派克脸上。他又精彩地做出一副思路被人打断、如今方才接续上的模样，继续说道——这一回，他要装的是假作的恐惧。

===Original===

“I propose that I withdraw to the kitchenette and there gag myself and tie myself up to your satisfaction. You, of course, would keep me covered all the time and it would be quite impossible for me to play you any trick. Or, if you preferred it, I could do the tying up in this room.”

===Chinese===

「我的章程是：我退进小厨房，塞上自己的嘴，再把自己捆个结实，捆到你称心。这期间你自然枪口始终罩着我，我万难耍半点花样。你若宁可，我就在这间屋里捆也使得。」

===Original===

Again he glanced at the door as if he could not keep his eyes off it. This time he slowly shifted the point at which he was looking to just behind Pyke, while he allowed relief and satisfaction to grow on his face. Once more he hurriedly withdrew his gaze and looked at Pyke.

===Chinese===

他又朝门口瞟了一眼，像是目光怎么也离不了它。这一回，他把注视的落点缓缓移到派克身后不远处，脸上则任凭宽慰与快意一层层漾开。随后他再次慌忙收回目光，望向派克。

===Original===

“I noticed a clothes line in the kitchenette which would do,” he went on, but now absentmindedly, and giving quick, as if involuntary, glances behind Pyke. “If you agree, I’ll back in there and get it down. If I attempt to play you false you can shoot.”

===Chinese===

「我瞧见小厨房里有根晾衣绳，正合用，」他接着说，此刻语气已有些心不在焉，一面朝派克身后飞快地、像是管束不住似的瞟着。「你若应允，我就退进去把它取下来。我若想诓你，你只管开枪。」

===Original===

He paused, and looking directly behind Pyke, allowed a slight triumphant smile to appear on his lips.

===Chinese===

他顿了顿，目光直望向派克身后，唇边浮起一丝得胜的微笑。

===Original===

Pyke had obviously followed the direction of his glances and he had been getting more and more uneasy. At French’s smile he could stand it no longer. For the tenth of a second he glanced behind him. And at that moment, French, standing braced and ready, sprang. Like lightning he dropped his head while his left fist struck the other’s right wrist upwards.

===Chinese===

派克显然一路循着他的目光望过去，心里越绷越紧。见弗伦奇这一笑，他再也按捺不住了。有十分之一秒的工夫，他回头望了一眼。就在那一刻，蓄势待发的弗伦奇扑了出去。快如闪电，他一低头，左拳同时朝上磕击对方的右腕。

===Original===

Instantly Pyke fired and a hot iron seemed to sear the crown of French’s head. But he was not disabled. Seizing Pyke’s right wrist with his left hand, he drove with his right for the man’s chin.

===Chinese===

派克当即开了一枪，弗伦奇只觉头顶像烙过一块烧红的铁。可他并未瘫倒。他左手攥住派克的右腕，右拳直捣那人的下巴。

===Original===

But Pyke ducked and he missed. Then the two men, clinching with their free hands, began a voiceless struggle for their lives. Pyke’s desperate efforts were to turn the pistol inwards, French’s to prevent him. Locked together, they swayed backwards and forwards. Then French tripped over a chair and they swung with a crash against the table. It gave way, and staggering across its wreckage, they fell. French found himself underneath and redoubled his efforts, but he was hampered by the blood from his wound, which ran down and blinded one of his eyes. Fortunately, he was the stronger man, and in spite of his handicap, slowly his strength and weight began to tell. Gradually he forced Pyke’s arm round until the other had to roll over on his back to save its dislocation.

===Chinese===

派克把头一低，这一拳落了空。跟着两人各用空着的手死死扭住对方，开始了一场无声的性命相搏。派克拼命要把枪口拧向里侧，弗伦奇拼命不让。两人绞作一团，前后摇晃。弗伦奇绊在一把椅子上，两人轰然撞上桌子。桌子塌了，两人踉跄着跨过残骸，摔倒在地。弗伦奇压在下面，加倍奋起，无奈伤口的血淌下来，糊住了一只眼睛，碍手碍脚。所幸他力气更大，虽吃这个亏，他的膂力与体重渐渐占了上风。他把派克那条胳膊一点点别过去，别得那人只好翻身仰卧，好歹保住胳膊不脱臼。

===Original===

Both men were now gasping and sobbing from want of breath. But French with a superhuman effort dropped Pyke’s left arm, and seizing his collar, twisted it tight. Pyke laid out with his free arm, but he was weakening, and French, spent and giddy, but thankful, felt he could hold on in spite of the blows, and that the affair was now only a matter of time.

===Chinese===

这时两人都憋得直喘、呜咽出声。弗伦奇却迸出一股超人的狠劲，撒开扭着派克左臂的手，一把攥住他的衣领，死命绞紧。派克抡起空着的那条胳膊乱打，只是劲头渐衰。弗伦奇虽已力竭头晕，心里却谢天谢地，觉得这些拳头他扛得住，这事如今只是个时间问题了。

===Original===

And then, lying grimly clinging to the choking man’s collar, he felt a real thrill of delight as he saw the door slowly open, just as he had pictured it. Carter at last! It was over.

===Chinese===

就在这时，他一面狠掐着那渐已窒息之人的衣领，一面望见房门缓缓推开——同他方才臆想的一般无二。他心头当真掠过一阵狂喜。卡特终于来了！结束了。

===Original===

But it was not Carter who appeared. There, gazing down on them, with amazement printed on her features, was Mrs. Berlyn.

===Chinese===

可进来的不是卡特。站在那里俯视着他们、满脸惊愕的，正是伯林太太。

===Original===

It did not take her long to appreciate the situation, and with a muffled scream she threw herself on the heaving mass.

===Chinese===

她只一眼便掂出了眼前的局面，闷声一叫，扑向那起伏扭动的一团。

===Original===

“Give me the pistol, Stanley,” she cried, softly. “I’ll settle him.”

===Chinese===

「把枪给我，斯坦利，」她轻声急道。「我来结果他。」

===Original===

But Pyke was beyond coherent thought. Half insensible, he still kept his hand locked and she could not release the fingers. French, seeing the end, put all his remaining strength into a shrill cry of “Help!” before he felt the woman’s fingers tighten round his throat.

===Chinese===

可派克早已谈不上什么清醒的思绪。他半昏半醒，那只手仍死锁着，她掰不开那些指头。弗伦奇眼见末路将至，把残存的力气尽数拼作一声尖厉的「救命」，随即便觉那女人的手指扣紧了自己的咽喉。

===Original===

Letting go of the now unconscious Pyke, he tried desperately to loosen their clinging grip. But he was too weak. Choking, he struggled impotently, while gradually it grew darker, and he sank slowly into a roaring abyss of nothingness.

===Chinese===

他撒开业已昏死过去的派克，拼命想扯开那双紧掐的手指。可他太虚弱了。他窒息着，徒然挣扎，眼前渐渐黑下去，终于缓缓沉入一片轰鸣的虚无深渊。

## XX Conclusion / XX 结局

===Original===

When French struggled back into consciousness he found himself lying on the floor of that upper room with Sergeant Carter bending solicitously over him.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇挣扎着恢复知觉时，发现自己躺在楼上那间屋子的地板上，卡特警士正俯身守在旁边，一脸关切。

===Original===

“He’s coming to,” he heard him say as if from a great distance. “He’ll be none the worse in a few minutes.”

===Chinese===

「他缓过来了，」他听见卡特说话，声音仿佛从极远的地方飘来。「再过几分钟就没事了。」

===Original===

“I’m all right,” French whispered, faintly. “What about﻿—”

===Chinese===

「我没事，」弗伦奇虚弱地低声说。「那两个怎么样——」

===Original===

“Both safe as a house,” Carter answered. “I thought you were taking too long over the job and was coming up the fire escape when I heard you shout. Lucky I got up in time. But it was a near thing, Mr. French; just as near a thing as I should like to see. Don’t you be in any hurry. You’ve all the day before you. Take a nip of this brandy that Harvey has brought.”

===Chinese===

「两个都稳稳当当，毫发无损，」卡特答道。「我看你这事办得太久，正顺着太平梯往上爬，就听见你喊了一声。幸亏我及时赶了上去。可真是险到家了，弗伦奇先生；险得我一辈子不想再碰上第二回。您千万别急。这一整天都归您使唤。哈维拿来的这瓶白兰地，您先抿一口。」

===Original===

The stimulant made French once again feel his own man, and he sat up to find that his assailants had been safely handcuffed. Mrs. Berlyn sat in one of the wicker armchairs, deadly pale and with an expression of murderous hate in her eyes. Pyke was still unconscious, and the others at once turned their attention to him, with the result that presently he, too, revived. The taxi was waiting and before many minutes had passed both prisoners were lodged in the cells.

===Chinese===

这口兴奋剂下了肚，弗伦奇重新觉得自己是个囫囵人了。他坐起身来，只见袭击他的那两个已经被稳稳当当地铐上手铐。伯林太太坐在一把柳条扶手椅里，脸色死灰，眼里透着杀气腾腾的恨意。派克还没有醒，众人当即转过去照拂他，不多时他也还过魂来。出租车在外面候着，没过几分钟，两名囚犯便都押进了拘押室。

===Original===

When French sat down in his own room to think over this unexpected development he very soon saw that he had made a terrible error in his handling of the case. Never before had he blundered so inexcusably! The clue to the truth was there in his hand and he had missed it. Though even now he could not understand all that had happened, he saw enough to appreciate his mistake, to locate the point at which he had strayed from the right path.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇回到自己房里坐下，细细思量这意料之外的变故，很快便看出，自己办这桩案子时犯下了一个可怕的错误。他从来没有失手得这样不可原谅！真相的线索分明就攥在他手心里，他却错过了。虽说直到此刻他也不能把发生的一切尽数想通，但他看明白的已经足够令他痛感自己的失误，找准自己究竟是从哪一步走岔了正道。

===Original===

He had accepted the identification of the remains, but on whose testimony? On that of the criminals, Mrs. Berlyn and the man whom he had thought was Jefferson Pyke. Of course at the time at which he interviewed them he had no idea of their connection with the crime, and therefore no reason to doubt their statements, but his error came in just here: that by the time he began to suspect them the identification was so firmly fixed in his mind that he overlooked the fact that it depended on them. If he had remembered that supremely important point he would have questioned the dead man’s identity. This would have led him to investigate, even more closely than he had, the movements of the Pykes and no doubt he would have thus discovered the impersonation which had been carried out.

===Chinese===

他采信了对遗骸的身份认定，可这认定凭的是谁的证词？凭的是那两个罪犯——伯林太太，还有那个他一直当作杰斐逊·派克的人。当然，他讯问他们的时候，压根不知道他们与罪案有干系，因此也没有理由怀疑他们的说辞；可他的错处恰恰出在这里：等到他开始对他们起疑时，那身份认定早已在他脑中根深蒂固，以致他忽略了一个事实——这认定全凭他们一面之词。倘若他记起了这个至关紧要之点，他就会去追问死者的真实身份。那样一来，他就会把派克家兄弟的行迹查得比先前更透，无疑也就识破了那套冒名顶替的把戏。

===Original===

The first question, then, which demanded solution was: If the man whom he had thought was Jefferson Pyke was really his cousin, where was Jefferson himself?

===Chinese===

那么，头一个必须求解的问题便是：如果那个他一直当作杰斐逊·派克的人其实是杰斐逊的堂兄，那么杰斐逊本人又在哪儿？

===Original===

Like a man in a dream French went back to Kepple Street. Was Mrs. Welsh *absolutely* sure that the Mr. Pyke who had engaged her rooms on the 22nd of July was the same man who had occupied them ever since? Mrs. Welsh, when at last she had been made to understand the question, was absolutely sure.

===Chinese===

弗伦奇如堕梦中，又回到凯普尔街。7月22日租下她房间的那位派克先生，和此后一直住着的是不是同一个人——韦尔什太太对这一点能*绝对*肯定吗？韦尔什太太好容易弄明白了这个问题之后，表示绝对肯定。

===Original===

If she were right, Stanley’s impersonation of Jefferson must have begun on that 22nd of July. They had both been at the Houston in the morning. By the evening Jefferson, the real Jefferson, apparently had vanished.

===Chinese===

果真如此的话，斯坦利冒充杰斐逊就必定始于7月22日那天。那天上午，两人都还在休斯敦饭店。到了晚上，杰斐逊——真正的杰斐逊——显然已经无影无踪。

===Original===

Then suddenly French remembered the episode of the broken basin. It had not occurred to him before, but now he wondered if there was not more here than met the eye. The accident was unlikely. Had the basin been deliberately broken to help on some trick?

===Chinese===

接着弗伦奇猛然记起了打碎脸盆那段插曲。先前他从未把这事故在心上，如今却不免疑心其中另有文章。那场意外实在不像意外。那只脸盆会不会是故意打碎的，好给什么鬼把戏搭桥？

===Original===

He went back to the Houston and once again interviewed the reception clerk. But she could add nothing to her former statement. Then he reexamined the chambermaid. From her at last he obtained a new fact, an apparently trifling fact, but what a difference it made in his conclusion!

===Chinese===

他回到休斯敦饭店，再次讯问那位接待员。可她在先前说过的话之外，再补充不出半点东西。于是他重新盘问客房女侍，从她嘴里总算问出一个新情况——一个表面上无足轻重的情况，可它给他的结论带来了多大的改观！

===Original===

The girl had stated that the gentleman who broke the basin had told her that he had overslept himself and had asked her to see that his friend in No. 351 was awake. She had gone and found that the friend was already up. Now French learned that by “up” she had meant that the man was up and dressed and had left his room.

===Chinese===

女侍先前说过，打碎脸盆的那位绅士告诉她，自己睡过了头，托她去看看 351 号房里的朋友醒了没有。她过去一看，那位朋友已经起身。如今弗伦奇才问明白：她说的「起身」，是指那人起了床、穿好衣裳、已经离开了房间。

===Original===

At this a light shone into French’s mind. He retired to a corner of the smoking room, and after half an hour’s hard thinking he reached a detailed solution.

===Chinese===

这一来，一道亮光照进了弗伦奇的脑子。他退到吸烟室的一角，苦苦思索了半个钟头，理出了一套环环入扣的解答。

===Original===

He saw that it would be possible for Stanley to arrive at the hotel and engage two rooms, ostensibly for himself and his cousin. He would go to his own room after explaining that his cousin would arrive later in the day. After impressing his personality on the staff, he would go out, make up as Jefferson, return with more luggage, and occupy the second room which had been engaged. He would sleep in Jefferson’s room, and in the morning ring early for shaving-water, get up, dress, take his luggage down, pay his bill, and leave the hotel. Then hurrying back, he would slip into his own room unobserved, go to bed, and ring again for hot water, giving the maid the message about his friend. Lest the incident should be forgotten in the event of future enquiries, he would smash the basin, thus impressing his identity on all concerned.

===Chinese===

他看明白了：斯坦利尽可以先行到店，订下两个房间，名义上给自己和堂弟各留一间。他推说堂弟当天晚些时候才到，便先住进自己那间。等把自己的相貌在饭店职工心里印下了印象，他就出门，化装成杰斐逊的模样，带着更多的行李回店，住进订下的第二间房。他在杰斐逊的房间里过夜，一清早按铃要剃须热水，起床，穿衣，把行李拎下楼，结账，离店。随即火速赶回，趁人不备溜进自己那间房，上床躺下，再按铃要热水，捎带给女侍留下那句关于朋友的话。为免日后一旦有人查问、这段情节被忘到脑后，他还砸破脸盆，把自己的身份牢牢烙进所有相关人员的记忆里。

===Original===

That this was what Stanley had done, French was now pretty sure, and he despatched wires to the French and Italian police asking if the same trick had been carried out in Paris and at Grasse and San Remo. After some time there were replies. It had been carried out in Paris, but not at the other two places. At the latter there was no doubt that both men had been present.

===Chinese===

斯坦利干的就是这一套，弗伦奇如今已有八九分把握。他向法国和意大利警方发出电报，询问在巴黎、格拉斯和圣雷莫是否也耍过同样的把戏。过了一阵子，回电来了：巴黎耍过，另外两地没有。在后两地，两个人无疑都确实在场。

===Original===

Jefferson Pyke had, therefore, disappeared at some point between Grasse and Paris, and French soon saw that there was nothing for it but to go to the Riviera and himself enquire into the men’s movements. Accordingly, after consultation with his chief he obtained a letter to the French police, and travelling to Marseilles, began work. Slowly and painfully he traced the two from Marseilles to San Remo, to Monte Carlo, to Grasse, and finally to Nice. And there in the pleasure city, on the shores of the Mediterranean, he came on the explanation he sought.

===Chinese===

如此说来，杰斐逊·派克是在格拉斯与巴黎之间的某个当口失踪的。弗伦奇很快看出，别无他法，只能亲自跑一趟里维埃拉，查一查这两人的行踪。于是他与上司商议之后，拿到一封致法国警方的介绍信，动身前往马赛，着手查访。他缓慢而费力地一路追索，从马赛到圣雷莫，到蒙特卡洛，到格拉斯，最后到尼斯。就在那座地中海之滨的游乐城里，他撞见了他苦苦寻索的答案。

===Original===

For at Nice, Jefferson Pyke had died. At first, knowing what he did, French had suspected foul play. But in this he found he was mistaken. Jefferson had been taken ill at his hotel and had at once been moved to a hospital. There he had been operated on for appendicitis. French saw the doctor who had had charge of the case and learned the details. There had been complications and the operation could not save him.

===Chinese===

原来在尼斯，杰斐逊·派克死了。起初，鉴于他所掌握的种种内情，弗伦奇曾疑心这是一场谋杀。但他发现这一回是自己想岔了。杰斐逊在饭店里骤然病倒，当即被送进一家医院，在那里接受了阑尾炎手术。弗伦奇见了主治这个病例的医生，问明了详情：术后出现了并发症，手术没能救回他的命。

===Original===

French was deeply chagrined at his failure to learn so essential a fact at an earlier stage in his investigation. He swore great oaths to his gods that never, *never* again should he fail to follow up to the very end every clue which presented itself, whether he thought it likely to prove valuable or not.

===Chinese===

这么要紧的一个情况，竟没能在侦办的更早阶段查出来，弗伦奇懊恼已极。他对着自己信奉的神明们连发毒誓：从今往后，无论冒出什么线索，也不管它看着有价值还是没价值，都非追查到底不可——永远、*永远*不再重蹈这回的覆辙。

===Original===

With identity of murderer and victim established, a comparatively short further enquiry sufficed to clear up the details of the affair which were still in doubt. And dreadful reading they made.

===Chinese===

凶手与死者的身份既已坐实，再顺藤摸瓜问上不长一段，案中尚存疑窦的细节便一一水落石出。只是这份细节读来，令人不寒而栗。

===Original===

It seemed that soon after Phyllis Considine came to Ashburton as the bride of Charles Berlyn she found she had made a terrible mistake in her marriage. The feeling which she had imagined was love died away, and she saw herself tied for life to a man whom she disliked. A mutual coldness inevitably resulted, which rapidly widened as the husband also found himself disillusioned. On Phyllis’s side less than a year sufficed to turn it into a bitter hatred.

===Chinese===

原来，菲莉斯·康西丁作为查尔斯·伯林的新娘来到阿什伯顿后不久，便发现自己在这桩婚事上犯了个天大的错误。她原以为是爱情的那种感情渐渐枯萎了，她眼看自己被拴在一个她厌恶的男人身上，一辈子不得脱身。夫妻间不可避免地冷淡下来，而丈夫那边也觉出自己错付了痴心，这层冷淡便愈裂愈宽。在菲莉斯这方面，不到一年，冷淡便酿成了切齿的仇恨。

===Original===

Under these miserable circumstances she began to look elsewhere for companionship, and Stanley Pyke, who found himself strongly attracted to his former playmate, was only too ready to fill the breach. They saw a good deal of each other and the inevitable happened. Before long both were deeply in love.

===Chinese===

在这等愁苦的境地里，她开始到别处寻觅陪伴，而斯坦利·派克发觉自己对这个昔日的玩伴萌生了强烈的情愫，自然巴不得乘虚而入。两人你来我往，越走越近，该发生的终究发生了。不多时，双双坠入热恋。

===Original===

Though they were careful to act discreetly when under observation, they were not careful enough, and Berlyn became aware of what was going on. He at once remonstrated with his wife, finally telling her in no ambiguous terms that if given cause he would not hesitate to divorce her. If this had been all, Phyllis would no doubt have eagerly seized so complete a solution of her difficulty, but both she and Pyke saw that it was by no means all. Berlyn, as joint owner of the business, would certainly have dismissed Pyke as well as cut Phyllis out of his will. The couple would, therefore, have been without money or prospects. This she was not prepared for, and while admitting unwise behaviour, she denied that there was anything serious in her relations with Pyke and undertook that Berlyn should have no further cause of complaint. Then she began to meet Pyke secretly, and gradually the terrible solution which they afterwards adopted grew subconsciously in both their minds. If Berlyn should die, the whole situation would be straightened out.

===Chinese===

两人虽然在人前处处留神检点，却终究检点得不够，伯林察觉了其中的蹊跷。他当即向妻子摊牌，最后毫不含糊地告诉她：只要再让他抓到把柄，他会毫不迟疑地同她离婚。倘若仅止于此，菲莉斯无疑会巴不得抓住这个一劳永逸的解困之道；可她同派克都明白，事情远不止于此。伯林身为商行的合股老板，到时候必定会把派克辞出门墙，还会把菲莉斯从遗嘱里一笔勾掉。这一来，这对情侣便既没了钱，也没了前程。这样的下场她可没有胆量承受，于是她一面承认自己行为失检，一面矢口否认她同派克之间有什么见不得人的认真事，并保证不再让伯林有任何抱怨的由头。可暗地里，她开始同派克偷偷相会，而那桩日后果真付诸实行的可怕方案，便在两人心底潜移默化地扎下了根：只要伯林一死，整个困局便迎刃而解。

===Original===

From that time, the idea of murder was never far from the thoughts of either. But they could see no way in which the dreadful deed could be safely accomplished. Pyke, however, was sufficiently callous and farseeing to suggest the flirtation with Domlio, partly as a proof that the lady’s feeling for himself was a thing of the past, and partly lest a scapegoat should afterwards be wanted in connection with the murder.

===Chinese===

从那时起，杀人的念头再没有离开过两人的心头。可那桩骇人的勾当怎样才能干得干净利落、不留后患，他们看不出半条门路。倒是派克心肠够硬、眼光够远，提议让菲莉斯去同多姆利奥调情——一来证明这位太太对派克本人的情意早已成为过去，二来也防着日后那桩谋杀案需要一个替罪羊。

===Original===

Then came Jefferson’s visit and the cousins’ holiday and Jefferson’s unexpected death. Pyke was about to give up the tour and come home when it suddenly struck him that here was the solution for which he and Phyllis had been looking. He travelled up to Paris and there spent a few days in working out his plan.

===Chinese===

接着便是杰斐逊来访、堂兄弟俩结伴出游、杰斐逊猝然客死。派克正打算中断旅程、返回英国，忽然灵光一闪：他和菲莉斯苦苦寻索的解决方案，这不就送上门来了吗？他径直北上巴黎，在那里盘桓数日，把计划打磨成形。

===Original===

The idea of diverting suspicion from the murder of Berlyn by staging the accident on the moor had already occurred to him. But this plan had the objection that it involved his own disappearance as well as Berlyn’s. If, however, he disappeared, the whole fruits of the murder would be lost, as he was committing it simply to enable him to marry Phyllis. Jefferson’s death showed him how he might escape this dilemma.

===Chinese===

在荒原上伪造一场意外，把谋杀伯林的嫌疑引开——这个念头他早已有之。但这方案有个难处：它意味着他得同伯林一道消失。可他若消失了，这场谋杀的全部果实便将付诸东流，因为他行凶本就只是为了能娶到菲莉斯。杰斐逊之死，让他看到了跳出这一两难困局的路子。

===Original===

He would, in brief, murder Berlyn, stage the accident on the moor, and disappear﻿—as Stanley Pyke. He would immediately reappear﻿—as Jefferson. By impersonating Jefferson he could marry Phyllis and get all his cousin’s money as well.

===Chinese===

简而言之：他要谋杀伯林，在荒原上布置那场意外，然后消失——以斯坦利·派克的身份消失；随即再露面——以杰斐逊的身份露面。只要冒充杰斐逊，他既能娶到菲莉斯，还能把堂弟的钱财一并收入囊中。

===Original===

In this scheme there was a risk, of course, but the chances of anyone learning of Jefferson’s death were, he thought, sufficiently remote to make the scheme practicable. As soon as he could, without suspicion, he would go abroad, where Phyllis could presently follow him.

===Chinese===

这计划当然有风险，但依他盘算，有人得知杰斐逊死讯的机会微乎其微，足以令计划行得通。只等瞅准一个不惹疑窦的时机，他就出国去，菲莉斯随后便可前来相会。

===Original===

At first the plan seemed full of snags, but as he thought over it, he saw ways of overcoming one difficulty after another, until the whole ghastly affair grew coherent and feasible. When he returned to Ashburton the scheme was cut and dry and he had only to get Phyllis’s approval and promise of help.

===Chinese===

起初这计划处处是绊子，可他反复推敲，一个难题一个难题地想出了破解之道，直到整桩骇人的勾当变得首尾贯通、切实可行。待他回到阿什伯顿，方案已安排得滴水不漏，只欠征得菲莉斯的首肯、讨来她出手相助的承诺了。

===Original===

Jefferson, the real Jefferson, had already visited Ashburton, but he had only been seen closely by the landlady, Mrs. Billing, the Berlyns and their servants, Domlio, and one or two others. Of these the only one Stanley need interview was Mrs. Billing, and he felt sure he could deceive her. Sergeant Daw, with whom in his enquiries about the tragedy he would have most to do, had only seen Jefferson in the distance. Personation would, therefore, be possible.

===Chinese===

杰斐逊——真正的杰斐逊——先前倒是来过阿什伯顿，但把他端详仔细过的，只有房东太太比林太太、伯林夫妇和他们的仆人、多姆利奥，以及一两个旁人。这里面，斯坦利需要应付的只有比林太太一人，而他自信骗得过她。至于道警士——日后追查那桩惨案时要同他打最多交道的——只在远处望见过杰斐逊一眼。因此，冒名顶替是办得到的。

===Original===

The first thing necessary was to prove Jefferson still alive. Stanley found it easier than he had expected. From a theatrical supplies shop he bought shoes with false internal heels to increase his height, padded underclothes to give him the necessary girth, rubber discs to wear inside his cheeks to alter the shape of his face, and glasses. When in addition to these he wore Jefferson’s clothes and copied as best he could Jefferson’s walk, speech, and deportment, it was not surprising that the unsuspecting and unobservant persons at Ashburton should be taken in.

===Chinese===

头一件非办不可的事，是证明杰斐逊还活着。斯坦利发现这事比他预想的容易。他从一家戏剧行头铺买来暗藏内增高跟的鞋，衬出必要腰身的加垫内衣，改变脸型的腮内橡胶垫片，还有一副眼镜。在这一身之外再穿上杰斐逊的衣服，竭力模仿杰斐逊的步态、谈吐和举止做派，阿什伯顿那些无意提防又不善观察的人会上当受骗，也就不足为怪了。

===Original===

On his way home he carried out the tricks at the hotels, then taking the room in Kepple Street. For some hectic weeks he managed to live at Kepple Street as Jefferson and at Ashburton as Stanley. His continual absences from Ashburton, travelling for his firm, enabled him to put in the necessary appearances in Kepple Street, where he gave out that in the intervals he was making business trips in England and France.

===Chinese===

归途中，他把那套把戏在几家饭店一一耍过，随后租下凯普尔街的房间。有那么几个忙乱焦灼的星期，他硬是一边在凯普尔街当杰斐逊、一边在阿什伯顿当斯坦利，两头活了过来。他替商行跑外、经常离开阿什伯顿，这使他得以在凯普尔街作必要的露面，并对人放风说，闲暇时他在英格兰和法国各地出差。

===Original===

He saw that by a judicious interview with the clerk of the Tavistock Urban District Council he could arrange for the evening journey across the moor. His first idea had been to dispose of Berlyn’s body on the way back by throwing it into one of the small mires close to the road. But when he considered this in detail he realised that the difficulties were overwhelming, just as had been suggested to French by Sergeant Daw. He therefore devised the episode of the duplicator in order to provide a means for the removal of the remains. He knew the Burry Inlet, and the date of a suitable tide became the foundation on which the rest of the plan was built.

===Chinese===

他看准了：只要跟塔维斯托克城区议会的书记官见上一面、话说得得体，便能安排下夜间横越荒原的那趟行程。他起初的打算是，回程路上把伯林的尸体扔进大路近旁的一处小泥沼里销尸灭迹。可他把这事细加掂量之后，便看出难处重重、无从下手——正如道警士后来向弗伦奇点破的那样。于是他设计出复印机那一幕，为遗骸的转移安排一条出路。他熟悉伯里湾，一股合适潮汛的日期，便成了整座计划得以立足的基石。

===Original===

In London he bought the secondhand typewriter and wrote the letters ordering the duplicator and crane lorry on paper he had obtained from the L.M.S. hotels. These letters he handed to the tobacconist, Ganope, who for a consideration undertook to post them on the proper dates. Neither he nor Phyllis was, therefore, in London when they were sent out, though he called in person at the hotels for the replies. He similarly obtained the duplicate magneto. This he handed to Phyllis and she damaged it and ran it on the car until it gave up, then replacing the original.

===Chinese===

在伦敦，他买下那台二手打字机，用从 L.M.S. 各旅馆弄来的信纸，写下订购复印机和吊车卡车的信件。这些信他交给了烟草商甘诺普（Ganope），对方得了一笔酬劳，应承在指定日期逐一寄出。因此信件寄出时，他和菲莉斯都不在伦敦，只是回信由他亲自到那几家旅馆去取。那台一模一样的备用磁电机也是如法炮制到手的。他把它交给菲莉斯；她把它弄出损伤，装在汽车上一直用到彻底报废，再换回原来那台。

===Original===

On the fatal night every movement of each conspirator was carefully timed and cut and dry to the last detail. When near the works on the return journey Stanley called out to Berlyn to stop, saying that he had seen a man lying on the road. He jumped out and ran round the car, and on Berlyn following him he slipped behind his victim and sandbagged him. Hastily throwing the body into the car, he drove to the works gate. There he was met by Phyllis, who had already drugged Gurney’s tea in the way French had surmised.

===Chinese===

出事那晚，两个同谋的一举一动都掐着钟点，桩桩件件安排到了最后一细节。回程行至工厂附近，斯坦利喊伯林停车，说看见路旁躺着一个人。他跳下车，绕着汽车跑；待伯林跟下来，他闪到猎物身后，抡起沙袋照他打了下去。他急急忙忙把尸体塞进车里，径直开到工厂大门口。菲莉斯在那里接应——她已照弗伦奇推断过的那样，在格尼的茶里下了药。

===Original===

At the works Phyllis helped Pyke to carry the body to the packing-shed. After replacing Gurney’s drugged tea with fresh, she put her bicycle in the tonneau of the car and drove out to the selected spot on the moor. She changed the magneto and made the two lines of footmarks by putting on shoes of Berlyn’s which she had brought for the purpose. Then mounting her bicycle, she returned to the works. The bicycle and the magneto she hid in a clump of shrubs, waiting until Pyke came out to say that the ghastly job in the works was complete. She then went home, silently let herself in, replaced Berlyn’s shoes in his room, changed her clothes, wakened the maid and called up Sergeant Daw.

===Chinese===

到了工厂，菲莉斯帮派克把尸体抬进包装棚。她用新茶换下格尼那杯下了药的茶，把自行车放进汽车后座，驱车前往荒原上选定的地点。她换掉磁电机，又穿上特意带来的伯林的皮鞋，踩出那两行脚印。随后她跨上自行车返回工厂，把车和磁电机藏进一丛灌木，直等派克出来说，厂里那桩可怕的活计已经办妥。她这才回家，悄没声儿地开了门进屋，把伯林的皮鞋放回他房里，换过衣服，唤醒女仆，给道警士挂了电话。

===Original===

In the meantime Stanley Pyke rode on the bicycle to Colonel Domlio’s, dropped the clothes and other objects into the well, and continued on his way to Plymouth. There he left the bicycle in a dark entry in the lower part of the town, where he was sure it would speedily be found and annexed. He took the first train to London, returning to Ashburton in the character of Jefferson on receipt of Daw’s wire about the tragedy.

===Chinese===

与此同时，斯坦利·派克骑着那辆自行车到了多姆利奥上校家，把衣裳和那几样物件丢进井里，而后继续赶路，奔普利茅斯去了。在城的下城区，他把自行车撂在一条黑洞洞的过道里，料定它很快就会被人发现、顺手骑走。他搭上头一班去伦敦的火车，接到道警士通报惨案的电报后，便以杰斐逊的身份回到阿什伯顿。

===Original===

Such was the plan, and, had the conspirators been sure their actions would have remained unquestioned, they would have stopped there. But both were afraid that in some unforeseen way suspicion might be aroused and they took several other steps in the hope of turning such possible suspicion on to other persons.

===Chinese===

计划便是如此。倘若两个同谋能确信自己的所作所为不会招人盘问，他们本可以到此收手。可两人都害怕疑心会以某种始料未及的方式被勾起来，于是又走了几步棋，指望把这层可能的嫌疑引到旁人身上。

===Original===

First they considered it necessary that, should the crate be discovered, the body therein should be taken as Stanley Pyke’s. This would effectually prevent a doubt arising as to Jefferson’s identity. Pyke, therefore, changed his own underclothes with those of the dead man and dropped his own suit down the well, while both he and Phyllis stated that the birthmark on Berlyn’s arm was really on Pyke’s.

===Chinese===

首先，他们认为必须做到：万一板条箱被人发现，箱中的尸首要被认作斯坦利·派克的。这样才能万无一失地堵死人们对杰斐逊身份的疑问。于是，派克把自己贴身的内衣同死者的对换了，又把自己那套外衣丢进井里；而他和菲莉斯都一口咬定，伯林胳膊上的胎记其实长在派克身上。

===Original===

To carry on the same deception, Pyke decided to make up as Berlyn while disposing of the crate. In height and build he was, in his disguise, not unlike Berlyn. The chief difference between them was their colouring, both the Pykes being dark and Berlyn fair. Stanley therefore dyed his hair and lightened his complexion before going to Wales.

===Chinese===

为把这出骗局接着唱下去，派克决定在处理板条箱时化装成伯林的模样。论身量和体格，他化装之后同伯林相去不远。两人主要的差别在毛发肤色：派克家兄弟俩都是黑发深肤，伯林却生得金发白皙。因此斯坦利动身去威尔士之前，先把头发染了色，又把肤色涂白了些。

===Original===

The episode of the blackmailing letter was designed with a similar object. Phyllis Berlyn had staged the farce of the twisted ankle. She had fallen into Domlio’s arms in such a way that a photograph might have really been a profitable investment to a blackmailer. Pyke had, of course, forged the letter. The confederates hoped that the taking out of the car which they believed would result, together with the finding of the articles in the well, which they thought a competent detective might achieve, would throw the suspicion on Domlio.

===Chinese===

那封敲诈信的一幕，也是抱着同样的目的设计出来的。菲莉斯·伯林自导自演了扭伤脚踝的闹剧。她跌进多姆利奥怀抱的那副光景，真足以教一张照片成为敲诈者一桩稳赚的买卖。那封信自然是派克伪造的。两个同谋指望：那辆汽车会因此被警方提去查验（他们认定必然如此），再加上井里那些物件被起获（他们认为一个能干的侦探办得到这一点），两下合力，嫌疑便会扣到多姆利奥头上。

===Original===

With regard to money, Pyke had resolved on a bold stroke. French found that he had instructed a firm of solicitors in London to get in touch with Jefferson’s lawyers in the Argentine for the purpose of arranging the sale of Jefferson’s property there. He had forged Jefferson’s signature to the necessary documents, and the matter was well advanced.

===Chinese===

在钱财上，派克还打定了一个大胆的主意。弗伦奇查出，他已委托伦敦一家律师行去接洽杰斐逊在阿根廷的律师，安排变卖杰斐逊在那边的产业。必要文件上杰斐逊的签名由他一手伪造，而且此事已办得大有眉目。

===Original===

This piece of greed had prevented Stanley from leaving London and had caused him to rent the Victory Place flat as a refuge in case Kepple Street got too hot to hold him. Flight thither under such circumstances had been decided on, and, therefore, when danger threatened, only the time and method of reaching it remained to be settled.

===Chinese===

正是这桩贪心绊住了斯坦利，使他没能离开伦敦，也使他租下胜利街那套公寓，以备凯普尔街一旦待不下去时有个退身之所。在那种情势下逃往那套公寓，本是早就定下的；因此临到危险逼来，需要斟酌的不过是动身的时辰与路径而已。

===Original===

As all Berlyn’s property went to Phyllis under the deceased’s will, she also would have been well off, so that when the two took up their abode in a rather out-of-the-way part of Algeria, as they had intended, they would have been comparatively rich.

===Chinese===

依照死者的遗嘱，伯林的全部产业都归菲莉斯所有，她也将坐享富足。因此，等两人照着本意搬到阿尔及利亚一处相当偏僻的角落安家之后，本可以过得相当阔绰。

===Original===

But thanks to Evan Morgan’s desire for a fishing expedition on the last day of his holidays, together with Inspector French’s steady pegging away at a puzzling and wearisome case, these evil hopes were frustrated. Some three weeks after the next assizes, both Stanley Pyke and Phyllis Berlyn paid for their crime with their lives, leaving the ownership of Jefferson’s money as a juicy morsel on which the lawyers of England and the Argentine could pile up fees to their hearts’ content.

===Chinese===

无奈多亏埃文·摩根偏在假期最后一天兴起钓鱼之念，再加上弗伦奇探长对一桩伤神劳形的疑案咬定不放、步步紧逼，这些罪恶的指望终告落空。下一届巡回法庭开庭约莫三个星期之后，斯坦利·派克与菲莉斯·伯林相继以命抵罪，只把杰斐逊那笔钱的归属留作一块肥肉，任凭英格兰和阿根廷的律师们称心如意地往上堆砌讼费。

===Original===

For French the case had not been an unmixed triumph. Though in the end he had taken the criminals and added to his reputation with his superiors, in his heart of hearts he knew that he had been saved by the skin of his teeth alone and that he had had a lesson in the danger of neglecting to follow up unlikely clues to the very end which would last him for his life. But on the whole he was satisfied.

===Chinese===

对弗伦奇来说，这桩案子算不上十足的凯旋。虽说到头来他拿住了罪犯，在上司跟前又添了一分声名，可他心底明白，这回自己纯粹是死里逃生、侥幸过关；至于疏忽看似无望的线索、不曾追查到底会有多大的凶险，这一课他受得结结实实，够他记一辈子。不过总的来说，他还是满意的。
